<script id="universal-window-player-3d-2026-09-20-js">
(function(){
 var oldDrag=window.dragWindow;
 function makeDrag(w){
   if(!w)return;
   var head=w.querySelector('.win-head');if(!head||head.dataset.universalDrag==='1')return;
   head.dataset.universalDrag='1';
   head.onmousedown=function(e){
     if(e.target.closest('button,input,select,textarea,a'))return;
     e.preventDefault();
     var r=w.getBoundingClientRect(),sx=e.clientX,sy=e.clientY;
     w.style.transform='none';w.style.left=r.left+'px';w.style.top=r.top+'px';
     function move(ev){
       var x=r.left+ev.clientX-sx,y=r.top+ev.clientY-sy;
       var maxX=Math.max(0,window.innerWidth-w.offsetWidth),maxY=window.innerHeight*2;
       w.style.setProperty('left',Math.max(0,Math.min(maxX,x))+'px','important');w.style.setProperty('top',Math.max(70,y)+'px','important');
     }
     function up(){document.removeEventListener('mousemove',move);document.removeEventListener('mouseup',up)}
     document.addEventListener('mousemove',move);document.addEventListener('mouseup',up);
   };
 }
 function all(){document.querySelectorAll('.window').forEach(makeDrag)}
 window.makeAllWindowsDraggable=all;
 document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',all);setTimeout(all,300);setTimeout(all,1000);
 /* Replace the old media rendering visually without changing the Drive-backed storage. */
 var oldRender=window.renderMedia;
 window.renderMedia=function(){
   var box=document.getElementById('mediaStageContent');if(!box)return;
   if(!window.media||!media.length){box.innerHTML='<span class="media-center-badge">মিডিয়া আপলোড করে স্লাইড দেখুন</span>';return}
   var i=((window.mediaIndex||0)%media.length+media.length)%media.length,x=media[i],u=String(x.DataURL||'');
   var badge='<span class="media-center-badge">'+(x.Name||('Media '+(i+1)))+'</span>';
   if(x.Type==='image')box.innerHTML=badge+'<img src="'+esc(u)+'" alt="">';
   else if(x.Type==='video')box.innerHTML=badge+'<video src="'+esc(u)+'" controls preload="metadata"></video>';
   else box.innerHTML=badge+'<audio src="'+esc(u)+'" controls preload="metadata"></audio>';
   box.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<button class="media-arrow-new left" type="button" onclick="previousMedia()" aria-label="Previous">❮</button><button class="media-arrow-new right" type="button" onclick="nextMedia()" aria-label="Next">❯</button>');
   var controls=document.querySelector('.media-player-controls');
   if(!controls){
     controls=document.createElement('div');controls.className='media-player-controls';
     controls.innerHTML='<button type="button" onclick="openModule(\'gallery\')">⬆️ আপলোড</button><button id="newMediaPlay" type="button" onclick="toggleMediaPlay()">▶️ প্লে</button><button type="button" onclick="deleteCurrentMedia()">🗑️ ডিলেট</button>';
     box.parentElement.appendChild(controls);
   }
 };
 window.nextMedia=function(){if(!media||!media.length)return;mediaIndex=(mediaIndex+1)%media.length;renderMedia()};
 window.previousMedia=function(){if(!media||!media.length)return;mediaIndex=(mediaIndex-1+media.length)%media.length;renderMedia()};
 window.toggleMediaPlay=function(){
   var box=document.getElementById('mediaStageContent'),el=box&&box.querySelector('video,audio'),b=document.getElementById('newMediaPlay');
   if(el){if(el.paused){el.play().then(function(){if(b)b.textContent='⏸️ Pause'}).catch(function(){toast('প্লে করা যাচ্ছে না।')})}else{el.pause();if(b)b.textContent='▶️ প্লে'}}
   else {clearInterval(window._newMediaTimer);window._newMediaTimer=setInterval(function(){nextMedia()},5000);if(b)b.textContent='⏸️ Pause'}
 };
 window.deleteCurrentMedia=function(){if(!media||!media.length)return toast('ডিলেট করার মিডিয়া নেই।');deleteMediaByIndex(mediaIndex)};
})();

<script id="final-authoritative-window-drag-js">
(function(){
  function install(w){
    if(!w)return;
    var h=w.querySelector('.win-head');
    if(!h)return;
    h.onmousedown=null;
    h.onpointerdown=null;
    h.onmousedown=function(e){
      if(e.button!==0)return;
      if(e.target.closest('button,input,select,textarea,a'))return;
      e.preventDefault();
      var r=w.getBoundingClientRect();
      var sx=e.clientX,sy=e.clientY,ox=r.left,oy=r.top;
      w.style.setProperty('position','fixed','important');
      w.style.setProperty('transform','none','important');
      w.style.setProperty('left',ox+'px','important');
      w.style.setProperty('top',oy+'px','important');
      w.style.setProperty('right','auto','important');
      w.style.setProperty('bottom','auto','important');
      w.style.setProperty('margin','0','important');
      w.style.setProperty('z-index','2147483646','important');
      function move(ev){
        var x=ox+(ev.clientX-sx),y=oy+(ev.clientY-sy);
        var maxX=Math.max(0,window.innerWidth-w.getBoundingClientRect().width);
        x=Math.max(0,Math.min(maxX,x));
        y=Math.max(0,y);
        w.style.setProperty('left',x+'px','important');
        w.style.setProperty('top',y+'px','important');
      }
      function stop(){
        document.removeEventListener('mousemove',move,true);
        document.removeEventListener('mouseup',stop,true);
      }
      document.addEventListener('mousemove',move,true);
      document.addEventListener('mouseup',stop,true);
    };
  }
  function installAll(){
    document.querySelectorAll('.window').forEach(install);
  }
  window.installFinalWindowDrag=installAll;
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',installAll);
  else installAll();
  setTimeout(installAll,100);
  setTimeout(installAll,500);
  setTimeout(installAll,1200);
  setTimeout(installAll,2500);
  new MutationObserver(function(){installAll()}).observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>

<script id="clean-media-player-fix-js-2026-09-20">
(function(){
  function cleanExtraControls(){
    document.querySelectorAll('.media-player-controls').forEach(function(x){x.remove()});
  }
  function renderClean(){
    var box=document.getElementById('mediaStageContent');
    if(!box)return;
    cleanExtraControls();
    if(!window.media||!media.length){
      box.innerHTML='<span class="media-center-badge">মিডিয়া আপলোড করে স্লাইড দেখুন</span>';
      var pb=document.getElementById('mediaPlayBtn');if(pb)pb.textContent='▶ Play';
      return;
    }
    mediaIndex=((Number(mediaIndex)||0)%media.length+media.length)%media.length;
    var x=media[mediaIndex],u=String(x.DataURL||'');
    var badge='<span class="media-center-badge">'+(typeof esc==='function'?esc(x.Name||('Media '+(mediaIndex+1))):'Media')+'</span>';
    if(x.Type==='image')box.innerHTML=badge+'<img src="'+u+'" alt="">';
    else if(x.Type==='video')box.innerHTML=badge+'<video src="'+u+'" controls playsinline preload="metadata"></video>';
    else box.innerHTML=badge+'<audio src="'+u+'" controls preload="metadata"></audio>';
    box.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<button class="media-arrow-new left" type="button" onclick="prevMedia()">❮</button><button class="media-arrow-new right" type="button" onclick="nextMedia()">❯</button>');
    var pb=document.getElementById('mediaPlayBtn');if(pb)pb.textContent='▶ Play';
    if(typeof renderMediaList==='function')renderMediaList();
  }
  window.renderMedia=renderClean;
  window.toggleMediaPlay=function(){
    var el=document.querySelector('#mediaStageContent video,#mediaStageContent audio');
    var pb=document.getElementById('mediaPlayBtn');
    if(el){
      if(el.paused){
        var p=el.play();
        if(p&&p.then)p.then(function(){if(pb)pb.textContent='⏸ Pause'}).catch(function(){if(pb)pb.textContent='▶ Play';toast('এই মিডিয়া প্লে করা যাচ্ছে না।')});
      }else{el.pause();if(pb)pb.textContent='▶ Play'}
    }else if(window.media&&media.length){
      if(window.dashboardMediaPlaying){
        window.dashboardMediaPlaying=false;clearInterval(window.dashboardMediaTimer);if(pb)pb.textContent='▶ Play';
      }else{
        window.dashboardMediaPlaying=true;clearInterval(window.dashboardMediaTimer);
        window.dashboardMediaTimer=setInterval(function(){nextMedia()},5000);
        if(pb)pb.textContent='⏸ Pause';
      }
    }
  };
  window.nextMedia=function(){if(window.media&&media.length){mediaIndex=(Number(mediaIndex)+1)%media.length;renderClean()}};
  window.prevMedia=function(){if(window.media&&media.length){mediaIndex=(Number(mediaIndex)-1+media.length)%media.length;renderClean()}};
  window.deleteCurrentMedia=async function(){
    if(!window.media||!media.length)return toast('ডিলেট করার মিডিয়া নেই।');
    var x=media[mediaIndex];
    var r=await call('deleteMedia',token,x.Serial);
    if(r&&r.ok){media.splice(mediaIndex,1);mediaIndex=media.length?mediaIndex%media.length:0;renderClean();toast('মিডিয়া মুছে ফেলা হয়েছে।')}else toast((r&&r.message)||'ডিলিট ব্যর্থ');
  };
  function start(){cleanExtraControls();if(window.media&&media.length)renderClean()}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',start);else start();
  setTimeout(start,500);setTimeout(start,1500);
})();
</script>

<script id="final-clean-media-controls-js-2026-09-20">
(function(){
 function ensureList(){
   var controls=document.querySelector('#mediaStage .dashboard-slider-controls');
   if(!controls)return null;
   var list=document.getElementById('mediaDeleteList');
   if(!list){list=document.createElement('div');list.id='mediaDeleteList';controls.parentElement.appendChild(list);}
   return list;
 }
 function clean(){
   var controls=document.querySelector('#mediaStage .dashboard-slider-controls');
   if(controls){
     controls.querySelectorAll('.slider-arrow').forEach(function(b){b.style.display='none';});
     var load=controls.querySelector('[onclick*="openModule"]');
     var play=controls.querySelector('[onclick*="toggleMediaPlay"]');
     var del=controls.querySelector('[onclick*="deleteCurrentMedia"]');
     if(load){load.textContent='⬆️ লোড';load.style.display='inline-flex';}
     if(play){play.textContent='▶️ প্লে';play.style.display='inline-flex';}
     if(del){del.textContent='🗑️ ডিলেট';del.style.display='inline-flex';}
   }
   var box=document.getElementById('mediaStageContent');
   if(box){
     box.querySelectorAll('.media-center-badge,.media-arrow-new').forEach(function(e){e.remove();});
   }
   ensureList();
 }
 window.deleteCurrentMedia=async function(){
   var list=ensureList(); if(!list)return;
   /* Always refresh from server first, so media uploaded in Gallery is visible here. */
   try{
     var fresh=await call('listMedia',token);
     if(Array.isArray(fresh))window.media=fresh;
   }catch(e){}
   if(!window.media||!media.length){
     list.innerHTML='<div>ডিলেট করার কোনো মিডিয়া নেই।</div>';list.classList.add('show');return;
   }
   list.innerHTML='<div style="font-weight:900;margin-bottom:6px">মিডিয়া তালিকা — যেটি মুছতে চান তার ডিলেট চাপুন</div>'+
     media.map(function(x,i){
       var n=typeof escapeHtml==='function'?escapeHtml(x.Name||('Media '+(i+1))):String(x.Name||('Media '+(i+1)));
       return '<div class="media-delete-row"><span>'+n+'</span><button type="button" class="secondary" onclick="deleteMediaByIndex('+i+')">🗑️ ডিলেট</button></div>';
     }).join('');
   list.classList.add('show');
   mediaIndex=((Number(mediaIndex)||0)%media.length+media.length)%media.length;
   renderAuthoritative();
 };
 window.deleteMediaByIndex=async function(i){
   if(!window.media||!media[i])return;
   var x=media[i],r=await call('deleteMedia',token,x.Serial);
   if(r&&r.ok){
     media.splice(i,1);
     if(mediaIndex>=media.length)mediaIndex=Math.max(0,media.length-1);
     renderMedia();clean();
     var list=ensureList();if(list)window.deleteCurrentMedia();
     toast('মিডিয়া মুছে ফেলা হয়েছে।');
   }else toast((r&&r.message)||'ডিলেট ব্যর্থ');
 };
 window.renderMedia=function(){
   var box=document.getElementById('mediaStageContent');if(!box)return;
   if(!window.media||!media.length){box.innerHTML='<span>মিডিয়া লোড করে দেখুন</span>';clean();return;}
   mediaIndex=((Number(mediaIndex)||0)%media.length+media.length)%media.length;
   var x=media[mediaIndex],u=String(x.DataURL||''),t=String(x.Type||'').toLowerCase();
   if(t==='image')box.innerHTML='<img src="'+u+'" alt="">';
   else if(t==='video'){
     var vid=(u.match(/[?&]id=([^&]+)/)||[])[1]||'',m=u.match(/\/d\/([^/]+)/);if(!vid&&m)vid=m[1];
     box.innerHTML=vid?'<iframe class="drive-media-frame" src="https://drive.google.com/file/d/'+encodeURIComponent(vid)+'/preview" allow="autoplay; fullscreen" allowfullscreen></iframe>':'<span>ভিডিও পাওয়া যায়নি</span>';
   }else{
     var aid=(u.match(/[?&]id=([^&]+)/)||[])[1]||'',am=u.match(/\/d\/([^/]+)/);if(!aid&&am)aid=am[1];
     box.innerHTML=aid?'<iframe class="drive-media-frame" src="https://drive.google.com/file/d/'+encodeURIComponent(aid)+'/preview" allow="autoplay" allowfullscreen></iframe>':'<span>অডিও পাওয়া যায়নি</span>';
   }
   clean();
 };
 window.toggleMediaPlay=function(){
   var el=document.querySelector('#mediaStageContent video,#mediaStageContent audio');
   if(el){if(el.paused){el.play().catch(function(){toast('এই মিডিয়া চালানো যাচ্ছে না।')});}else el.pause();}
   else if(window.media&&media.length){toast('প্লেয়ার থেকে ▶ চাপুন।');}
 };
 function start(){clean();}
 if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',start);else start();
 setTimeout(start,300);setTimeout(start,1000);
})();
</script>

}

<script id="media-authoritative-player-js-2026-09-20">
(function(){
  function driveId(u){
    var s=String(u||''),m=s.match(/[?&]id=([^&]+)/);
    if(m)return decodeURIComponent(m[1]);    m=s.match(/\/d\/([^/]+)/);
    return m?m[1]:'';
  }
  function arrows(){
    var box=document.getElementById('mediaStageContent');
    if(!box)return;
    box.querySelectorAll('.media-inner-arrow').forEach(function(x){x.remove()});
    if(!window.media||!media.length)return;
    box.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',
      '<button type="button" class="media-inner-arrow left" onclick="prevMedia()" aria-label="আগের মিডিয়া">❮</button>'+
      '<button type="button" class="media-inner-arrow right" onclick="nextMedia()" aria-label="পরের মিডিয়া">❯</button>');
  }
  function renderAuthoritative(){
    var box=document.getElementById('mediaStageContent');
    if(!box)return;
    if(!window.media||!media.length){
      box.innerHTML='<span class="media-empty">মিডিয়া লোড করে দেখুন</span>';
      return;
    }
    mediaIndex=((Number(mediaIndex)||0)%media.length+media.length)%media.length;
    var x=media[mediaIndex]||{},u=String(x.DataURL||''),t=String(x.Type||'').toLowerCase(),id=driveId(u);
    box.innerHTML='';
    if(t==='image'){
      var im=document.createElement('img');
      im.src=u; im.alt=''; im.style.cssText='width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:contain;background:#111';
      box.appendChild(im);
    }else if(id){
      var fr=document.createElement('iframe');
      fr.className='media-drive-player';
      fr.allow='autoplay; fullscreen; encrypted-media; picture-in-picture';
      fr.setAttribute('allowfullscreen','');
      fr.loading='eager';
      fr.src='https://drive.google.com/file/'+encodeURIComponent(id)+'/preview?rm=minimal';
      box.appendChild(fr);
    }else{
      box.innerHTML='<span class="media-empty">মিডিয়ার লিংক পাওয়া যায়নি।</span>';
    }
    arrows();
    var pb=document.getElementById('mediaPlayBtn');
    if(pb)pb.textContent='▶ প্লে';
    if(typeof renderMediaList==='function')renderMediaList();
    ensureAutoSlide();
  }
  window.renderMedia=renderAuthoritative;
  window.nextMedia=function(){
    if(window.media&&media.length){mediaIndex=(Number(mediaIndex)+1)%media.length;renderAuthoritative();}
  };
  window.prevMedia=function(){
    if(window.media&&media.length){mediaIndex=(Number(mediaIndex)-1+media.length)%media.length;renderAuthoritative();}
  };
  window.toggleMediaPlay=function(){
    var frame=document.querySelector('#mediaStageContent .media-drive-player');
    if(!frame){renderAuthoritative();return;}
    try{frame.contentWindow.postMessage({type:'play'},'*');}catch(e){}
    frame.focus();
  };
  function ensureAutoSlide(){
    clearInterval(window.finalMediaAutoSlideTimer);
    window.finalMediaAutoSlideTimer=null;
    if(window.media&&media.length>1){
      window.finalMediaAutoSlideTimer=setInterval(function(){
        if(window.media&&media.length>1){
          mediaIndex=(Number(mediaIndex)+1)%media.length;
          renderAuthoritative();
        }
      },5000);
    }
  }
  function start(){
    if(window.media&&media.length)renderAuthoritative();
    else arrows();
    ensureAutoSlide();
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',start);else start();
  setTimeout(start,400);setTimeout(start,1000);
})();
</script>
<!-- ===== FINAL MEDIA PLAYBACK FIX — DIRECT DRIVE MEDIA URL 2026-09-21 ===== -->
<style id="final-direct-media-playback-fix-2026-09-21">
#mediaStageContent video,#mediaStageContent audio{width:100%!important;height:100%!important;object-fit:contain!important;background:#111!important;display:block!important}
#mediaStageContent audio{width:90%!important;height:auto!important;max-height:80px!important}</style>
<script id="final-direct-media-playback-js-2026-09-21">
(function(){
  function fileId(u){
    var s=String(u||''),m=s.match(/[?&]id=([^&]+)/);
    if(m)return decodeURIComponent(m[1]);    m=s.match(/\/d\/([^/]+)/);
    return m?m[1]:'';
  }
  function directUrl(u){
    var s=String(u||'');
    if(/^https?:\/\//i.test(s) && s.indexOf('drive.google.com/uc?')>=0)return s;
    var id=fileId(s);
    return id?'https://drive.google.com/uc?export=download&id='+encodeURIComponent(id):s;
  }
  function renderFinal(){
    var box=document.getElementById('mediaStageContent');
    if(!box)return;
    if(!window.media||!media.length){box.innerHTML='<span class="media-empty">মিডিয়া লোড করে দেখুন</span>';return}
    mediaIndex=((Number(mediaIndex)||0)%media.length+media.length)%media.length;
    var x=media[mediaIndex]||{},u=directUrl(x.DataURL||''),t=String(x.Type||'').toLowerCase();
    box.innerHTML='';
    if(t==='image'){
      var im=document.createElement('img');im.src=u;im.alt='';
      im.style.cssText='width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:contain;background:#111;display:block';box.appendChild(im);
    }else if(t==='video'){
      var v=document.createElement('video');v.src=u;v.controls=true;v.playsInline=true;v.preload='metadata';
      v.style.cssText='width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:contain;background:#111;display:block';box.appendChild(v);
    }else if(t==='audio'){
      var a=document.createElement('audio');a.src=u;a.controls=true;a.preload='metadata';
      a.style.cssText='width:90%;max-height:80px';box.appendChild(a);
    }else box.innerHTML='<span class="media-empty">মিডিয়ার ধরন পাওয়া যায়নি।</span>';
  }
  window.renderMedia=renderFinal;
  window.nextMedia=function(){if(window.media&&media.length){mediaIndex=(Number(mediaIndex)+1)%media.length;renderFinal()}};
  window.prevMedia=function(){if(window.media&&media.length){mediaIndex=(Number(mediaIndex)-1+media.length)%media.length;renderFinal()}};
  window.toggleMediaPlay=function(){
    var el=document.querySelector('#mediaStageContent video,#mediaStageContent audio');
    if(!el){renderFinal();return}
    if(el.paused){var p=el.play();if(p&&p.catch)p.catch(function(){toast('ভিডিওটি চালানো যাচ্ছে না। Drive ফাইলটি Anyone with the link — Viewer হতে হবে।')})}else el.pause();
  };
  function auto(){
    clearInterval(window.finalMediaAutoSlideTimer);window.finalMediaAutoSlideTimer=null;
    if(window.media&&media.length>1)window.finalMediaAutoSlideTimer=setInterval(function(){
      if(window.media&&media.length>1){mediaIndex=(Number(mediaIndex)+1)%media.length;renderFinal()}
    },5000);
  }
  function start(){if(window.media&&media.length)renderFinal();auto()}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',start);else start();
  setTimeout(start,700);setTimeout(start,1800);
})();
</script>

<!-- ===== FINAL MEDIA DIRECT PLAYBACK — NO URI FIELD 2026-09-21 ===== -->
<script id="final-media-direct-playback-2026-09-21">
(function(){
  function mediaUrl(x){
    var u=String((x&&x.DirectURL)||'').trim();
    if(u)return u;
    var id=String((x&&x.FileID)||x&&x.DriveFileId||'').trim();
    var rk=String((x&&x.ResourceKey)||'').trim();
    if(!id)return '';
    return 'https://drive.google.com/uc?export=download&id='+encodeURIComponent(id)+(rk?'&resourcekey='+encodeURIComponent(rk):'');
  }
  function previewUrl(x){
    var id=String((x&&x.FileID)||x&&x.DriveFileId||'').trim();
    var rk=String((x&&x.ResourceKey)||'').trim();
    return id?'https://drive.google.com/file/d/'+encodeURIComponent(id)+'/preview'+(rk?'?resourcekey='+encodeURIComponent(rk):''):'';
  }
  function render(){
    var box=document.getElementById('mediaStageContent');
    if(!box)return;
    if(!window.media||!media.length){
      box.innerHTML='<span class="media-empty">মিডিয়া লোড করে দেখুন</span>';return;
    }
    mediaIndex=((Number(mediaIndex)||0)%media.length+media.length)%media.length;
    var x=media[mediaIndex]||{},u=mediaUrl(x),p=previewUrl(x),t=String(x.Type||'').toLowerCase();
    box.innerHTML='';
    if(t==='image'){
      var im=new Image();
      im.src=u;im.alt=x.Name||'';
      im.style.cssText='width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:contain;background:#111;display:block';
      im.onerror=function(){if(p)showPreview(box,p);else box.innerHTML='<span class="media-empty">ফটো চালানো যাচ্ছে না।</span>'};
      box.appendChild(im);
    }else if(t==='video'){
      var v=document.createElement('video');
      v.controls=true;v.playsInline=true;v.preload='metadata';
      v.setAttribute('controlsList','nodownload');
      v.style.cssText='width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:contain;background:#111;display:block';
      v.src=u;
      v.onerror=function(){if(p)showPreview(box,p);else toast('ভিডিও চালানো যাচ্ছে না।')};
      box.appendChild(v);
    }else if(t==='audio'){
      var a=document.createElement('audio');
      a.controls=true;a.preload='metadata';a.src=u;
      a.style.cssText='width:90%;max-height:80px';
      a.onerror=function(){if(p)showPreview(box,p);else toast('অডিও চালানো যাচ্ছে না.')};
      box.appendChild(a);
    }else{
      box.innerHTML='<span class="media-empty">মিডিয়া টাইপ সঠিক নয়।</span>';
    }
    if(typeof renderMediaList==='function')renderMediaList();
  }
  function showPreview(box,url){
    box.innerHTML='';
    var fr=document.createElement('iframe');
    fr.className='media-drive-player';
    fr.allow='autoplay; fullscreen; encrypted-media; picture-in-picture';
    fr.setAttribute('allowfullscreen','');
    fr.style.cssText='width:100%;height:100%;border:0;background:#111';
    fr.src=url;
    box.appendChild(fr);
  }
  window.renderMedia=render;
  window.nextMedia=function(){if(window.media&&media.length){mediaIndex=(Number(mediaIndex)+1)%media.length;render()}};
  window.prevMedia=function(){if(window.media&&media.length){mediaIndex=(Number(mediaIndex)-1+media.length)%media.length;render()}};
  window.toggleMediaPlay=function(){
    var el=document.querySelector('#mediaStageContent video,#mediaStageContent audio');
    if(!el){render();return}
    if(el.paused){var p=el.play();if(p&&p.catch)p.catch(function(){toast('মিডিয়া চালানো যাচ্ছে না।')})}else el.pause();
  };
  function load(){
    if(typeof call==='function'&&token){
      call('listMedia',token).then(function(r){
        if(Array.isArray(r)){window.media=r;mediaIndex=Math.min(Number(mediaIndex)||0,Math.max(0,media.length-1));render()}
        else if(r&&Array.isArray(r.media)){window.media=r.media;render()}
      }).catch(function(){});
    }else if(window.media&&media.length)render();
  }
  function start(){load()}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',start);else start();
  setTimeout(start,900);setTimeout(start,2200);
})();
</script>

<script id="consolidated-final-ui-js-2026-09-21">
(function(){
  function makeNews(el){
    if(!el)return;
    var old=el.querySelector('.running-news-text');
    if(!old){
      var text='';
      Array.prototype.slice.call(el.childNodes).forEach(function(n){
        if(n.nodeType===3)text+=n.nodeValue||'';
      });
      old=document.createElement('span');
      old.className='running-news-text';
      old.textContent=(text||el.dataset.news||'জান্নাতুল বাক্বী মহিলা মাদ্রাসা ও এতিমখানার ডিজিটাল প্লাটফর্মে আপনাকে স্বাগতম').trim();
      el.innerHTML='';
      el.appendChild(old);
    }
    var run=old.querySelector('.news-full-run');
    if(!run){
      var v=(old.textContent||'').trim();
      old.innerHTML='';
      run=document.createElement('span');
      run.className='news-full-run';
      run.textContent=v||'জান্নাতুল বাক্বী মহিলা মাদ্রাসা ও এতিমখানার ডিজিটাল প্লাটফর্মে আপনাকে স্বাগতম';
      old.appendChild(run);
      run.style.display='inline-block';
      run.style.width='max-content';
      run.style.animation='finalNewsRun 22s linear infinite';
    }
    var mic=el.querySelector('.news-mic');
    if(!mic){
      mic=document.createElement('button');
      mic.type='button';mic.className='news-mic';
      mic.textContent='🎙️';mic.title='নতুন নিউজ যোগ করুন';
      mic.onclick=function(){
        if(window.openRunningNewsEditor)window.openRunningNewsEditor();
      };
      el.appendChild(mic);
    }
  }
  function clamp(w,x,y){
    var ww=w.offsetWidth;
    x=Math.max(0,Math.min(Math.max(0,innerWidth-ww),x));
    /* No taskbar/top-bar stopping point: top edge may reach viewport bottom. */
    y=Math.max(0,Math.min(innerHeight,y));
    w.style.setProperty('left',x+'px','important');
    w.style.setProperty('top',y+'px','important');
    w.style.setProperty('transform','none','important');
  }
  function bindWindow(w){
    if(!w||w.dataset.finalXY==='1')return;
    var h=w.querySelector('.win-head');if(!h)return;
    w.dataset.finalXY='1';
    h.addEventListener('mousedown',function(e){
      if(e.button!==0||e.target.closest('button,input,select,textarea,a,label'))return;
      var r=w.getBoundingClientRect(),sx=e.clientX,sy=e.clientY;
      var ox=r.left,oy=r.top,dragging=true;
      function move(ev){
        if(!dragging)return;
        clamp(w,ox+ev.clientX-sx,oy+ev.clientY-sy);
      }
      function up(){
        dragging=false;
        document.removeEventListener('mousemove',move);
        document.removeEventListener('mouseup',up);
      }
      document.addEventListener('mousemove',move);
      document.addEventListener('mouseup',up);
      e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
    },true);
  }
  function init(){
    document.querySelectorAll('#app .header-mainline .header-news').forEach(makeNews);
    document.querySelectorAll('#app .window,#app .form-window').forEach(bindWindow);
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',init);else init();
  setTimeout(init,300);setTimeout(init,1000);setInterval(init,2000);
})();
</script>

<script id="user-task-1-final-layout-js-2026-09-21">
(function(){
  function fix(){
    var bar=document.querySelector('#app .top-action-bar');
    if(bar){
      bar.style.position='fixed';
      bar.style.top='0';
      bar.style.left='0';
      bar.style.right='0';
      bar.style.zIndex='1000000';
    }
    var rows=document.querySelectorAll('#app .header > .header-running-row');
    if(rows.length>1) rows[0].style.display='none';
    var news=document.querySelector('#app .header-mainline .header-news');
    if(news && !news.querySelector('.news-mic')){
      var mic=document.createElement('button');
      mic.type='button';mic.className='news-mic';mic.textContent='🎙️';
      mic.title='নতুন নিউজ যোগ করুন';
      mic.onclick=function(){if(window.openRunningNewsEditor)window.openRunningNewsEditor();};
      news.appendChild(mic);
    }
    var logo=document.getElementById('headerLogo');
    if(logo){
      logo.style.width='105px';
      logo.style.height='105px';
      logo.style.objectFit='contain';
      logo.style.background='transparent';
      logo.style.border='0';
    }
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',fix);
  else fix();
  setTimeout(fix,300);setTimeout(fix,1000);setInterval(fix,2500);
})();
</script>

<script id="user-task-1-news-window-fix-2026-09-21">
(function(){
  var DEFAULT_NEWS='জান্নাতুল বাক্বী মহিলা মাদ্রাসা ও এতিমখানার ডিজিটাল প্লাটফর্মে আপনাকে স্বাগতম';

  function getNewsText(){
    var saved=localStorage.getItem('jannatulBaqiRunningNews');
    if(saved)return saved;
    var el=document.querySelector('#app .header-mainline .header-news .running-news-text');
    return (el&&el.textContent.trim())||DEFAULT_NEWS;
  }

  function applyNews(text){
    text=String(text||'').trim()||DEFAULT_NEWS;
    localStorage.setItem('jannatulBaqiRunningNews',text);
    document.querySelectorAll('#app .header-news .running-news-text').forEach(function(el){
      var run=el.querySelector('.news-full-run');
      if(run)run.textContent=text;
      else el.textContent=text;
    });
    document.querySelectorAll('#app .header-news').forEach(function(box){
      box.setAttribute('data-news',text);
    });
  }

  window.openRunningNewsEditor=function(){
    var id='runningNewsEditorWin';
    var old=document.getElementById(id);
    if(!old){
      old=document.createElement('div');
      old.id=id;
      old.className='window running-news-editor-window';
      old.style.cssText='position:fixed;z-index:1000002;display:block;width:min(620px,92vw);top:110px;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);';
      old.innerHTML=
        '<div class="win-head" style="cursor:move"><b>📢 চলমান নিউজ লিখুন</b>'+
        '<button class="secondary" type="button" id="closeRunningNewsEditor">✕</button></div>'+
        '<div class="win-body">'+
        '<label style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;font-weight:800">নিউজ / ঘোষণা'+
        '<textarea id="runningNewsEditorText" class="field" rows="6" placeholder="চলমান নিউজ লিখুন..."></textarea></label>'+
        '<div style="display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;gap:7px;margin-top:10px">'+
        '<button class="secondary" type="button" id="cancelRunningNewsEditor">বাতিল</button>'+
        '<button class="primary" type="button" id="saveRunningNewsEditor">💾 সংরক্ষণ</button>'+
        '</div></div>';
      document.body.appendChild(old);
      document.getElementById('closeRunningNewsEditor').onclick=function(){old.remove();};
      document.getElementById('cancelRunningNewsEditor').onclick=function(){old.remove();};
      document.getElementById('saveRunningNewsEditor').onclick=function(){
        applyNews(document.getElementById('runningNewsEditorText').value);
        old.remove();
        if(window.toast)toast('চলমান নিউজ সংরক্ষণ হয়েছে।');
      };
      var head=old.querySelector('.win-head');
      head.addEventListener('mousedown',function(e){
        if(e.target.closest('button'))return;
        var r=old.getBoundingClientRect(),sx=e.clientX,sy=e.clientY,ox=r.left,oy=r.top;
        function mv(ev){
          old.style.transform='none';
          old.style.left=Math.max(0,Math.min(innerWidth-old.offsetWidth,ox+ev.clientX-sx))+'px';
          old.style.top=Math.max(0,Math.min(innerHeight-20,oy+ev.clientY-sy))+'px';
        }
        function up(){document.removeEventListener('mousemove',mv);document.removeEventListener('mouseup',up);}
        document.addEventListener('mousemove',mv);document.addEventListener('mouseup',up);
        e.preventDefault();
      });
    }
    var ta=document.getElementById('runningNewsEditorText');
    if(ta)ta.value=getNewsText();
    old.style.display='block';
    if(ta){ta.focus();ta.select();}
  };

  function ensureMic(){
    var news=document.querySelector('#app .header-mainline .header-news');
    if(!news)return;
    var mic=news.querySelector('.news-mic');
    if(!mic){
      mic=document.createElement('button');
      mic.type='button';
      mic.className='news-mic';
      mic.textContent='🎙️';
      mic.title='নতুন নিউজ লিখুন';
      news.appendChild(mic);
    }
    mic.onclick=window.openRunningNewsEditor;
  }

  function applySaved(){
    var saved=localStorage.getItem('jannatulBaqiRunningNews');
    if(saved){
      document.querySelectorAll('#app .header-news .running-news-text').forEach(function(el){
        var run=el.querySelector('.news-full-run');
        if(run)run.textContent=saved;
        else el.textContent=saved;
      });
    }
  }

  function init(){
    ensureMic();
    applySaved();
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',init);else init();
  setTimeout(init,300);
  setTimeout(init,1000);
  setInterval(init,2500);
})();
</script>

<script id="task1-final-mic-v2">
(function(){
  function bindMic(){
    document.querySelectorAll('#app .header-mainline .header-news').forEach(function(news){
      var mic=news.querySelector('.news-mic');
      if(!mic){
        mic=document.createElement('button');
        mic.type='button';
        mic.className='news-mic';
        mic.textContent='🎙️';
        mic.title='নতুন নিউজ লিখুন';
        news.appendChild(mic);
      }
      mic.onclick=function(e){
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
        if(typeof window.openRunningNewsEditor==='function'){
          window.openRunningNewsEditor();
        }else{
          alert('চলমান নিউজ উইন্ডো প্রস্তুত হচ্ছে। আবার মাইকে চাপুন।');
        }
      };
    });
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',bindMic);else bindMic();
  setTimeout(bindMic,300);setTimeout(bindMic,1000);setInterval(bindMic,2000);
})();
</script>

.window .win-edge-down-2026{bottom:2px!important;left:50%!important;transform:translateX(-50%)!important}
.window.win-fullscreen-2026{
  left:0!important;top:0!important;width:100vw!important;height:100vh!important;
  max-width:none!important;max-height:none!important;transform:none!important;
  border-radius:0!important;
}
.window.win-fullscreen-2026 .win-body{max-height:calc(100vh - 55px)!important;overflow:auto!important}
.window.win-fullscreen-2026 .win-edge-arrow-2026{display:none!important}
@media(max-width:700px){
  .window .win-edge-arrow-2026{width:24px!important;height:24px!important}
  .window .win-head .win-fullscreen-2026{width:32px!important;min-width:32px!important}
}

<script id="all-window-fullscreen-resize-js-2026-09-22">
(function(){
  var MIN_W=320, MIN_H=180, STEP=80, initialized=new WeakSet();

  function isWindow(el){
    return el && el.classList && el.classList.contains('window');
  }
  function ensureControls(w){
    if(!isWindow(w))return;
    var head=w.querySelector(':scope > .win-head') || w.querySelector('.win-head');
    if(!head)return;

    /* Convert the existing top-right close control into fullscreen, without deleting it. */
    var oldFs=w.querySelector('.win-fullscreen-2026');
    if(!oldFs){
      oldFs=document.createElement('button');
      oldFs.type='button';
      oldFs.className='win-fullscreen-2026';
      oldFs.title='ফুল স্ক্রিন / স্বাভাবিক';
      oldFs.setAttribute('aria-label','ফুল স্ক্রিন / স্বাভাবিক');
      oldFs.textContent='⛶';
      var tools=head.querySelector('.win-tools-2026');
      if(!tools){
        tools=document.createElement('span');
        tools.className='win-tools-2026';
        head.appendChild(tools);
      }
      var close=head.querySelector('button');
      if(close && !close.classList.contains('win-fullscreen-2026')){        /* The first header button is the previous close button in the existing windows. */
        close.textContent='⛶';
        close.title='ফুল স্ক্রিন / স্বাভাবিক';
        close.setAttribute('aria-label','ফুল স্ক্রিন / স্বাভাবিক');
        close.classList.add('win-fullscreen-2026');
        oldFs=close;
      }else{
        tools.appendChild(oldFs);
      }
    }
    if(!oldFs.dataset.fsBound){
      oldFs.dataset.fsBound='1';
      oldFs.addEventListener('click',function(e){
        e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
        toggleFullscreen(w);
      });
    }

    var specs=[
      ['left','‹','বাম দিক থেকে ছোট / বড়','win-edge-left-2026'],
      ['right','›','ডান দিক থেকে ছোট / বড়','win-edge-right-2026'],
      ['up','⌃','উপর থেকে ছোট / বড়','win-edge-up-2026'],
      ['down','⌄','নিচ থেকে ছোট / বড়','win-edge-down-2026']
    ];
    specs.forEach(function(s){
      if(w.querySelector('.'+s[3]))return;
      var b=document.createElement('button');
      b.type='button';b.className='win-edge-arrow-2026 '+s[3];
      b.textContent=s[1];b.title=s[2];b.setAttribute('aria-label',s[2]);
      b.addEventListener('click',function(e){
        e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
        resizeFromEdge(w,s[0]);
      });
      w.appendChild(b);
    });

    if(!w.dataset.edgeState){
      w.dataset.edgeState='1';
      w.dataset.edgeW=Math.round(w.getBoundingClientRect().width||700);
      w.dataset.edgeH=Math.round(w.getBoundingClientRect().height||450);
    }
  }

  function clamp(v,min,max){return Math.max(min,Math.min(max,v));}
  function resizeFromEdge(w,side){
    if(w.classList.contains('win-fullscreen-2026'))return;
    var r=w.getBoundingClientRect();
    var vw=window.innerWidth, vh=window.innerHeight;
    var w0=r.width, h0=r.height, l=r.left, t=r.top;
    if(side==='left'){
      var nw=clamp(w0-STEP,MIN_W,vw-20);
      w.style.width=nw+'px';
      w.style.left=clamp(l+STEP,0,vw-nw)+'px';
      w.style.transform='none';
    }else if(side==='right'){
      w.style.width=clamp(w0-STEP,MIN_W,vw-20)+'px';
      w.style.transform='none';
      w.style.left=clamp(l,0,vw-w.offsetWidth)+'px';
    }else if(side==='up'){
      var nh=clamp(h0-STEP,MIN_H,vh-20);
      w.style.height=nh+'px';w.style.maxHeight=nh+'px';
      w.style.top=clamp(t+STEP,0,vh-nh)+'px';w.style.transform='none';
    }else if(side==='down'){
      var nh2=clamp(h0-STEP,MIN_H,vh-20);
      w.style.height=nh2+'px';w.style.maxHeight=nh2+'px';
      w.style.top=clamp(t,0,vh-nh2)+'px';w.style.transform='none';
    }
  }

  function toggleFullscreen(w){
    if(!w)return;
    if(w.classList.contains('win-fullscreen-2026')){
      w.classList.remove('win-fullscreen-2026');
      w.style.width=w.dataset.prevWidth||'';
      w.style.height=w.dataset.prevHeight||'';
      w.style.maxHeight=w.dataset.prevMaxHeight||'82vh';
      w.style.left=w.dataset.prevLeft||'50%';
      w.style.top=w.dataset.prevTop||'90px';
      w.style.transform=w.dataset.prevTransform||'translateX(-50%)';    }else{
      var r=w.getBoundingClientRect();
      w.dataset.prevWidth=r.width+'px';
      w.dataset.prevHeight=r.height+'px';      w.dataset.prevMaxHeight=w.style.maxHeight||'82vh';
      w.dataset.prevLeft=r.left+'px';
      w.dataset.prevTop=r.top+'px';
      w.dataset.prevTransform=w.style.transform||'none';
      w.classList.add('win-fullscreen-2026');
    }
  }

  function scan(){
    document.querySelectorAll('.window').forEach(ensureControls);
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',scan);else scan();
  setTimeout(scan,300);setTimeout(scan,1000);setInterval(scan,2000);

  /* Newly-created form windows are covered automatically. */
  var mo=new MutationObserver(function(list){
    list.forEach(function(m){
      m.addedNodes&&m.addedNodes.forEach(function(n){
        if(n.nodeType!==1)return;
        if(isWindow(n))ensureControls(n);
        n.querySelectorAll&&n.querySelectorAll('.window').forEach(ensureControls);
      });
    });
  });
  if(document.body)mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>


<!-- ===== USER TASK 2 CORRECTION: FULLSCREEN AT CLEAR BUTTON, KEEP CLOSE — 2026-09-22 ===== -->
<style id="fullscreen-at-clear-correction-2026-09-22">
.window .win-head .win-fullscreen-2026{display:none!important}
.window .win-head .win-clear-fullscreen-2026{
  min-width:34px!important;width:34px!important;height:30px!important;padding:0!important;
  display:inline-flex!important;align-items:center!important;justify-content:center!important;
  border:2px solid var(--border)!important;border-radius:7px!important;
  background:var(--panel)!important;color:inherit!important;font-weight:900!important;cursor:pointer!important;
}

<script id="fullscreen-at-clear-correction-js-2026-09-22">
(function(){
  function add(w){
    if(!w||!w.classList||!w.classList.contains('window'))return;
    var head=w.querySelector('.win-head'); if(!head)return;
    /* Restore the real Close button if the previous patch changed it. */
    var oldFs=head.querySelector('.win-fullscreen-2026');
    if(oldFs){
      oldFs.textContent='✕';
      oldFs.title='বন্ধ করুন';
      oldFs.setAttribute('aria-label','বন্ধ করুন');
      oldFs.classList.remove('win-fullscreen-2026');
      oldFs.classList.remove('win-clear-fullscreen-2026');
      oldFs.removeAttribute('data-fs-bound');
    }
    /* Remove any previous correction button before rebuilding. */
    var existing=head.querySelector('.win-clear-fullscreen-2026');
    if(existing)existing.remove();

    var clear=null;
    var buttons=head.querySelectorAll('button');
    buttons.forEach(function(b){
      var txt=(b.textContent||'').trim().toLowerCase();
      var title=(b.title||'').trim().toLowerCase();
      if(!clear && (txt==='clear'||txt==='ক্লিয়ার'||txt==='ক্লিয়ার'||txt==='পরিষ্কার'||title.indexOf('clear')>=0||title.indexOf('ক্লিয়ার')>=0||title.indexOf('ক্লিয়ার')>=0)) clear=b;
    });
    /* If Clear is in the body/footer, use the existing clear button there. */
    if(!clear){
      w.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(function(b){
        var txt=(b.textContent||'').trim().toLowerCase();
        var title=(b.title||'').trim().toLowerCase();
        if(!clear && (txt==='clear'||txt==='ক্লিয়ার'||txt==='ক্লিয়ার'||txt==='পরিষ্কার'||title.indexOf('clear')>=0||title.indexOf('ক্লিয়ার')>=0||title.indexOf('ক্লিয়ার')>=0)) clear=b;
      });
    }
    if(clear){
      clear.textContent='⛶';
      clear.title='ফুল স্ক্রিন / স্বাভাবিক';
      clear.setAttribute('aria-label','ফুল স্ক্রিন / স্বাভাবিক');
      clear.classList.add('win-clear-fullscreen-2026');
      clear.dataset.clearReplacedForFullscreen='1';
      clear.onclick=function(e){
        e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
        if(typeof window.toggleWindowFullscreen2026==='function')window.toggleWindowFullscreen2026(w);
      };
    }
  }
  window.toggleWindowFullscreen2026=function(w){
    if(!w)return;
    if(w.classList.contains('win-fullscreen-2026')){
      w.classList.remove('win-fullscreen-2026');
      w.style.width=w.dataset.prevWidth||'';
      w.style.height=w.dataset.prevHeight||'';
      w.style.maxHeight=w.dataset.prevMaxHeight||'82vh';
      w.style.left=w.dataset.prevLeft||'50%';
      w.style.top=w.dataset.prevTop||'90px';
      w.style.transform=w.dataset.prevTransform||'translateX(-50%)';
    }else{
      var r=w.getBoundingClientRect();
      w.dataset.prevWidth=r.width+'px';w.dataset.prevHeight=r.height+'px';
      w.dataset.prevMaxHeight=w.style.maxHeight||'82vh';
      w.dataset.prevLeft=r.left+'px';w.dataset.prevTop=r.top+'px';
      w.dataset.prevTransform=w.style.transform||'none';
      w.classList.add('win-fullscreen-2026');
    }
  };
  function scan(){document.querySelectorAll('.window').forEach(add)}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',scan);else scan();
  setTimeout(scan,300);setTimeout(scan,1000);setInterval(scan,2000);
  new MutationObserver(function(ms){ms.forEach(function(m){m.addedNodes&&m.addedNodes.forEach(function(n){if(n.nodeType===1){if(n.matches&&n.matches('.window'))add(n);n.querySelectorAll&&n.querySelectorAll('.window').forEach(add)}})})}).observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>

<script id="user-task3-news-window-controls-js-2026-09-22">
(function(){
  function getTitle(w){
    var b=w.querySelector('.win-head b');
    return b ? (b.textContent||'উইন্ডো').trim() : 'উইন্ডো';
  }
  function taskbar(){
    var bar=document.getElementById('task3WinTaskbar');
    if(!bar){
      bar=document.createElement('div');
      bar.id='task3WinTaskbar';
      document.body.appendChild(bar);
    }
    return bar;
  }
  function addTaskbarItem(w){
    var bar=taskbar();
    var id='task3Restore_'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2);
    var btn=document.createElement('button');
    btn.type='button';
    btn.id=id;
    btn.textContent='▣ '+getTitle(w);
    btn.onclick=function(){
      w.classList.remove('task3-minimized-2026');
      btn.remove();
    };
    bar.appendChild(btn);
    w.dataset.task3BarId=id;
  }
  function removeTaskbarItem(w){
    var id=w.dataset.task3BarId;
    if(id){
      var b=document.getElementById(id);
      if(b)b.remove();
      delete w.dataset.task3BarId;
    }
  }
  function minimize(w){
    if(!w)return;
    if(w.classList.contains('win-fullscreen-2026')){
      w.classList.remove('win-fullscreen-2026');
    }
    removeTaskbarItem(w);
    addTaskbarItem(w);
    w.classList.add('task3-minimized-2026');
  }
  function fullscreen(w){
    if(!w)return;
    if(w.classList.contains('win-fullscreen-2026')){
      w.classList.remove('win-fullscreen-2026');
      w.style.width=w.dataset.task3PrevWidth||'';
      w.style.height=w.dataset.task3PrevHeight||'';
      w.style.maxHeight=w.dataset.task3PrevMaxHeight||'82vh';
      w.style.left=w.dataset.task3PrevLeft||'50%';
      w.style.top=w.dataset.task3PrevTop||'90px';
      w.style.transform=w.dataset.task3PrevTransform||'translateX(-50%)';
    }else{
      var r=w.getBoundingClientRect();
      w.dataset.task3PrevWidth=r.width+'px';
      w.dataset.task3PrevHeight=r.height+'px';
      w.dataset.task3PrevMaxHeight=w.style.maxHeight||'82vh';
      w.dataset.task3PrevLeft=r.left+'px';
      w.dataset.task3PrevTop=r.top+'px';
      w.dataset.task3PrevTransform=w.style.transform||'none';
      w.classList.add('win-fullscreen-2026');
    }
  }
  function closeExisting(w){
    var close=w.querySelector('.win-head button[onclick*="closeWindow"]');
    if(!close)close=w.querySelector('.win-head button[title="Close"]');
    if(!close && w.id==='runningNewsEditorWin')close=w.querySelector('#closeRunningNewsEditor');
    if(close){ close.click(); return; }
    w.classList.remove('show');
    w.style.display='none';
    removeTaskbarItem(w);
  }
  function hideOldWindowControls(w){
    w.querySelectorAll('.win-head button').forEach(function(b){
      if(b.closest('.task3-window-controls'))return;
      var oc=(b.getAttribute('onclick')||'').toLowerCase();
      var tx=(b.textContent||'').trim();
      var title=(b.getAttribute('title')||'').toLowerCase();
      /* Hide only old window-management buttons; keep useful buttons like refresh/print/download. */
      if(oc.indexOf('closewindow')>=0 || title==='close' || tx==='✕' || tx==='—' || tx==='🧹' ||
         title==='minimize' || title==='clear' || title.indexOf('fullscreen')>=0){
        b.style.display='none';
      }
    });
  }
  function addControls(w){
    if(!w || !w.classList.contains('window'))return;
    var head=w.querySelector('.win-head');
    if(!head)return;
    hideOldWindowControls(w);
    var old=head.querySelector('.task3-window-controls');
    if(old)return;
    var box=document.createElement('span');
    box.className='task3-window-controls';
    var min=document.createElement('button');
    min.type='button';min.textContent='—';min.title='মিনিমাইজ';min.setAttribute('aria-label','মিনিমাইজ');
    min.onclick=function(e){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();minimize(w);};
    var fs=document.createElement('button');
    fs.type='button';fs.textContent='⛶';fs.title='ফুল স্ক্রিন / স্বাভাবিক';fs.setAttribute('aria-label','ফুল স্ক্রিন / স্বাভাবিক');
    fs.onclick=function(e){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();fullscreen(w);};
    var cl=document.createElement('button');
    cl.type='button';cl.textContent='✕';cl.title='বন্ধ করুন';cl.setAttribute('aria-label','বন্ধ করুন');
    cl.onclick=function(e){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();closeExisting(w);};
    box.appendChild(min);box.appendChild(fs);box.appendChild(cl);
    head.appendChild(box);
  }
  function scan(){
    document.querySelectorAll('.window').forEach(addControls);
  }
  window.task3MinimizeWindow=minimize;
  window.task3FullscreenWindow=fullscreen;
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',scan);else scan();
  setTimeout(scan,300);setTimeout(scan,1000);setInterval(scan,1500);
  new MutationObserver(function(ms){
    ms.forEach(function(m){
      m.addedNodes&&m.addedNodes.forEach(function(n){
        if(n.nodeType!==1)return;
        if(n.matches&&n.matches('.window'))addControls(n);
        if(n.querySelectorAll)n.querySelectorAll('.window').forEach(addControls);
      });
    });
  }).observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>

<script id="user-task4-logo-clock-news-js-2026-09-22">
(function(){
  function loginLogoSrc(){
    var x=document.getElementById('loginLogo');
    return x && x.src ? x.src : '';
  }

  function addHeaderLogo(){
    var header=document.querySelector('#app > header.header');
    if(!header || document.querySelector('.task4-header-logo'))return;
    var src=loginLogoSrc();
    if(!src)return;
    var wrap=document.createElement('div');
    wrap.className='task4-header-logo';
    var img=document.createElement('img');
    img.src=src;
    img.alt='Logo';
    wrap.appendChild(img);
    header.parentNode.insertBefore(wrap,header);
  }

  function addWindowLogo(w){
    if(!w || !w.classList.contains('window'))return;
    var head=w.querySelector('.win-head');
    if(!head || head.querySelector('.task4-window-title-wrap'))return;
    var src=loginLogoSrc();
    if(!src)return;

    /* Existing title is kept; only its position is wrapped with the same Login logo. */
    var title=null;
    Array.prototype.some.call(head.children,function(el){
      if(el.tagName==='B'){title=el;return true;}
      return false;
    });
    if(!title)return;

    var wrap=document.createElement('span');
    wrap.className='task4-window-title-wrap';
    var img=document.createElement('img');
    img.className='task4-window-logo';
    img.src=src;
    img.alt='Logo';
    wrap.appendChild(img);
    wrap.appendChild(title);
    head.insertBefore(wrap,head.firstChild);
  }

  function scan(){
    addHeaderLogo();
    document.querySelectorAll('.window').forEach(addWindowLogo);
  }

  if(document.readyState==='loading'){
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',scan);
  }else{
    scan();
  }
  setTimeout(scan,300);
  setTimeout(scan,1000);
  setTimeout(scan,2000);

  new MutationObserver(function(ms){
    var changed=false;
    ms.forEach(function(m){
      if(m.addedNodes && m.addedNodes.length)changed=true;
    });
    if(changed)scan();
  }).observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>

<script id="user-task5-header-logo-print-news-js-2026-09-22">
(function(){
  function src(){
    var x=document.getElementById('loginLogo');
    return x && x.src ? x.src : '';
  }

  /* Move the already-created logo INSIDE the Header, not above/outside it. */
  function putLogoInsideHeader(){
    var header=document.querySelector('#app > header.header');
    if(!header)return;
    var logo=document.querySelector('.task4-header-logo');
    if(!logo)return;
    if(logo.parentNode!==header){
      header.insertBefore(logo,header.firstChild);
    }else if(header.firstElementChild!==logo){
      header.insertBefore(logo,header.firstChild);
    }
    logo.classList.remove('task5-outside-header');
  }

  /* All normal form/profile windows get the Login logo at left. */
  function addLeftLogo(w){
    if(!w || !w.classList || !w.classList.contains('window'))return;
    var head=w.querySelector('.win-head');
    if(!head || head.querySelector('.task4-window-title-wrap'))return;
    var s=src(); if(!s)return;
    var title=null;
    Array.prototype.some.call(head.children,function(el){
      if(el.tagName==='B'){title=el;return true;}
      return false;
    });
    if(!title)return;
    var wrap=document.createElement('span');
    wrap.className='task4-window-title-wrap';
    var img=document.createElement('img');
    img.className='task4-window-logo';
    img.src=s; img.alt='Logo';
    wrap.appendChild(img); wrap.appendChild(title);
    head.insertBefore(wrap,head.firstChild);
  }

  /* If a printable profile/form is printed from the current page, add the same logo at LEFT. */
  function addPrintLogo(root){
    if(!root || !root.querySelectorAll)return;
    var s=src(); if(!s)return;
    var heads=root.querySelectorAll('.print-head,.print-header,.profile-print-header,.profile-header,.print-title');
    heads.forEach(function(h){
      if(h.querySelector('.task5-print-logo'))return;
      var img=document.createElement('img');
      img.className='task5-print-logo';
      img.src=s; img.alt='Logo';
      h.classList.add('task5-print-head');
      h.insertBefore(img,h.firstChild);
    });
  }

  function scan(){
    putLogoInsideHeader();
    document.querySelectorAll('.window').forEach(addLeftLogo);
    addPrintLogo(document);
  }

  window.addEventListener('beforeprint',function(){scan();});
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',scan);else scan();
  setTimeout(scan,300);setTimeout(scan,1000);setTimeout(scan,2000);
  setInterval(scan,1500);

  new MutationObserver(function(ms){
    var changed=false;
    ms.forEach(function(m){
      if(m.addedNodes&&m.addedNodes.length)changed=true;
    });
    if(changed)scan();
  }).observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>

<script id="user-task7-final-logo-print-js-2026-09-22">
(function(){
  function getLogo(){
    var x=document.getElementById('loginLogo');
    return x&&x.src?x.src:'';
  }
  function cleanHeaderLogo(){
    document.querySelectorAll('#app > header.header > .task4-header-logo').forEach(function(w){
      /* Keep the real image only; remove old label/extra elements. */
      Array.prototype.slice.call(w.children).forEach(function(ch){
        if(ch.tagName!=='IMG') ch.remove();
      });
      Array.prototype.slice.call(w.childNodes).forEach(function(n){
        if(n.nodeType===3 && (n.textContent||'').trim()) n.remove();
      });
      w.style.height='76px';
      w.style.minHeight='76px';
      w.style.maxHeight='76px';
      w.style.padding='0';
      w.style.margin='0';
      w.style.lineHeight='0';
      var img=w.querySelector('img');
      if(img){
        img.width=76; img.height=76;
        img.style.width='76px';
        img.style.height='76px';
        img.style.margin='0';
      }
    });
  }
  function ensurePrintLogo(){
    var src=getLogo();
    if(!src)return;
    var img=document.getElementById('task7PrintLogo');
    if(!img){
      img=document.createElement('img');
      img.id='task7PrintLogo';
      img.className='task7-print-logo';
      img.alt='';
      document.body.appendChild(img);
    }
    img.src=src;
  }
  function scan(){
    cleanHeaderLogo();
    ensurePrintLogo();
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',scan);
  else scan();
  setTimeout(scan,200);setTimeout(scan,800);setTimeout(scan,1600);
  setInterval(cleanHeaderLogo,1000);
  window.addEventListener('beforeprint',function(){
    scan();
    var p=document.getElementById('task7PrintLogo');
    if(p)p.style.display='block';
  });
})();
</script>

<script id="public-login-access-js-2026-09-22">
(function(){
  /* Login-page All Search is public: no login/token gate. */
  window.doSearch=async function(where){
    var isLogin=where==='login';
    var sigEl=document.getElementById(isLogin?'loginSignal':'appSignal');
    var qEl=document.getElementById(isLogin?'loginSearch':'appSearch');
    var sig=sigEl?sigEl.value:'';
    var q=qEl?(qEl.value||'').trim():'';
    if(!q)return toast('ID / মোবাইল / ইমেইল / নাম লিখুন।');
    if(isLogin && !sig)return toast('সংকেত নির্বাচন করুন। তারপর নিজ নিজ ID / মোবাইল / ইমেইল / নাম লিখুন।');
    try{
      /* Empty token is intentional for the public login-page search. */
      var r=await call('searchAll',isLogin?'':token,sig,q);
      if(!r||!r.ok)return toast((r&&r.message)||'সার্চের ফল পাওয়া যায়নি।');
      var body=document.getElementById('searchBody');
      if(body)body.innerHTML='<h3>'+(r.signal||sig)+' / '+(r.query||q)+' — '+(r.count||0)+' ফলাফল</h3>'+table(r.matches||[]);
      openWindow('searchWin');
    }catch(e){toast('সার্চ সার্ভার ত্রুটি: '+(e&&e.message?e.message:e));}
  };
  /* Keep the six login-page features directly usable without first logging in. */
  window.openPublicFeature=function(key){
    try{
      if(typeof openModule==='function'){openModule(key);return;}
      toast('ফিচার প্রস্তুত হয়নি।');
    }catch(e){toast('ফিচার খোলার সময় ত্রুটি হয়েছে।');}
  };
  function bind(){
    var q=document.querySelectorAll('#loginPage .quick button');
    var keys=['payment','result','exam','admin','madrasa','quick'];
    q.forEach(function(b,i){
      if(i<5){b.setAttribute('type','button');b.onclick=function(){openPublicFeature(keys[i]);};}
      else{b.setAttribute('type','button');b.onclick=function(){openWindow('quickWin');};}
    });
    var inp=document.getElementById('loginSearch');
    if(inp){inp.placeholder='সংকেত নির্বাচন করে ID / মোবাইল / ইমেইল / নাম লিখুন';}
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',bind);else bind();
  setTimeout(bind,300);setTimeout(bind,1000);
})();
</script>


<!-- ===== MEDIA PLAYBACK STABILITY FIX 2026-09-22 ===== -->
<style id="media-playback-stability-fix-2026-09-22">
#mediaStageContent .media-stable-frame{
  width:100%!important;height:100%!important;border:0!important;
  background:#111!important;display:block!important;
}
#mediaStageContent .media-stable-video{
  width:100%!important;height:100%!important;object-fit:contain!important;  background:#111!important;display:block!important;
}
</style>
<script id="media-playback-stability-js-2026-09-22">
(function(){
  function driveId(u){
    var s=String(u||''),m=s.match(/[?&]id=([^&]+)/i);
    if(m)return decodeURIComponent(m[1]);
    m=s.match(/\/d\/([^/]+)/i);
    return m?m[1]:'';
  }
  function mediaUrl(x){
    var u=String((x&&x.DirectURL)||'');
    if(!u)u=String((x&&x.DataURL)||'');
    return u;
  }
  function renderStable(){
    var box=document.getElementById('mediaStageContent');
    if(!box)return;
    if(!window.media||!media.length){
      box.innerHTML='<span class="media-empty">মিডিয়া লোড করে দেখুন</span>';
      return;
    }
    mediaIndex=((Number(mediaIndex)||0)%media.length+media.length)%media.length;
    var x=media[mediaIndex]||{},u=mediaUrl(x),t=String(x.Type||'').toLowerCase(),id=driveId(u);
    box.innerHTML='';
    if(!u){
      box.innerHTML='<span class="media-empty">মিডিয়ার লিংক পাওয়া যায়নি।</span>';
      return;
    }
    if(t==='image'){
      var im=document.createElement('img');
      im.src=u;im.alt='';
      im.style.cssText='width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:contain;background:#111;display:block';
      im.onerror=function(){box.innerHTML='<span class="media-empty">ফটো চালানো যাচ্ছে না।</span>';};
      box.appendChild(im);
    }else if(id){
      var fr=document.createElement('iframe');
      fr.className='media-stable-frame';
      fr.src='https://drive.google.com/file/'+encodeURIComponent(id)+'/preview';
      fr.allow='autoplay; fullscreen; encrypted-media; picture-in-picture';
      fr.setAttribute('allowfullscreen','');
      fr.setAttribute('referrerpolicy','no-referrer-when-downgrade');
      box.appendChild(fr);
    }else if(t==='video'){
      var v=document.createElement('video');
      v.className='media-stable-video';v.controls=true;v.playsInline=true;v.preload='metadata';v.src=u;
      v.onerror=function(){toast('ভিডিও চালানো যাচ্ছে না। URL বা ফাইল access পরীক্ষা করুন।');};
      box.appendChild(v);
    }else if(t==='audio'){
      var a=document.createElement('audio');
      a.controls=true;a.preload='metadata';a.src=u;a.style.cssText='width:90%;max-height:80px';
      a.onerror=function(){toast('অডিও চালানো যাচ্ছে না। URL বা ফাইল access পরীক্ষা করুন।');};
      box.appendChild(a);
    }else{
      box.innerHTML='<span class="media-empty">মিডিয়ার ধরন পাওয়া যায়নি।</span>';
    }
    if(typeof renderMediaList==='function')renderMediaList();
  }
  window.renderMedia=renderStable;
  window.nextMedia=function(){
    if(window.media&&media.length){mediaIndex=(Number(mediaIndex)+1)%media.length;renderStable();}
  };
  window.prevMedia=function(){
    if(window.media&&media.length){mediaIndex=(Number(mediaIndex)-1+media.length)%media.length;renderStable();}
  };
  window.toggleMediaPlay=function(){
    var el=document.querySelector('#mediaStageContent video,#mediaStageContent audio');
    if(el){
      if(el.paused){var p=el.play();if(p&&p.catch)p.catch(function(){toast('মিডিয়া চালু করা যাচ্ছে না।');});}
      else el.pause();
      return;
    }
    var fr=document.querySelector('#mediaStageContent .media-stable-frame');
    if(fr)fr.focus();
  };
  function start(){if(window.media&&media.length)renderStable();}  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',start);else start();
  setTimeout(start,500);setTimeout(start,1500);setTimeout(start,3000);})();
</script>

<!-- ===== FINAL MEDIA PLAYER OVERRIDE — DRIVE PREVIEW PRIMARY 2026-09-22 ===== -->
<style id="final-media-player-override-2026-09-22">
#mediaStageContent .media-drive-player{width:100%!important;height:100%!important;border:0!important;background:#111!important;display:block!important}
</style>
<script id="final-media-player-override-js-2026-09-22">
(function(){
  function idOf(x){
    var s=String((x&&x.FileID)|| (x&&x.DriveFileId)|| (x&&x.DataURL)||'');
    var m=s.match(/[?&]id=([^&]+)/i); if(m)return decodeURIComponent(m[1]);
    m=s.match(/\\/d\\/([^/]+)/i); return m?m[1]:'';
  }
  function previewOf(x){
    var id=idOf(x); if(!id)return '';
    var rk=String((x&&x.ResourceKey)||'').trim();
    return 'https://drive.google.com/file/'+encodeURIComponent(id)+'/preview'+(rk?'?resourcekey='+encodeURIComponent(rk):'');
  }
  function directOf(x){
    var u=String((x&&x.DirectURL)||'').trim();
    if(u)return u;
    var id=idOf(x),rk=String((x&&x.ResourceKey)||'').trim();
    return id?'https://drive.google.com/uc?export=download&id='+encodeURIComponent(id)+(rk?'&resourcekey='+encodeURIComponent(rk):''):'';
  }
  function renderFixed(){
    var box=document.getElementById('mediaStageContent');
    if(!box)return;
    if(!window.media||!media.length){box.innerHTML='<span class="media-empty">মিডিয়া লোড করে দেখুন</span>';return;}
    mediaIndex=((Number(mediaIndex)||0)%media.length+media.length)%media.length;
    var x=media[mediaIndex]||{},t=String(x.Type||'').toLowerCase(),p=previewOf(x),u=directOf(x);
    box.innerHTML='';
    if(t==='image'){
      var im=document.createElement('img'); im.src=u; im.alt=x.Name||'';
      im.style.cssText='width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:contain;background:#111;display:block';
      im.onerror=function(){if(p)showFrame(box,p);else box.innerHTML='<span class="media-empty">ফটো চালানো যাচ্ছে না।</span>';};
      box.appendChild(im);
    }else if(t==='video' || t==='audio'){
      /* Google Drive preview is primary. The previous direct-download <video> failed because Drive download responses are not a reliable browser streaming endpoint. */
      if(p) showFrame(box,p);
      else box.innerHTML='<span class="media-empty">ভিডিও/অডিওর Preview লিংক পাওয়া যায়নি।</span>';
    }else{
      box.innerHTML='<span class="media-empty">মিডিয়ার ধরন সঠিক নয়।</span>';
    }
    if(typeof renderMediaList==='function')renderMediaList();
  }
  function showFrame(box,url){
    box.innerHTML='';
    var fr=document.createElement('iframe');
    fr.className='media-drive-player';
    fr.src=url;
    fr.allow='autoplay; fullscreen; encrypted-media; picture-in-picture';
    fr.setAttribute('allowfullscreen','');
    fr.setAttribute('referrerpolicy','no-referrer-when-downgrade');
    fr.setAttribute('title','Media Player');
    box.appendChild(fr);
  }
  window.renderMedia=renderFixed;
  window.nextMedia=function(){if(window.media&&media.length){mediaIndex=(Number(mediaIndex)+1)%media.length;renderFixed();}};
  window.prevMedia=function(){if(window.media&&media.length){mediaIndex=(Number(mediaIndex)-1+media.length)%media.length;renderFixed();}};
  window.toggleMediaPlay=function(){
    var fr=document.querySelector('#mediaStageContent .media-drive-player');
    if(fr){fr.focus();return;}
    var el=document.querySelector('#mediaStageContent video,#mediaStageContent audio');
    if(el){if(el.paused)el.play().catch(function(){toast('মিডিয়া চালানো যাচ্ছে না।')});else el.pause();}
  };
  function loadFixed(){
    if(typeof call==='function'&&window.token){
      call('listMedia',token).then(function(r){
        if(Array.isArray(r))window.media=r;
        else if(r&&Array.isArray(r.media))window.media=r.media;
        if(window.media&&media.length)renderFixed();
      }).catch(function(e){toast('মিডিয়া লোড হয়নি। আবার চেষ্টা করুন।');});
    }else if(window.media&&media.length)renderFixed();
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',loadFixed);else loadFixed();
  setTimeout(loadFixed,800);setTimeout(loadFixed,1800);
})();
</script>


<!-- ===== MEDIA ID EXTRACTION + PREVIEW FIX — 2026-09-22 ===== -->
<script id="media-id-extraction-preview-fix-2026-09-22">
(function(){
  function findId(v, seen){
    if(v==null) return '';
    if(typeof v==='string'){
      var s=v.trim();
      if(!s) return '';
      var m=s.match(/drive\.google\.com\/file\/d\/([^/?#]+)/i);
      if(m) return decodeURIComponent(m[1]);
      m=s.match(/[?&]id=([^&#]+)/i);
      if(m) return decodeURIComponent(m[1]);
      m=s.match(/\/d\/([^/?#]+)/i);
      if(m) return decodeURIComponent(m[1]);
      m=s.match(/(?:^|[?&])(fileId|fileID|driveId|driveFileId)=([^&#]+)/i);
      if(m) return decodeURIComponent(m[2]);
      return '';
    }
    if(typeof v!=='object') return '';
    seen=seen||[];
    if(seen.indexOf(v)>=0) return '';
    seen.push(v);
    var keys=['FileID','FileId','fileId','fileID','DriveFileId','DriveFileID','driveFileId','DriveId','driveId','ID','Id','id','DataURL','DirectURL','FileURL','FileUrl','DriveURL','DriveUrl','URL','Url','Link','link','PreviewURL','PreviewUrl'];
    for(var i=0;i<keys.length;i++){
      var id=findId(v[keys[i]],seen);
      if(id) return id;
    }
    for(var k in v){
      try{
        var id2=findId(v[k],seen);
        if(id2) return id2;
      }catch(e){}
    }
    return '';
  }

  function resourceKey(x){
    return String(
      (x&&x.ResourceKey) ||
      (x&&x.resourceKey) ||
      (x&&x.resourcekey) ||
      ''
    ).trim();
  }

  function sourceUrl(x){
    var keys=['DirectURL','DirectUrl','directURL','DataURL','DataUrl','URL','Url','FileURL','FileUrl','DriveURL','DriveUrl','Link','link','PreviewURL','PreviewUrl'];
    for(var i=0;i<keys.length;i++){
      var u=String((x&&x[keys[i]])||'').trim();
      if(u) return u;
    }
    return '';
  }

  function previewOf(x){
    var src=sourceUrl(x);
    if(/drive\.google\.com\/file\/[^/]+\/preview/i.test(src)) return src;
    var id=findId(x);
    if(!id) return '';
    var rk=resourceKey(x);
    return 'https://drive.google.com/file/d/'+encodeURIComponent(id)+'/preview'+
      (rk?'?resourcekey='+encodeURIComponent(rk):'');
  }

  function directOf(x){
    var src=sourceUrl(x);
    if(src && !/drive\.google\.com\/(?:file\/[^/]+\/preview|uc\?|open\?)/i.test(src)) return src;
    var id=findId(x);
    if(!id) return src;
    var rk=resourceKey(x);
    return 'https://drive.google.com/uc?export=download&id='+encodeURIComponent(id)+
      (rk?'&resourcekey='+encodeURIComponent(rk):'');
  }

  function showFrame(box,url){
    box.innerHTML='';
    var fr=document.createElement('iframe');
    fr.className='media-drive-player';
    fr.src=url;
    fr.allow='autoplay; fullscreen; encrypted-media; picture-in-picture';
    fr.setAttribute('allowfullscreen','');
    fr.setAttribute('referrerpolicy','no-referrer-when-downgrade');
    fr.setAttribute('title','Media Player');
    box.appendChild(fr);
  }

  function renderMediaFixed(){
    var box=document.getElementById('mediaStageContent');
    if(!box) return;

    if(!window.media || !media.length){
      box.innerHTML='<span class="media-empty">মিডিয়া লোড করে দেখুন</span>';
      return;
    }

    mediaIndex=((Number(mediaIndex)||0)%media.length+media.length)%media.length;

    var x=media[mediaIndex]||{};
    var t=String(x.Type||x.type||'').toLowerCase();
    var p=previewOf(x);
    var u=directOf(x);

    box.innerHTML='';

    if(t==='image'){
      var im=document.createElement('img');
      im.src=u;
      im.alt=String(x.Name||x.name||'');
      im.style.cssText='width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:contain;background:#111;display:block';
      im.onerror=function(){
        if(p) showFrame(box,p);
        else box.innerHTML='<span class="media-empty">ফটো চালানো যাচ্ছে না।</span>';
      };
      box.appendChild(im);

    }else if(t==='video' || t==='audio'){
      if(p){
        showFrame(box,p);
      }else if(u){
        var el=document.createElement(t);
        el.controls=true;
        el.preload='metadata';
        if(t==='video'){
          el.playsInline=true;
          el.style.cssText='width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:contain;background:#111;display:block';
        }else{
          el.style.cssText='width:90%;max-height:80px';
        }
        el.src=u;
        el.onerror=function(){
          box.innerHTML='<span class="media-empty">মিডিয়া চালানো যাচ্ছে না।</span>';
        };
        box.appendChild(el);
      }else{
        box.innerHTML='<span class="media-empty">মিডিয়ার লিংক পাওয়া যায়নি।</span>';
      }
    }else{
      box.innerHTML='<span class="media-empty">মিডিয়ার ধরন সঠিক নয়।</span>';
    }

    if(typeof renderMediaList==='function') renderMediaList();
  }

  window.renderMedia=renderMediaFixed;

  window.nextMedia=function(){
    if(window.media&&media.length){
      mediaIndex=(Number(mediaIndex)+1)%media.length;
      renderMediaFixed();
    }
  };

  window.prevMedia=function(){
    if(window.media&&media.length){
      mediaIndex=(Number(mediaIndex)-1+media.length)%media.length;
      renderMediaFixed();
    }
  };

  window.toggleMediaPlay=function(){
    var el=document.querySelector('#mediaStageContent video,#mediaStageContent audio');
    if(el){
      if(el.paused){
        var p=el.play();
        if(p&&p.catch)p.catch(function(){
          if(typeof toast==='function')toast('মিডিয়া চালানো যাচ্ছে না।');
        });
      }else{
        el.pause();
      }
      return;
    }
    var fr=document.querySelector('#mediaStageContent .media-drive-player');
    if(fr) fr.focus();
  };

  function loadMediaFixed(){
    if(typeof call==='function' && window.token){
      call('listMedia',token).then(function(r){
        if(Array.isArray(r)) window.media=r;
        else if(r&&Array.isArray(r.media)) window.media=r.media;
        if(window.media&&media.length) renderMediaFixed();
      }).catch(function(){});
    }else if(window.media&&media.length){
      renderMediaFixed();
    }
  }

  if(document.readyState==='loading'){
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',loadMediaFixed);
  }else{
    loadMediaFixed();
  }

  setTimeout(loadMediaFixed,700);
  setTimeout(loadMediaFixed,1800);
})();
</script>

<script id="final-media-window-arrows-fix-js-2026-09-22">
(function(){
  function ensureArrows(){
    var box=document.getElementById('mediaStageContent');
    if(!box)return;
    box.querySelectorAll('.final-media-arrow').forEach(function(b){b.remove()});
    if(!window.media||!media.length)return;
    box.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',
      '<button type="button" class="final-media-arrow left" onclick="prevMedia()" aria-label="আগের মিডিয়া">❮</button>'+
      '<button type="button" class="final-media-arrow right" onclick="nextMedia()" aria-label="পরের মিডিয়া">❯</button>');
  }

  function ensureTaskbar(){
    var t=document.getElementById('finalMediaTaskbar');
    if(t)return t;
    t=document.createElement('div');
    t.id='finalMediaTaskbar';
    t.className='final-media-taskbar';
    t.innerHTML='<button type="button" onclick="restoreFinalMediaDelete()">🗑️ মিডিয়া ডিলেট উইন্ডো</button>';
    document.body.appendChild(t);
    return t;
  }

  function makeWindowDrag(w){
    if(!w||w.dataset.finalMediaDrag==='1')return;
    var h=w.querySelector('.win-head');if(!h)return;
    w.dataset.finalMediaDrag='1';
    h.onmousedown=function(e){
      if(e.target.closest('button,input,select,textarea,a'))return;
      e.preventDefault();
      var r=w.getBoundingClientRect(),sx=e.clientX,sy=e.clientY,ox=r.left,oy=r.top;
      w.style.setProperty('position','fixed','important');
      w.style.setProperty('transform','none','important');
      w.style.setProperty('left',ox+'px','important');
      w.style.setProperty('top',oy+'px','important');
      w.style.setProperty('right','auto','important');
      w.style.setProperty('bottom','auto','important');
      function move(ev){
        var x=ox+ev.clientX-sx,y=oy+ev.clientY-sy;
        var maxX=Math.max(0,window.innerWidth-w.offsetWidth);
        x=Math.max(0,Math.min(maxX,x));
        w.style.setProperty('left',x+'px','important');
        w.style.setProperty('top',Math.max(0,y)+'px','important');
      }
      function up(){document.removeEventListener('mousemove',move,true);document.removeEventListener('mouseup',up,true)}
      document.addEventListener('mousemove',move,true);document.addEventListener('mouseup',up,true);
    };
  }

  function ensureDeleteWindow(){
    var w=document.getElementById('finalMediaDeleteWin');
    if(w){makeWindowDrag(w);return w;}
    w=document.createElement('div');
    w.id='finalMediaDeleteWin';
    w.className='window';
    w.innerHTML=
      '<div class="win-head"><b>🗑️ মিডিয়া ডিলেট তালিকা</b>'+
      '<span class="final-win-tools">'+
      '<button type="button" title="মিনিমাইজ" onclick="minimizeFinalMediaDelete()">—</button>'+
      '<button type="button" title="Taskbar-এর নিচে" onclick="moveFinalMediaDeleteBelow()">▾</button>'+
      '<button type="button" title="ক্লোজ" onclick="closeFinalMediaDelete()">✕</button>'+
      '</span></div>'+
      '<div class="win-body" id="finalMediaDeleteBody"><p>⏳ তালিকা লোড হচ্ছে...</p></div>';
    document.body.appendChild(w);
    makeWindowDrag(w);
    return w;
  }

  async function fillDeleteWindow(){
    var w=ensureDeleteWindow(),body=document.getElementById('finalMediaDeleteBody');
    if(!body)return;
    body.innerHTML='<p>⏳ মিডিয়া তালিকা লোড হচ্ছে...</p>';
    try{
      var fresh=await call('listMedia',token);
      if(Array.isArray(fresh))window.media=fresh;
      else if(fresh&&Array.isArray(fresh.media))window.media=fresh.media;
    }catch(e){}
    if(!window.media||!media.length){
      body.innerHTML='<p style="font-weight:900">ডিলেট করার কোনো মিডিয়া নেই।</p>';
      return;
    }
    body.innerHTML='<div style="font-weight:900;margin-bottom:8px">প্রতিটি মিডিয়া আলাদা করে ডিলেট করুন:</div>'+
      media.map(function(x,i){
        var name=typeof escapeHtml==='function'?escapeHtml(x.Name||('Media '+(i+1))):String(x.Name||('Media '+(i+1)));
        var typ=escapeHtml?escapeHtml(String(x.Type||'')):'';
        return '<div class="final-media-delete-row"><span><b>'+name+'</b> <small>('+typ+')</small></span>'+
          '<button type="button" class="secondary" onclick="deleteFinalMediaItem('+i+')">🗑️ ডিলেট</button></div>';
      }).join('');
  }

  window.openFinalMediaDelete=async function(){
    var w=ensureDeleteWindow();
    var t=ensureTaskbar();t.classList.remove('show');
    w.style.display='block';
    w.classList.add('show');
    w.style.setProperty('z-index','2147483646','important');
    w.style.removeProperty('left');w.style.removeProperty('top');
    w.style.setProperty('left','50%','important');
    w.style.setProperty('top','90px','important');
    w.style.setProperty('transform','translateX(-50%)','important');
    await fillDeleteWindow();
  };

  window.deleteCurrentMedia=window.openFinalMediaDelete;

  window.deleteFinalMediaItem=async function(i){
    if(!window.media||!media[i])return;
    var x=media[i];
    try{
      var r=await call('deleteMedia',token,x.Serial);
      if(r&&r.ok){
        media.splice(i,1);
        if(mediaIndex>=media.length)mediaIndex=Math.max(0,media.length-1);
        if(typeof renderMedia==='function')renderMedia();
        setTimeout(ensureArrows,80);
        await fillDeleteWindow();
        if(typeof toast==='function')toast('মিডিয়া মুছে ফেলা হয়েছে।');
      }else if(typeof toast==='function')toast((r&&r.message)||'ডিলেট ব্যর্থ হয়েছে।');
    }catch(e){if(typeof toast==='function')toast('ডিলেট সার্ভার ত্রুটি: '+(e&&e.message?e.message:e))}
  };

  window.minimizeFinalMediaDelete=function(){
    var w=document.getElementById('finalMediaDeleteWin');if(!w)return;
    w.style.display='none';w.classList.remove('show');
    ensureTaskbar().classList.add('show');
  };
  window.closeFinalMediaDelete=function(){
    var w=document.getElementById('finalMediaDeleteWin');if(w){w.style.display='none';w.classList.remove('show')}
    var t=ensureTaskbar();t.classList.remove('show');
  };
  window.moveFinalMediaDeleteBelow=function(){
    var w=document.getElementById('finalMediaDeleteWin');if(!w)return;
    w.classList.add('show');w.style.display='block';
    w.style.setProperty('transform','none','important');
    w.style.setProperty('left','20px','important');
    w.style.setProperty('top',(window.innerHeight+25)+'px','important');
    ensureTaskbar().classList.add('show');
  };
  window.restoreFinalMediaDelete=function(){
    var w=ensureDeleteWindow(),t=ensureTaskbar();t.classList.remove('show');
    w.classList.add('show');w.style.display='block';
    w.style.setProperty('left','50%','important');
    w.style.setProperty('top','90px','important');
    w.style.setProperty('transform','translateX(-50%)','important');
    makeWindowDrag(w);
  };

  function bindDeleteButton(){
    document.querySelectorAll('#mediaStage .dashboard-slider-controls button').forEach(function(b){
      var txt=(b.textContent||'').trim();
      if(txt.indexOf('ডিলেট')>=0 || txt.indexOf('Delete')>=0){
        b.onclick=function(e){e.preventDefault();openFinalMediaDelete();};
      }
    });
  }

  function refresh(){
    ensureArrows();
    bindDeleteButton();
    makeWindowDrag(document.getElementById('finalMediaDeleteWin'));
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',refresh);else refresh();
  setTimeout(refresh,300);setTimeout(refresh,800);setTimeout(refresh,1600);
  var oldRender=window.renderMedia;
  var tries=0;
  var timer=setInterval(function(){
    if(window.renderMedia!==oldRender){
      oldRender=window.renderMedia;
      tries++;
    }
    ensureArrows();bindDeleteButton();
    if(tries>30)clearInterval(timer);
  },250);
})();
</script>

<script id="media-controls-position-fix-js-2026-09-22">
(function(){
  function fix(){
    var stage=document.getElementById('mediaStage');
    if(!stage)return;
    stage.querySelectorAll('.media-player-controls').forEach(function(x){x.remove()});
    var controls=stage.querySelector('.dashboard-slider-controls');
    if(controls){
      controls.style.setProperty('position','relative','important');
      controls.style.setProperty('z-index','50','important');
      controls.style.setProperty('margin-top','14px','important');
      controls.style.setProperty('padding','10px 0','important');
      controls.style.setProperty('background','transparent','important');
      controls.style.setProperty('border','0','important');
      controls.style.setProperty('box-shadow','none','important');
    }
