<script id="media-autoplay-income-expense-fix-js-2026-09-22">
(function(){
  /* ডিলেট তালিকা না খুলেও প্রথম মিডিয়া/ভিডিও স্বাভাবিকভাবে দেখাবে */
  function autoRenderMedia(){
    try{
      if(typeof window.renderMedia==='function') window.renderMedia();
      else if(typeof renderMedia==='function') renderMedia();
    }catch(e){}
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',function(){setTimeout(autoRenderMedia,900)});
  else setTimeout(autoRenderMedia,900);
  [1800,3000].forEach(function(ms){setTimeout(autoRenderMedia,ms)});

  /* ডিলেট উইন্ডো খোলা/বন্ধ হওয়া মিডিয়া প্লেয়ারের উপর আর নির্ভর করবে না */
  document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
    var b=e.target.closest && e.target.closest('#mediaDeleteBtn,[data-action="delete-media"],.media-delete-btn');
    if(b){ setTimeout(autoRenderMedia,500); }
  },true);
})();
</script>

<script>
(function(){
 var busy=false;
 function norm(){if(!Array.isArray(window.media))window.media=[];if(media.length)mediaIndex=((Number(mediaIndex)||0)%media.length+media.length)%media.length}
 function attach(){var b=document.getElementById('mediaStageContent');if(!b)return;var e=b.querySelector('video,audio');if(e){e.onended=function(){if(media&&media.length>1){mediaIndex=(Number(mediaIndex)+1)%media.length;renderMedia();setTimeout(attach,150)}};var p=e.play();if(p&&p.catch)p.catch(function(){})}}
 async function load(){if(busy)return;busy=true;try{var r=await call('listMedia',token);if(Array.isArray(r))window.media=r;else if(r&&Array.isArray(r.rows))window.media=r.rows;norm();if(media.length&&typeof renderMedia==='function'){renderMedia();setTimeout(attach,100)}}catch(e){}busy=false}

<!-- ===== FINAL REQUEST FIX: PLAYER UP + 14 BOXES DOWN + TRUE MEDIA AUTO PLAY 2026-09-22 ===== -->
<style id="final-player-up-14-box-down-2026-09-22">
/* শুধু মূল ভিডিও প্লেয়ারটি একটু উপরে */
#mediaStage{
  margin-top:4px!important;
}
#mediaStageContent{
  transform:translateY(-18px)!important;
  margin-bottom:-8px!important;
}
/* প্লেয়ারের ঠিক নিচের ১৪টি ২-কলামের বক্স ১ ইঞ্চি নিচে */
#summary.dashboard-summary-inside{
  transform:translateY(96px)!important;
  margin-bottom:96px!important;
}
#summary.dashboard-summary-inside .stat{
  transform:none!important;
}</style>
<script id="final-true-media-autoplay-2026-09-22">
(function(){
  var busy=false;
  function getMedia(){return Array.isArray(window.media)?window.media:[]}
  function next(){
    var m=getMedia();
    if(m.length>1){mediaIndex=(Number(mediaIndex||0)+1)%m.length;window.renderMedia&&window.renderMedia();setTimeout(bind,120)}
  }
  function bind(){
    var box=document.getElementById('mediaStageContent');if(!box)return;
    var el=box.querySelector('video,audio');if(!el)return;
    el.onended=null;
    el.onended=next;
    el.autoplay=true;
    el.playsInline=true;
    el.preload='auto';
    /* ব্রাউজারের autoplay policy মেনে প্রথমে muted autoplay চেষ্টা */
    var p=el.play();
    if(p&&p.catch)p.catch(function(){
      try{el.muted=true;var q=el.play();if(q&&q.catch)q.catch(function(){})}catch(e){}
    });
  }
  async function load(){
    if(busy||typeof call!=='function'||!token)return;
    busy=true;
    try{
      var r=await call('listMedia',token);
      var m=Array.isArray(r)?r:(r&&Array.isArray(r.media)?r.media:(r&&Array.isArray(r.rows)?r.rows:null));
      if(m){window.media=m;if(media.length){mediaIndex=((Number(mediaIndex)||0)%media.length+media.length)%media.length;window.renderMedia&&window.renderMedia();setTimeout(bind,150)}}
    }catch(e){}
    busy=false;
  }
  function boot(){load();setTimeout(load,900);setTimeout(load,2200);setTimeout(bind,3000)}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot);else boot();
  document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
    var b=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('#mediaPlayBtn,.slider-action');
    if(b)setTimeout(bind,120);
  },true);
})();
</script>

<script id="dashboard-period-dynamic-large-2026-09-22">
(function(){
  var periods=['দৈনিক','সাপ্তাহিক','মাসিক','বাৎসরিক'];
  var periodIndex=0, timer=null, loading=false, refreshTimer=null;

  function esc(v){return String(v==null?'':v).replace(/[&<>"']/g,function(s){return ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'})[s]})}
  function money(v){
    var n=Number(v)||0;
    try{return new Intl.NumberFormat('bn-BD',{maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(n)}catch(e){return String(Math.round(n))}
  }
  function rangeLabel(r){
    if(!r)return '';
    return esc(r.rangeStart||'')+(r.rangeEnd&&r.rangeEnd!==r.rangeStart?' → '+esc(r.rangeEnd):'');
  }
  function renderLarge(r,period){
    var box=document.getElementById('dashboardCharts');
    if(!box)return;
    var income=Number(r&&r.income)||0, expense=Number(r&&r.expense)||0, cash=Number(r&&r.cash)||0, due=Number(r&&r.due)||0;
    var p=Number(r&&r.attendance&&r.attendance.present)||0, a=Number(r&&r.attendance&&r.attendance.absent)||0;
    var total=p+a, pct=total?Math.round(p/total*100):0, vals=[income,expense,cash,due], max=Math.max.apply(null,vals.concat([1]));
    var labels=['আয়','ব্যয়','ক্যাশ','বকেয়া'];
    var bars=vals.map(function(v,i){
      var h=Math.max(8,Math.round((v/max)*175));
      return '<div class="dashboard-period-bar-wrap">'+
        '<div class="dashboard-period-bar-value">'+money(v)+'</div>'+
        '<div class="dashboard-period-bar" style="height:'+h+'px;background:'+(i===0?'#20a464':i===1?'#e33b4a':i===2?'#248bd0':'#f0a51a')+'"></div>'+
        '<div class="dashboard-period-label">'+labels[i]+'</div></div>';
    }).join('');
    var tabs=periods.map(function(v,i){return '<button class="period-tab '+(v===period?'active':'')+'" data-period="'+esc(v)+'" onclick="selectDashboardPeriod(this,\''+v+'\')">'+v+'</button>'}).join('');
    box.innerHTML=
      '<div class="chart-card">'+
        '<div class="chart-title">📊 আয় + ব্যয় + ক্যাশ + বকেয়া — '+esc(period)+' সামারি</div>'+
        '<div class="period-tabs">'+tabs+'</div>'+
        '<div class="dashboard-period-range">সময়কাল: '+rangeLabel(r)+'</div>'+
        '<div class="dashboard-period-finance">'+bars+'</div>'+
        '<div class="dashboard-period-legend">'+
          '<span><i class="dashboard-period-dot" style="background:#20a464"></i>আয়</span>'+
          '<span><i class="dashboard-period-dot" style="background:#e33b4a"></i>ব্যয়</span>'+
          '<span><i class="dashboard-period-dot" style="background:#248bd0"></i>ক্যাশ</span>'+
          '<span><i class="dashboard-period-dot" style="background:#f0a51a"></i>বকেয়া</span>'+
        '</div>'+
        '<div class="dashboard-dynamic-badge">↻ লাইভ ডায়নামিক সামারি</div>'+
      '</div>'+
      '<div class="chart-card">'+
        '<div class="chart-title">👥 '+esc(period)+' উপস্থিত / অনুপস্থিত</div>'+
        '<div class="dashboard-period-range">সময়কাল: '+rangeLabel(r)+'</div>'+
        '<div class="dashboard-attendance-wrap">'+
          '<div class="dashboard-attendance-ring-large" style="--present-pct:'+pct+'%"><span>'+pct+'%</span></div>'+
          '<div class="dashboard-attendance-counts-large">'+
            '<div class="dashboard-attendance-count-large"><b>'+money(p)+'</b>উপস্থিত</div>'+
            '<div class="dashboard-attendance-count-large"><b>'+money(a)+'</b>অনুপস্থিত</div>'+
          '</div>'+
        '</div>'+
        '<div class="dashboard-dynamic-badge">↻ লাইভ ডায়নামিক হাজিরা সামারি</div>'+
      '</div>';
  }

  async function load(period,keepIndex){
    if(loading||!token||typeof call!=='function')return;
    loading=true;
    try{
      var r=await call('getDashboardPeriodStats',token,period);
      if(r&&r.ok)renderLarge(r,period);
    }catch(e){
      console.warn('Dashboard period summary:',e);
    }
    loading=false;
    if(!keepIndex){
      periodIndex=periods.indexOf(period);
      if(periodIndex<0)periodIndex=0;
    }
  }

  window.selectDashboardPeriod=function(btn,period){
    periodIndex=periods.indexOf(period);
    if(periodIndex<0)periodIndex=0;
    document.querySelectorAll('#dashboardCharts .period-tab').forEach(function(b){b.classList.remove('active')});
    if(btn)btn.classList.add('active');
    load(period,false);
  };

  function cycle(){
    periodIndex=(periodIndex+1)%periods.length;
    load(periods[periodIndex],true);
  }
  function start(){
    if(timer)clearInterval(timer);
    timer=setInterval(cycle,8000);
    if(refreshTimer)clearInterval(refreshTimer);
    refreshTimer=setInterval(function(){load(periods[periodIndex],true)},45000);
    load(periods[periodIndex],true);
  }
  function waitForToken(){
    if(token){start();return}
    setTimeout(waitForToken,1200);
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',waitForToken);
  else waitForToken();
})();
</script>

<style id="final-media-controls-clean-2026-09-23">
/* Play এবং দুই পাশের Arrow বাদ — শুধু Load ও Delete থাকবে */

#mediaStage #mediaPlayBtn{
  display:none!important;
}
#mediaStage .slider-center-controls{
  display:flex!important;
  justify-content:center!important;
  align-items:center!important;
  gap:10px!important;
  width:100%!important;
}
#mediaStage .slider-center-controls .slider-action{
  display:inline-flex!important;
}

<script id="final-media-sequential-load-fix-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  var seqTimer=null, loadBusy=false;

  function list(){
    return Array.isArray(window.media)?window.media:[];
  }

  function normalise(){
    var m=list();
    if(!m.length){window.media=[];return;}
    var n=Number(window.mediaIndex);
    if(!isFinite(n))n=0;
    window.mediaIndex=((n%m.length)+m.length)%m.length;
  }

  function playCurrent(){
    var box=document.getElementById('mediaStageContent');
    if(!box)return;
    var el=box.querySelector('video,audio');
    if(el){
      el.autoplay=true;
      el.playsInline=true;
      el.preload='auto';
      var p=el.play();
      if(p&&p.catch)p.catch(function(){
        try{el.muted=true;var q=el.play();if(q&&q.catch)q.catch(function(){})}catch(e){}
      });
    }
  }

  function scheduleNext(){
    clearTimeout(seqTimer);
    var m=list();
    if(m.length<=1)return;
    var box=document.getElementById('mediaStageContent');
    if(!box)return;
    var el=box.querySelector('video,audio');
    if(el){
      el.onended=function(){
        window.mediaIndex=(Number(window.mediaIndex||0)+1)%list().length;
        if(typeof window.renderMedia==='function')window.renderMedia();
        setTimeout(function(){playCurrent();scheduleNext();},120);
      };
    }else{
      /* ছবি নির্দিষ্ট সময় পরে পরের মিডিয়ায় যাবে */
      seqTimer=setTimeout(function(){
        window.mediaIndex=(Number(window.mediaIndex||0)+1)%list().length;
        if(typeof window.renderMedia==='function')window.renderMedia();
        setTimeout(function(){scheduleNext();},120);
      },6000);
    }
  }

  function renderAndPlay(){
    normalise();
    if(!list().length)return;
    if(typeof window.renderMedia==='function')window.renderMedia();
    setTimeout(function(){playCurrent();scheduleNext();},100);
  }

  async function refresh(){
    if(loadBusy||typeof window.call!=='function'||!window.token)return;
    loadBusy=true;
    try{
      var r=await window.call('listMedia',window.token);
      var m=Array.isArray(r)?r:(r&&Array.isArray(r.media)?r.media:(r&&Array.isArray(r.rows)?r.rows:[]));
      window.media=m||[];
      normalise();
      if(window.media.length)renderAndPlay();
    }catch(e){}
    loadBusy=false;
  }

  /* Login/token হওয়ার পর Delete চাপা ছাড়াই মিডিয়া নিজে লোড হবে */
  function boot(){
    refresh();
    [700,1600,3000].forEach(function(ms){setTimeout(refresh,ms)});
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot);else boot();

  /* Load বাটনে চাপলে আগে মিডিয়া refresh হবে; Delete লাগবে না */
  document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
    var b=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('#mediaStage .slider-center-controls .slider-action');
    if(!b)return;
    var title=(b.getAttribute('title')||'').toLowerCase();
    var txt=(b.textContent||'').toLowerCase();
    if(title.indexOf('upload')>=0||txt.indexOf('load')>=0){
      setTimeout(refresh,350);
      setTimeout(refresh,1400);
    }
    if(title.indexOf('delete')>=0||txt.indexOf('delete')>=0){
      setTimeout(refresh,600);
    }  },true);
  /* renderMedia অন্য স্ক্রিপ্ট থেকে কল হলেও sequential autoplay সচল থাকবে */
  var oldRender=window.renderMedia;
  if(typeof oldRender==='function'){
    window.renderMedia=function(){
      oldRender.apply(this,arguments);
      setTimeout(function(){playCurrent();scheduleNext();},80);
    };
  }
})();
</script>


<style id="prevent-duplicate-media-names-2026-09-23">
/* একই নামে দ্বিতীয়বার মিডিয়া লোড/সেভ হবে না */
</style>
<script id="prevent-duplicate-media-names-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  function key(v){return String(v||'').trim().toLowerCase();}
  var oldUpload=window.uploadMedia;
  if(typeof oldUpload!=='function')return;
  window.uploadMedia=async function(){
    var input=document.getElementById('mediaFiles');
    if(!input||!input.files||!input.files.length)return oldUpload.apply(this,arguments);
    var existing=Array.isArray(window.media)?window.media:[];
    var names={};
    existing.forEach(function(x){var n=key(x&&x.Name);if(n)names[n]=true;});
    var files=Array.prototype.slice.call(input.files);
    var dup=files.filter(function(f){return names[key(f.name)];});
    if(dup.length){
      if(typeof window.toast==='function')window.toast('একই নামে ফাইল আগে থেকেই আছে: '+dup.map(function(f){return f.name}).join(', '));
      /* নতুন নামের ফাইলগুলোই শুধু আপলোড হবে */
      var dt=new DataTransfer();
      files.filter(function(f){return !names[key(f.name)];}).forEach(function(f){dt.items.add(f);});
      input.files=dt.files;
      if(!input.files.length)return;
    }
    return oldUpload.apply(this,arguments);
  };
})();
</script>

<script id="header-final-clock-news-calendar-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  function pad(n){return String(n).padStart(2,'0');}
  function updateHeaderClock(){
    var now=new Date();
    var clock=document.getElementById('appClock'), date=document.getElementById('appDate');
    if(clock)clock.textContent=pad(now.getHours())+':'+pad(now.getMinutes())+':'+pad(now.getSeconds());
    if(date){
      var days=['রবিবার','সোমবার','মঙ্গলবার','বুধবার','বৃহস্পতিবার','শুক্রবার','শনিবার'];
      date.textContent=pad(now.getDate())+'-'+pad(now.getMonth()+1)+'-'+now.getFullYear()+'  '+days[now.getDay()];
    }
  }
  function setupNews(){
    document.querySelectorAll('#app > header.header .header-news-row-final .header-news').forEach(function(el){
      var txt=(el.textContent||'').trim()||'জান্নাতুল বাক্বী মহিলা মাদ্রাসা ও এতিমখানার ডিজিটাল প্লাটফর্মে আপনাকে স্বাগতম';
      el.innerHTML='';
      var track=document.createElement('span'); track.className='header-news-track';
      for(var i=0;i<2;i++){
        var item=document.createElement('span');
        item.className='header-news-item';
        item.textContent=txt;
        track.appendChild(item);
      }
      el.appendChild(track);
    });
  }
  function setupCalendar(){
    var s=document.getElementById('headerCalendarSelect');
    if(!s||s.dataset.bound)return;
    s.dataset.bound='1';
    s.onchange=function(){
      var v=s.value;
      if(!v)return;
      var d=new Date(), out='';
      if(v==='en')out='English Calendar: '+d.toLocaleDateString('en-GB');
      if(v==='ar')out='التقويم العربي: '+d.toLocaleDateString('ar-SA-u-ca-islamic');
      if(v==='bn')out='বাংলা ক্যালেন্ডার: '+d.toLocaleDateString('bn-BD-u-ca-beng');
      if(typeof window.toast==='function')window.toast(out);
      s.value='';
    };
  }
  function init(){updateHeaderClock();setupNews();setupCalendar();}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',init);else init();
  setInterval(updateHeaderClock,1000);
  setTimeout(init,700);
})();
</script>

<script id="header-slide-delete-list-final-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  function getData(){
    if(typeof window.__getHeaderSlideList==='function'){
      try{return window.__getHeaderSlideList();}catch(e){}
    }
    var box=document.getElementById('headerBackgroundSlides');
    var imgs=box?box.querySelectorAll('.header-background-slide'):[];
    return Array.prototype.map.call(imgs,function(img,i){
      return {src:img.src,label:img.alt||('Header Slide '+(i+1)),index:i};
    });
  }
  function ensureWindow(){
    var w=document.getElementById('headerSlideDeleteWindow');
    if(w)return w;
    w=document.createElement('div');
    w.id='headerSlideDeleteWindow';
    w.innerHTML='<div class="hsdw-head"><span>Header ছবির তালিকা — যেটি চান ডিলেট করুন</span><button type="button" class="hsdw-close" onclick="closeHeaderSlideDeleteList()">✕</button></div><div class="hsdw-body" id="headerSlideDeleteList"></div>';
    document.body.appendChild(w);
    return w;
  }
  function renderList(){
    var w=ensureWindow(), list=document.getElementById('headerSlideDeleteList');
    var data=getData();
    list.innerHTML='';
    if(!data.length){
      list.innerHTML='<div style="text-align:center;font-weight:900;padding:25px">কোনো Header স্লাইড নেই</div>';
      return;
    }
    data.forEach(function(x){
      var row=document.createElement('div');
      row.className='hsdw-item';
      row.innerHTML='<img src="'+x.src.replace(/"/g,'&quot;')+'" alt=""><div class="hsdw-name">'+String(x.label).replace(/[<>&"]/g,function(s){return {'<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','&':'&amp;','"':'&quot;'}[s]})+'</div><button type="button" class="hsdw-del">🗑 ডিলেট</button>';
      row.querySelector('.hsdw-del').onclick=function(){
        window.deleteHeaderSlideByListIndex(x.index);
      };
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    w.classList.add('show');
  }
  window.deleteHeaderSlideByListIndex=function(i){    /* মূল slider-এর render/arrays-এর ভেতরের delete function ব্যবহার */
    if(typeof window.deleteCurrentHeaderSlide!=='function')return;
    var all=window.__headerSlideDeleteState;    if(!all||!Array.isArray(all))return;
    if(i<0||i>=all.length)return;
    /* বর্তমান slider index-এ নিয়ে গিয়ে authoritative delete চালানো */
    try{
      if(typeof window.__setHeaderSlideIndex==='function')window.__setHeaderSlideIndex(i);
      window.deleteCurrentHeaderSlide();
      setTimeout(renderList,80);
    }catch(e){}
  };
  window.openHeaderSlideDeleteList=function(){
    renderList();
  };
  window.closeHeaderSlideDeleteList=function(){
    var w=document.getElementById('headerSlideDeleteWindow');
    if(w)w.classList.remove('show');
  };
  document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
    var b=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('#headerSlideDeleteBtn');
    if(!b)return;
    e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    renderList();
  },true);
})();
</script>

<script id="header-slide-delete-state-bridge-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  /* Final bridge: use the original slider's private index setter/delete function.
     Do not override either function; the original slider already exposes both correctly. */
  function sync(){
    try{
      var data=typeof window.__getHeaderSlideList==='function'?window.__getHeaderSlideList():[];
      window.__headerSlideDeleteState=data;
    }catch(e){window.__headerSlideDeleteState=[];}
  }
  sync();
  setInterval(sync,500);
})();
</script>

<script id="header-calendar-full-month-final-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  var state={cal:'en',cursor:new Date()};
  var cfg={
    en:{locale:'en-GB',label:'ইংরেজী ক্যালেন্ডার',week:['Sun','Mon','Tue','Wed','Thu','Fri','Sat']},
    ar:{locale:'ar-SA-u-ca-islamic',label:'আরবী ক্যালেন্ডার',week:['الأحد','الإثنين','الثلاثاء','الأربعاء','الخميس','الجمعة','السبت']},
    bn:{locale:'bn-BD-u-ca-beng',label:'বাংলা ক্যালেন্ডার',week:['রবি','সোম','মঙ্গল','বুধ','বৃহস্পতি','শুক্র','শনি']}
  };
  function parts(d,locale){
    var a=new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale,{year:'numeric',month:'numeric',day:'numeric'}).formatToParts(d),o={};
    a.forEach(function(x){if(x.type!=='literal')o[x.type]=x.value});
    return o;
  }
  function monthKey(d,locale){
    var p=parts(d,locale);return p.year+'|'+p.month;
  }
  function monthLabel(d,locale){
    return new Intl.DateTimeFormat(locale,{year:'numeric',month:'long'}).format(d);
  }
  function makeDays(){
    var locale=cfg[state.cal].locale, cur=state.cursor;
    var target=monthKey(cur,locale), arr=[], start=new Date(cur.getTime());
    start.setDate(start.getDate()-45);
    for(var i=0;i<140;i++){
      var d=new Date(start.getTime());d.setDate(start.getDate()+i);
      if(monthKey(d,locale)===target)arr.push(d);
    }
    return arr;
  }
  function render(){
    var w=document.getElementById('headerCalendarWindow');if(!w)return;
    var c=cfg[state.cal], days=makeDays(), grid=w.querySelector('.hc-grid'), label=w.querySelector('.hc-month-label');
    label.textContent=c.label+' — '+monthLabel(state.cursor,c.locale);
    grid.innerHTML='';
    c.week.forEach(function(x){
      var h=document.createElement('div');h.className='hc-weekday';h.textContent=x;grid.appendChild(h);
    });
    var first=days[0], lead=first?first.getDay():0;
    for(var z=0;z<lead;z++){var e=document.createElement('div');e.className='hc-day empty';grid.appendChild(e);}
    var today=new Date(), todayKey=monthKey(today,c.locale)+'|'+parts(today,c.locale).day;
    days.forEach(function(d){
      var p=parts(d,c.locale), cell=document.createElement('div');cell.className='hc-day';
      if(monthKey(d,c.locale)+'|'+p.day===todayKey)cell.classList.add('today');
      cell.innerHTML='<div><div class="num">'+p.day+'</div><span class="greg">'+d.getDate()+'-'+(d.getMonth()+1)+'</span></div>';
      grid.appendChild(cell);
    });
  }
  function ensure(){
    var w=document.getElementById('headerCalendarWindow');
    if(w)return w;
    w=document.createElement('div');w.id='headerCalendarWindow';
    w.innerHTML='<div class="hc-head"><button class="hc-nav" id="hcPrev">‹</button><div class="hc-title">ক্যালেন্ডার</div><button class="hc-nav" id="hcNext">›</button><button class="hc-close" onclick="closeHeaderCalendar()">✕</button></div><div class="hc-body"><div class="hc-month-label"></div><div class="hc-grid"></div></div>';
    document.body.appendChild(w);
    w.querySelector('#hcPrev').onclick=function(){state.cursor.setMonth(state.cursor.getMonth()-1);render();};
    w.querySelector('#hcNext').onclick=function(){state.cursor.setMonth(state.cursor.getMonth()+1);render();};
    return w;
  }
  window.openHeaderCalendar=function(cal){
    state.cal=cal||'en';state.cursor=new Date();
    ensure().classList.add('show');render();
  };
  window.closeHeaderCalendar=function(){
    var w=document.getElementById('headerCalendarWindow');if(w)w.classList.remove('show');
  };
  var t=setInterval(function(){
    var s=document.getElementById('headerCalendarSelect');
    if(!s)return;
    clearInterval(t);
    s.onchange=function(){
      var v=s.value;if(!v)return;
      openHeaderCalendar(v);
      s.value='';
    };
  },100);
})();
</script>

<script id="search-profile-photo-and-actions-authoritative-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  function esc(v){return String(v==null?'':v).replace(/[&<>"']/g,function(c){return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]})}
  function photoUrl(v){
    var s=String(v==null?'':v).trim();
    if(!s)return '';
    if(/^data:image\//i.test(s))return s;
    if(/^DRIVE:/i.test(s))return 'https://drive.google.com/thumbnail?id='+encodeURIComponent(s.slice(6).trim())+'&sz=w800';
    var m=s.match(/(?:[?&]id=|\/d\/|\/file\/d\/|open\?id=)([A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,})/i);
    if(m)return 'https://drive.google.com/thumbnail?id='+encodeURIComponent(m[1])+'&sz=w800';
    if(/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,}$/.test(s))return 'https://drive.google.com/thumbnail?id='+encodeURIComponent(s)+'&sz=w800';
    if(/^https?:\/\//i.test(s))return s;
    return '';
  }
  window.__searchProfilePhotoUrl=photoUrl;
  document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
    var b=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('#searchProfilePrint .sp-bottom-actions button');
    if(!b)return;
    var t=(b.textContent||'').trim();
    if(t.indexOf('সেভ')>=0 && typeof window.saveSearchProfile==='function'){e.preventDefault();window.saveSearchProfile();return}
    if(t.indexOf('এডিট')>=0 && typeof window.editSearchProfile==='function'){e.preventDefault();window.editSearchProfile();return}
    if(t.indexOf('প্রিন্ট')>=0 && typeof window.printSearchProfile==='function'){e.preventDefault();window.printSearchProfile();return}
    if(t.indexOf('ডাউনলোড')>=0 && typeof window.downloadSearchProfile==='function'){e.preventDefault();window.downloadSearchProfile();return}
  },true);
})();
</script>

<script id="header-delete-open-list-authoritative-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  function bind(){
    var b=document.getElementById('headerSlideDeleteBtn');
    if(!b||b.dataset.finalBound==='1')return;
    b.dataset.finalBound='1';
    b.onclick=function(e){
      e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
      if(typeof window.openHeaderSlideDeleteList==='function')window.openHeaderSlideDeleteList();
    };
  }
  function syncState(){
    try{
      if(typeof window.__getHeaderSlideList==='function'){
        window.__headerSlideDeleteState=window.__getHeaderSlideList();
      }
    }catch(e){}
    bind();
  }
  setTimeout(syncState,100);
  setInterval(syncState,500);
})();

<script id="header-delete-list-authoritative-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  function removeAt(i){
    var list=typeof window.__getHeaderSlideList==='function'?window.__getHeaderSlideList():[];
    i=Number(i);
    if(!isFinite(i)||i<0||i>=list.length)return;
    if(typeof window.__setHeaderSlideIndex==='function')window.__setHeaderSlideIndex(i);
    if(typeof window.deleteCurrentHeaderSlide==='function')window.deleteCurrentHeaderSlide();
    setTimeout(function(){
      var w=document.getElementById('headerSlideDeleteWindow');
      var body=document.getElementById('headerSlideDeleteList');
      if(!w||!body)return;
      body.innerHTML='';
      var now=typeof window.__getHeaderSlideList==='function'?window.__getHeaderSlideList():[];
      if(!now.length){
        body.innerHTML='<div style="text-align:center;font-weight:900;padding:25px">কোনো Header স্লাইড নেই</div>';
        return;
      }
      now.forEach(function(x){
        var row=document.createElement('div');
        row.className='hsdw-item';
        var img=document.createElement('img');img.src=x.src||'';img.alt='';
        var name=document.createElement('div');name.className='hsdw-name';name.textContent=x.label||('Header Slide '+(x.index+1));
        var btn=document.createElement('button');btn.type='button';btn.className='hsdw-del';btn.textContent='🗑 ডিলেট';
        btn.onclick=function(e){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();removeAt(x.index);};
        row.appendChild(img);row.appendChild(name);row.appendChild(btn);body.appendChild(row);
      });
      w.classList.add('show');
    },120);
  }
  window.deleteHeaderSlideByListIndex=removeAt;
})();
</script>

<script id="final-search-profile-photo-actions-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  function photoCandidates(src){
    var s=String(src||'').trim(), out=[];
    if(!s)return out;
    if(/^data:image\//i.test(s))return [s];
    var m=s.match(/(?:[?&]id=|\/d\/|\/file\/d\/|open\?id=)([A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,})/i);
    if(!m && /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,}$/.test(s))m=[s,s];
    if(m){
      var id=m[1]||m[0];
      out.push('https://drive.google.com/uc?export=view&id='+encodeURIComponent(id));
      out.push('https://drive.google.com/thumbnail?id='+encodeURIComponent(id)+'&sz=w1000');
      return out;
    }
    if(/^https?:\/\//i.test(s))out.push(s);
    return out;
  }
  function fixPhotos(root){
    root=root||document;
    root.querySelectorAll('#searchProfilePrint .sp-photo').forEach(function(img){
      if(img.dataset.photoFinal==='1')return;
      var src=img.getAttribute('src')||'';
      var cs=photoCandidates(src);
      if(!cs.length)return;
      img.dataset.photoFinal='1';
      var n=0;
      function next(){
        if(n>=cs.length)return;
        img.src=cs[n++];
      }
      img.onerror=next;
      next();
    });
  }
  function ensureActions(){
    var p=document.getElementById('searchProfilePrint');
    if(!p)return;
    var old=p.querySelector('.sp-bottom-actions');
    if(old)return;
    var d=document.createElement('div');
    d.className='sp-bottom-actions';
    d.innerHTML='<button type="button" class="primary">💾 সেভ</button><button type="button" class="secondary">✏️ এডিট</button><button type="button" class="secondary">🖨️ প্রিন্ট</button><button type="button" class="secondary">⬇️ ডাউনলোড</button>';
    d.children[0].onclick=function(){if(typeof window.saveSearchProfile==='function')window.saveSearchProfile();};
    d.children[1].onclick=function(){if(typeof window.editSearchProfile==='function')window.editSearchProfile();};
    d.children[2].onclick=function(){if(typeof window.printSearchProfile==='function')window.printSearchProfile();};
    d.children[3].onclick=function(){if(typeof window.downloadSearchProfile==='function')window.downloadSearchProfile();};
    p.appendChild(d);
  }
  function run(){ensureActions();fixPhotos(document);}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',run);else run();
  new MutationObserver(function(){run();}).observe(document.documentElement,{subtree:true,childList:true});
})();
</script>

<script id="final-header-delete-open-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  function openList(){
    var w=document.getElementById('headerSlideDeleteWindow');
    if(!w){
      w=document.createElement('div');
      w.id='headerSlideDeleteWindow';
      w.innerHTML='<div class="hsdw-head"><span>Header ছবির তালিকা</span><button type="button" class="hsdw-close">✕</button></div><div class="hsdw-body" id="headerSlideDeleteList"></div>';
      document.body.appendChild(w);
      w.querySelector('.hsdw-close').onclick=function(){w.classList.remove('show');};
    }
    var body=document.getElementById('headerSlideDeleteList');
    var list=[];
    try{if(typeof window.__getHeaderSlideList==='function')list=window.__getHeaderSlideList()||[];}catch(e){}
    body.innerHTML='';
    list.forEach(function(x,i){
      var row=document.createElement('div');row.className='hsdw-item';
      var im=document.createElement('img');im.src=x.src||'';im.alt='';
      var nm=document.createElement('div');nm.className='hsdw-name';nm.textContent=x.label||('Header Slide '+(i+1));
      var del=document.createElement('button');del.type='button';del.className='hsdw-del';del.textContent='🗑 ডিলেট';
      del.onclick=function(e){
        e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
        try{
          if(typeof window.__setHeaderSlideIndex==='function')window.__setHeaderSlideIndex(x.index);
          if(typeof window.deleteCurrentHeaderSlide==='function')window.deleteCurrentHeaderSlide();
        }catch(err){}
        setTimeout(openList,150);
      };
      row.appendChild(im);row.appendChild(nm);row.appendChild(del);body.appendChild(row);
    });
    if(!list.length)body.innerHTML='<div style="text-align:center;font-weight:900;padding:25px">কোনো Header স্লাইড নেই</div>';
    w.classList.add('show');  }
  function hook(){
    var b=document.getElementById('headerSlideDeleteBtn');
    if(b)b.onclick=function(e){e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();openList();};  }
  hook();setInterval(hook,300);
  document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
    var b=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('#headerSlideDeleteBtn');
    if(b){e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();openList();}
  },true);
  window.openHeaderSlideDeleteList=openList;
})();
</script>

<script id="admission-form-header-only-js-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  function addHeader(){
    var w=document.getElementById('formWin');
    if(!w || !w.classList.contains('show'))return;
    var title=document.getElementById('winTitle');
    if(!title || !/ভর্তি|admission/i.test(title.textContent||''))return;
    var body=document.getElementById('winBody');
    if(!body)return;
    var form=body.querySelector('.form-grid');
    if(!form)return;
    var old=body.querySelector('.admission-form-header-only');
    if(!old){
      old=document.createElement('div');
      old.className='admission-form-header-only';
      old.innerHTML='<img class="afh-logo" alt="Logo"><div class="afh-text"><div class="afh-bn">জান্নাতুল বাক্বী মহিলা মাদ্রাসা ও এতিমখানা</div><div class="afh-ar">المدرسة الجنة الباقية للبنات ودار الايتام</div><div class="afh-address">উপজেলা মডেল টাউন কোনাখোলা, কেরাণীগঞ্জ, ঢাকা-১৩১০</div><div class="afh-mobile">মোবাইল: 01823316630, 01717339288</div></div><div class="afh-photo-wrap"><img class="afh-photo" alt="স্টুডেন্ট ফটো"><div class="afh-photo-label">স্টুডেন্ট ফটো</div></div>';
      body.insertBefore(old,form);
    }
    var logo=document.getElementById('headerLogo')||document.querySelector('.header .logo');
    var li=old.querySelector('.afh-logo');
    if(logo&&li&&logo.src)li.src=logo.src;
    var input=document.getElementById('adPhoto'),pi=old.querySelector('.afh-photo');
    if(input&&input.dataset.admissionHeaderBound!=='1'){
      input.dataset.admissionHeaderBound='1';
      input.addEventListener('change',function(){
        var f=input.files&&input.files[0]; if(!f)return;
        if(!/^image\//i.test(f.type))return;
        var fr=new FileReader();
        fr.onload=function(){pi.src=fr.result;pi.dataset.fileName=f.name};
        fr.readAsDataURL(f);
      });
    }
  }
  function run(){addHeader()}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',run);else run();
  new MutationObserver(function(){addHeader()}).observe(document.documentElement,{subtree:true,childList:true});
  window.addAdmissionHeader=addHeader;
})();
</script>

<script id="search-profile-a4-pad-3col-js-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  function esc(v){return String(v==null?'':v).replace(/[&<>"']/g,function(c){return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]})}
  function first(r,keys){for(var i=0;i<keys.length;i++){if(r&&r[keys[i]]!==undefined&&r[keys[i]]!==null&&String(r[keys[i]])!=='')return r[keys[i]]}return ''}
  function qr(text){return 'https://quickchart.io/qr?size=160&text='+encodeURIComponent(text||'Jannatul Baqi Digital Platform 2026')}
  function photo(v){
    var s=String(v||'').trim(); if(!s)return '';
    if(/^data:image\\//i.test(s))return s;
    var m=s.match(/(?:[?&]id=|\\/d\\/|\\/file\\/d\\/|open\\?id=)([A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,})/i);
    if(/^DRIVE:/i.test(s))return 'https://drive.google.com/thumbnail?id='+encodeURIComponent(s.slice(6).trim())+'&sz=w600';
    if(m)return 'https://drive.google.com/thumbnail?id='+encodeURIComponent(m[1])+'&sz=w600';
    if(/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,}$/.test(s))return 'https://drive.google.com/thumbnail?id='+encodeURIComponent(s)+'&sz=w600';
    return /^https?:\\/\\//i.test(s)?s:'';
  }
  window.renderSearchProfilesA4Pad=function(rows,signal,q){
    rows=rows||[];
    var p=rows[0]||{};
    var pv=photo(first(p,['Photo','ছবি','PhotoURL','Image','ImageURL']));

    var cards=rows.map(function(r){

      var fields=[
        ['স্টুডেন্ট ID',first(r,['StudentID','ID','Serial','Student Id'])],

        ['NID',first(r,['NID'])]
      ];
      return '<div class="sp-student"><div class="sp-student-top"><div class="sp-student-info"><div class="sp-student-title">'+esc(title)+' — স্টুডেন্ট তথ্য</div></div></div><div class="sp-data">'+fields.map(function(f){return '<div class="sp-field"><b>'+esc(f[0])+'</b><span>'+esc(f[1])+'</span></div>'}).join('')+'</div></div>';
    }).join('');

    $('searchBody').innerHTML='<div id="searchProfilePrint" class="search-profile"><div class="sp-pad-meta" style="font-size:10px;margin:2px 0 5px;font-weight:800">সার্চ ফলাফল: '+esc(signal)+' / '+esc(q)+' — '+rows.length+' জন</div>'+head+cards+actions+'</div>';
  };
  var oldRender=window.renderSearchProfiles;

  window.printSearchProfile=function(){

<!-- ===== FINAL SEARCH PROFILE ROUTING FIX — 2026-09-23 ===== -->
<script id="final-search-profile-routing-fix-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  /* সর্বশেষ doSearch/table override যেন নতুন A4 Pad renderer-কে আর bypass না করে। */
  window.doSearch=function(where){
    var sigEl=document.getElementById(where==='login'?'loginSignal':'appSignal');
    var qEl=document.getElementById(where==='login'?'loginSearch':'appSearch');
    var sig=sigEl?sigEl.value:'', q=qEl?(qEl.value||'').trim():'';
    if(!sig)return toast('প্রথমে সংকেত নির্বাচন করুন।');
    if(!q)return toast('ID / মোবাইল / ইমেইল দিন।');
    var searchToken=(where==='login')?'':(window.token||token||'');
    call('searchAll',searchToken,sig,q).then(function(r){
      if(!r||!r.ok)return toast((r&&r.message)||'সার্চের ফল পাওয়া যায়নি।');
      if(typeof window.renderSearchProfilesA4Pad==='function'){
        window.renderSearchProfilesA4Pad(r.matches||[],r.signal||sig,r.query||q);
      }else if(typeof window.renderSearchProfiles==='function'){
        window.renderSearchProfiles(r.matches||[],r.signal||sig,r.query||q);
      }else{
        var body=document.getElementById('searchBody');
        if(body)body.innerHTML='<h3>'+(r.signal||sig)+' / '+(r.query||q)+' — '+(r.count||0)+' ফলাফল</h3>'+table(r.matches||[]);
      }
      openWindow('searchWin');
    }).catch(function(e){toast('সার্চ সার্ভার ত্রুটি: '+(e&&e.message?e.message:e));});
  };
})();
</script>

<!-- ===== FINAL DIRECT VIDEO PLAYER + MEDIA LIST + DELETE LIVE FIX — 2026-09-23 ===== -->
<style id="final-direct-video-media-live-fix-2026-09-23">
#mediaStageContent video.media-direct-player{
  width:100%!important;height:100%!important;max-width:none!important;max-height:none!important;
  object-fit:contain!important;display:block!important;background:#000!important;
}
#mediaList{display:block!important;width:100%!important;margin-top:8px!important}
.media-list-item{
  display:flex!important;align-items:center!important;justify-content:space-between!important;
  gap:8px!important;padding:7px 9px!important;margin:4px 0!important;
  border:2px solid var(--border)!important;background:var(--panel)!important;
}
.media-list-item span{min-width:0!important;overflow:hidden!important;text-overflow:ellipsis!important;white-space:nowrap!important}
.media-list-item button{flex:0 0 auto!important}
</style>
<script id="final-direct-video-media-live-fix-js-2026-09-23">
(function(){
  var loading=false, timer=null;

  function arr(){ return Array.isArray(window.media)?window.media:[]; }
  function norm(){
    var m=arr(); if(!m.length){window.media=[];window.mediaIndex=0;return;}
    var n=Number(window.mediaIndex); if(!isFinite(n))n=0;
    window.mediaIndex=((n%m.length)+m.length)%m.length;
  }
  function direct(x){
    return String((x&&(
      x.DirectURL||x.DataURL||x.Url||x.URL||x.FileURL||x.fileUrl
    ))||'').trim();
  }
  function typeOf(x){return String(x&& (x.Type||x.type)||'').toLowerCase();}
  function esc(v){
    return String(v==null?'':v).replace(/[&<>"']/g,function(c){      return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c];
    });
  }

  function draw(){
    norm();
    var box=document.getElementById('mediaStageContent');
    if(!box)return;
    var m=arr();
    if(!m.length){
      box.innerHTML='<span class="media-empty">মিডিয়া আপলোড করে স্লাইড দেখুন</span>';
      renderList();
      return;
    }
    var x=m[window.mediaIndex]||{}, t=typeOf(x), u=direct(x);
    box.innerHTML='';
    if(t==='video'){
      var v=document.createElement('video');
      v.className='media-direct-player';
      v.controls=true;
      v.playsInline=true;
      v.preload='auto';
      v.muted=true;
      if(u)v.src=u;
      v.addEventListener('ended',function(){
        var a=arr();if(!a.length)return;
        window.mediaIndex=(Number(window.mediaIndex||0)+1)%a.length;
        draw();
        autoplay();
      });
      v.addEventListener('loadedmetadata',function(){autoplay();},{once:true});
      v.addEventListener('canplay',function(){autoplay();},{once:true});
      v.addEventListener('error',function(){});
      box.appendChild(v);
      setTimeout(autoplay,60);
    }else if(t==='audio'){
      var a=document.createElement('audio');
      a.controls=true;a.preload='auto';a.src=u;
      a.addEventListener('ended',function(){
        var m2=arr();if(!m2.length)return;
        window.mediaIndex=(Number(window.mediaIndex||0)+1)%m2.length;
        draw();autoplay();
      });
      box.appendChild(a);
      setTimeout(autoplay,60);
    }else{
      var im=document.createElement('img');
      im.src=u;im.alt=String(x.Name||'');
      im.style.cssText='width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:contain;display:block;background:#111';
      im.addEventListener('error',function(){box.innerHTML='<span class="media-empty">ফটো চালানো যাচ্ছে না।</span>'});
      box.appendChild(im);
      clearTimeout(timer);
      timer=setTimeout(function(){
        var a=arr();if(a.length<=1)return;
        window.mediaIndex=(Number(window.mediaIndex||0)+1)%a.length;
        draw();
      },6000);
    }
    renderList();
  }

  function autoplay(){
    var v=document.querySelector('#mediaStageContent video.media-direct-player,#mediaStageContent audio');
    if(!v)return;
    try{
      v.muted=true;
      var p=v.play();      if(p&&p.catch)p.catch(function(){});
    }catch(e){}
  }

  function renderList(){    var box=document.getElementById('mediaList');if(!box)return;
    var m=arr();
    box.innerHTML=m.map(function(x,i){
      var active=(i===Number(window.mediaIndex||0))?' style="font-weight:900"':'';
      return '<div class="media-list-item"><span'+active+'>'+esc(x.Name||('Media '+(i+1)))+'</span>'+
        '<button type="button" class="secondary" data-direct-media-delete="'+i+'">🗑 Delete</button></div>';
    }).join('');
  }

  async function refresh(){
    if(loading||typeof window.call!=='function'||!window.token)return;
    loading=true;
    try{
      var r=await window.call('listMedia',window.token);
      var m=Array.isArray(r)?r:(r&&Array.isArray(r.media)?r.media:(r&&Array.isArray(r.rows)?r.rows:[]));
      window.media=m||[];
      norm();
      draw();
    }catch(e){}
    loading=false;
  }

  async function del(i){
    var m=arr(), x=m[i];if(!x)return;
    try{
      var r=await window.call('deleteMedia',window.token,x.Serial);
      if(r&&r.ok){
        m.splice(i,1);
        if(window.mediaIndex>=m.length)window.mediaIndex=Math.max(0,m.length-1);
        draw();
        renderList();
        setTimeout(refresh,100);
        if(typeof window.toast==='function')window.toast('মিডিয়া মুছে ফেলা হয়েছে।');
      }else if(typeof window.toast==='function'){
        window.toast((r&&r.message)||'ডিলিট ব্যর্থ');
      }
    }catch(e){
      if(typeof window.toast==='function')window.toast('ডিলিটে সার্ভার ত্রুটি হয়েছে।');
    }
  }

  window.renderMedia=draw;
  window.renderMediaList=renderList;
  window.deleteMediaByIndex=del;
  window.nextMedia=function(){
    var m=arr();if(!m.length)return;
    window.mediaIndex=(Number(window.mediaIndex||0)+1)%m.length;draw();autoplay();
  };
  window.prevMedia=function(){
    var m=arr();if(!m.length)return;
    window.mediaIndex=(Number(window.mediaIndex||0)-1+m.length)%m.length;draw();autoplay();
  };
  window.toggleMediaPlay=function(){autoplay()};

  document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
    var b=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-direct-media-delete]');
    if(b){
      e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
      del(Number(b.getAttribute('data-direct-media-delete')));
      return;
    }
  },true);

  function boot(){
    refresh();
    var tries=0;
    clearInterval(window.__mediaDirectLiveTimer);
    window.__mediaDirectLiveTimer=setInterval(function(){
      if(window.token){refresh();tries++;if(tries>=12)clearInterval(window.__mediaDirectLiveTimer);}
    },1000);
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot);else boot();

  var oldUpload=window.uploadMedia;
  if(typeof oldUpload==='function'){
    window.uploadMedia=async function(){
      var r=await oldUpload.apply(this,arguments);
      setTimeout(refresh,150);
      setTimeout(refresh,900);
      return r;
    };
  }
})();
</script>

#formWin.user-fullscreen-2026{
  position:fixed!important;
  left:0!important;
  top:0!important;
  width:100vw!important;
  height:100vh!important;
  max-width:none!important;
  max-height:none!important;

(function(){
  function toggle(w){
    if(!w)return;
    if(w.classList.contains('user-fullscreen-2026')){
      w.classList.remove('user-fullscreen-2026');
      w.style.width=w.dataset.ufWidth||'';w.style.height=w.dataset.ufHeight||'';
      w.style.maxHeight=w.dataset.ufMaxHeight||'';w.style.left=w.dataset.ufLeft||'';
      w.style.top=w.dataset.ufTop||'';w.style.transform=w.dataset.ufTransform||'';
    }else{
      var r=w.getBoundingClientRect();
      w.dataset.ufWidth=w.style.width||r.width+'px';w.dataset.ufHeight=w.style.height||'';
      w.dataset.ufMaxHeight=w.style.maxHeight||'';w.dataset.ufLeft=w.style.left||r.left+'px';
      w.dataset.ufTop=w.style.top||r.top+'px';w.dataset.ufTransform=w.style.transform||'';
      w.classList.add('user-fullscreen-2026');
    }
  }
  window.formWindowFullscreen2026=function(){var w=document.getElementById('formWin');if(w)toggle(w)};
  document.addEventListener('click',function(e){

  function subjectForm2026(k){
    const title = k==='marks' ? '📋 মার্কশিট' : '🏆 রেজাল্ট কার্ড';
    const classOptions = Object.keys(SUBJECTS_12_2026);
    let rows='';
    for(let i=1;i<=12;i++){
      rows += '<div class="form-grid" style="margin-top:7px">'+
        '<label>বিষয় '+i+' / Subject<select id="mrSubject'+i+'" class="field mr-subject-2026"><option value="">শ্রেণি নির্বাচন করুন</option></select></label>'+
        '<label>নম্বর '+i+' / Marks<input id="mrMark'+i+'" class="field" type="number" min="0" max="100" placeholder="০-১০০"></label>'+
        '<label>গ্রেড '+i+' / Grade<input id="mrGrade'+i+'" class="field" placeholder="ঐচ্ছিক"></label>'+
      '</div>';
    }
    return '<div class="student-meta-row-2026">'+
      '<label>আইডি লিখুন<input id="mrStudentID" class="field"></label>'+
      '<label>জন্ম তারিখ লিখুন<input id="mrDob" class="field" type="date"></label>'+
      '<label>তারিখ লিখুন<input id="mrRecordDate" class="field" type="date"></label>'+
      '</div>'+
      '<div class="form-grid">'+
      '<label>Student ID<input id="mrStudent" class="field"></label>'+
      '<label>নাম<input id="mrName" class="field"></label>'+
      '<label>শ্রেনি / জামাত<select id="mrClass" class="field" onchange="loadMarkResultSubjects2026()">'+
        classOptions.map(x=>'<option>'+x+'</option>').join('')+
      '</select></label>'+
      '<label>পরীক্ষা<input id="mrExam" class="field"></label>'+
      '<label>পূর্ণমান<input id="mrFullMark" class="field" type="number" value="100"></label>'+
      '<label>মন্তব্য<input id="mrNote" class="field"></label>'+
      '</div>'+
      '<div class="mr-subject-title-2026">'+title+' — ১২টি বিষয় / Subject</div>'+
      rows+
      '<button class="primary" type="button" onclick="saveMarkResult2026(\''+k+'\')">💾 '+title+' সংরক্ষণ</button>';
  }

  function fillSubjects2026(){
    const cls=document.getElementById('mrClass');
    const list=SUBJECTS_12_2026[(cls&&cls.value)||'']||[];
    for(let i=1;i<=12;i++){
      const s=document.getElementById('mrSubject'+i);
      if(!s)continue;
      const old=s.value;
      s.innerHTML='<option value="">বিষয় নির্বাচন করুন</option>'+list.map(x=>'<option value="'+String(x).replace(/"/g,'&quot;')+'">'+x+'</option>').join('');
      if(list.indexOf(old)>=0)s.value=old;
    }
  }

  window.loadMarkResultSubjects2026=fillSubjects2026;
  window.saveMarkResult2026=async function(k){
    const subjects=[];
    for(let i=1;i<=12;i++){
      const name=document.getElementById('mrSubject'+i)?.value||'';
      const mark=document.getElementById('mrMark'+i)?.value||'';
      const grade=document.getElementById('mrGrade'+i)?.value||'';
      if(name)subjects.push({Subject:name,Marks:mark,Grade:grade});
    }
    const d={
      StudentID:document.getElementById('mrStudent')?.value||document.getElementById('mrStudentID')?.value||'',
      Name:document.getElementById('mrName')?.value||'',
      Class:document.getElementById('mrClass')?.value||'',
      Exam:document.getElementById('mrExam')?.value||'',
      SubjectData:JSON.stringify(subjects),
      Total:subjects.reduce((a,x)=>a+(Number(x.Marks)||0),0),
      Published:'NO',
      Date:document.getElementById('mrRecordDate')?.value||new Date().toISOString(),
      DOB:document.getElementById('mrDob')?.value||''
    };
    try{
      const r=await call('saveRecord',token,'RESULTS',d);
      toast(r.ok?'সফলভাবে সংরক্ষণ হয়েছে':(r.message||'সংরক্ষণ ব্যর্থ'));
    }catch(e){toast('সংরক্ষণে সার্ভার ত্রুটি হয়েছে।');}
  };

  const oldModuleForm=window.moduleForm;
  if(typeof oldModuleForm==='function'){
    window.moduleForm=function(k){
      if(k==='marks'||k==='result')return subjectForm2026(k);
      return oldModuleForm(k);
    };
  }

  const style=document.createElement('style');
  style.textContent='.mr-subject-title-2026{margin-top:12px;padding:9px;border:2px solid var(--border);border-radius:10px;font-weight:900;text-align:center}.mr-subject-2026{font-weight:800}';
  document.head.appendChild(style);
})();
</script>

function loadExamSubjects2026(){
 const cls=document.getElementById('examClass2026')?.value||'';
 const list=(EXAM_SUBJECTS_2026[cls]||[]).slice(0,12);
 for(let i=1;i<=12;i++){
   const s=document.getElementById('examSubject'+i); if(!s)continue;
   s.innerHTML='<option value="">বিষয় নির্বাচন করুন</option>'+list.map(x=>'<option value="'+String(x).replace(/"/g,'&quot;')+'">'+x+'</option>').join('');
   if(i>list.length)s.disabled=true; else s.disabled=false;
 }
}
window.loadExamSubjects2026=loadExamSubjects2026;
const oldModuleForm=window.moduleForm;
window.moduleForm=function(k){
 if(k!=='exam')return oldModuleForm(k);
 let h='<div class="form-grid"><label>Student ID<input id="rStudent" class="field"></label><label>নাম<input id="rName" class="field"></label><label>শ্রেণি / জামাত<select id="examClass2026" class="field" onchange="loadExamSubjects2026()">'+classes.map(x=>'<option value="'+x+'">'+x+'</option>').join('')+'</select></label><label>পরীক্ষা<input id="rExam" class="field"></label></div><div class="mr-subject-title-2026">পরীক্ষার্থী নিবন্ধন — শ্রেণি/জামাত অনুযায়ী বিষয় নির্বাচন (সর্বোচ্চ ১২টি)</div>';
 for(let i=1;i<=12;i++)h+='<label style="display:block;margin:7px 0">বিষয় '+i+' / Subject<select id="examSubject'+i+'" class="field"><option value="">বিষয় নির্বাচন করুন</option></select></label>';
 h+='<div class="form-grid"><label>বিভাগ<select id="rDivisionSelect" class="field"></select></label><label>জেলা<select id="rDistrictSelect" class="field"></select></label><label>উপজেলা / থানা<select id="rUpazilaSelect" class="field"></select></label><label>ইউনিয়ন<select id="rUnionSelect" class="field"></select></label><label>ডাকঘর<select id="rPostSelect" class="field"></select></label><label>ওয়ার্ড<select id="rWardSelect" class="field"></select></label><label>গ্রাম<select id="rVillageSelect" class="field"></select></label></div><button class="primary" onclick="saveResult('exam')">Save</button>';
 return h;
};
const oldSave=window.saveResult;
window.saveResult=async function(k){
 if(k!=='exam')return oldSave(k);
 const subjects=[];for(let i=1;i<=12;i++){const v=document.getElementById('examSubject'+i)?.value||'';if(v)subjects.push(v);}
 const d={StudentID:document.getElementById('rStudent')?.value||'',Name:document.getElementById('rName')?.value||'',Class:document.getElementById('examClass2026')?.value||'',Exam:document.getElementById('rExam')?.value||'',SubjectData:JSON.stringify(subjects),Published:'NO',Date:new Date().toISOString()};
 const r=await call('saveRecord',token,'EXAM_REG',d);toast(r.ok?'পরীক্ষার্থী নিবন্ধন সফলভাবে সংরক্ষণ হয়েছে':(r.message||'ব্যর্থ'));
};
})();

<script id="list-button-window-fix-js-2026">
(function(){
  var ITEMS=[
    ['students','👩‍🎓 ছাত্র/ছাত্রী তালিকা','students'],
    ['teachers','👩‍🏫 শিক্ষক/শিক্ষিকা তালিকা','teachers'],
    ['donors','🤝 দাতা সদস্য তালিকা','donors'],

    ['admins','🔐 অ্যাডমিন তালিকা','admins'],
    ['registration','📝 নিবন্ধন তালিকা','exams'],
    ['femaleMadrasas','🏫 নিবন্ধনকৃত মহিলা মাদ্রাসার তালিকা','madrasas'],
    ['maleMadrasas','🏫 নিবন্ধনকৃত পুরুষ মাদ্রাসার তালিকা','madrasas'],
    ['onlineAdmission','📥 অনলাইন ভর্তি তালিকা','admissions'],
    ['marksheet','📊 মার্কশিট তালিকা','results'],
    ['mobileNumbers','📱 মোবাইল নাম্বার তালিকা','students'],
    ['mms','📨 এমএমএস তালিকা','sms'],
    ['sms','✉️ এসএমএস তালিকা','sms']
  ];
  function esc(v){return String(v==null?'':v).replace(/[&<>"']/g,function(c){return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]})}
  function ensureSelector(){
    var w=document.getElementById('listSelectorWindow2026');if(w)return w;
    w=document.createElement('div');w.id='listSelectorWindow2026';w.className='window';
    var h='<div class="win-head"><b>📋 তালিকা নির্বাচন</b><button class="secondary" type="button" onclick="closeWindow(\'listSelectorWindow2026\')">✕</button></div><div class="win-body"><div class="list-selector-grid">';
    ITEMS.forEach(function(x){h+='<button class="secondary" type="button" data-list-key="'+x[0]+'" data-backend-key="'+x[2]+'">'+x[1]+'</button>';});
    h+='</div></div>';w.innerHTML=h;document.body.appendChild(w);dragWindow(w);
    w.querySelectorAll('[data-list-key]').forEach(function(b){b.onclick=function(){closeWindow('listSelectorWindow2026');openListWindow(b.dataset.listKey,b.dataset.backendKey,b.textContent);};});
    return w;
  }
  function ensureListWindow(){
    var w=document.getElementById('moduleListWindow2026');if(w)return w;
    w=document.createElement('div');w.id='moduleListWindow2026';w.className='window';
    w.innerHTML='<div class="win-head"><b id="moduleListTitle2026">তালিকা</b><button class="secondary" type="button" onclick="closeWindow(\'moduleListWindow2026\')">✕</button></div><div class="win-body" id="moduleListBody2026">⏳ তালিকা লোড হচ্ছে...</div><div class="list-footer"><button class="primary" type="button" onclick="printListWindow2026()">🖨️ প্রিন্ট</button><button class="secondary" type="button" onclick="downloadListWindow2026()">⬇️ ডাউনলোড</button></div>';
    document.body.appendChild(w);dragWindow(w);return w;
  }
  function renderRows(rows,backendKey){
    var w=ensureListWindow(),body=document.getElementById('moduleListBody2026'),heads=rows.length?Object.keys(rows[0]):[];
    var h='<div class="table-wrap"><table class="data-table"><thead><tr>'+heads.map(function(k){return '<th>'+esc(k)+'</th>'}).join('')+'<th>কার্যক্রম</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
    if(!rows.length){h+='<tr><td colspan="99">কোন তথ্য পাওয়া যায়নি।</td></tr>';}
    rows.forEach(function(r,i){h+='<tr data-row-index="'+i+'">'+heads.map(function(k){return '<td data-key="'+esc(k)+'">'+esc(r[k])+'</td>'}).join('')+'<td><div class="list-actions"><button class="secondary" type="button" onclick="listDetail2026('+i+')">📄 বিস্তারিত দেখুন</button><button class="secondary" type="button" onclick="listEdit2026('+i+')">✏️</button></div></td></tr>';});
    body.innerHTML=h+'</tbody></table></div>';
  }
  var currentRows=[],currentBackend='',currentTitle='';
  window.openListSelector=function(){baseOpen('listSelectorWindow2026');};
  function baseOpen(id){ensureSelector();if(typeof baseOpenWindow==='function')baseOpenWindow(id);else{var w=document.getElementById(id);w.classList.add('show');}}
  window.openListWindow=function(key,backend,title){
    currentBackend=backend;currentTitle=title;ensureListWindow();
    document.getElementById('moduleListTitle2026').textContent=title;
    document.getElementById('moduleListBody2026').innerHTML='<p>⏳ '+esc(title)+' লোড হচ্ছে...</p>';
    if(typeof baseOpenWindow==='function')baseOpenWindow('moduleListWindow2026');else document.getElementById('moduleListWindow2026').classList.add('show');
    if(typeof call!=='function'||!window.token){document.getElementById('moduleListBody2026').innerHTML='<p>লগইন সেশন পাওয়া যায়নি।</p>';return;}
    call('getModuleList',token,backend).then(function(r){
      currentRows=Array.isArray(r&&r.rows)?r.rows:(Array.isArray(r&&r.data)?r.data:[]);
      if(key==='femaleMadrasas')currentRows=currentRows.filter(function(x){return /female|মহিলা|নারী/i.test(String(x.Gender||x.gender||x.Type||x.type||x.Category||x.category||''));});
      if(key==='maleMadrasas')currentRows=currentRows.filter(function(x){return /male|পুরুষ/i.test(String(x.Gender||x.gender||x.Type||x.type||x.Category||x.category||''));});
      if(key==='mobileNumbers')currentRows=currentRows.filter(function(x){return x.Mobile||x.mobile||x.Phone||x.phone;});
      renderRows(currentRows,backend);
    }).catch(function(e){document.getElementById('moduleListBody2026').innerHTML='<p>সার্ভার ত্রুটি: '+esc(e&&e.message||e)+'</p>';});
  };
  window.listDetail2026=function(i){
    var r=currentRows[i]||{},html='<div class="form-grid">'+Object.keys(r).map(function(k){return '<label>'+esc(k)+'<input class="field" readonly value="'+esc(r[k])+'"></label>';}).join('')+'</div>';
    document.getElementById('moduleListBody2026').innerHTML=html+'<button class="secondary" type="button" onclick="renderRows(window.__listRows2026||currentRows,currentBackend)">↩ তালিকায় ফিরুন</button>';
    window.__listRows2026=currentRows;
  };
  window.listEdit2026=function(i){
    var r=currentRows[i]||{},body=document.getElementById('moduleListBody2026'),heads=Object.keys(r),h='<div class="form-grid">';
    heads.forEach(function(k){h+='<label>'+esc(k)+'<input class="field" data-edit-key="'+esc(k)+'" value="'+esc(r[k])+'"></label>';});
    h+='</div><div style="display:flex;justify-content:center;gap:8px;margin-top:10px"><button class="primary" type="button" onclick="listSave2026('+i+')">💾 সেভ</button><button class="secondary" type="button" onclick="renderRows(window.__listRows2026||currentRows,currentBackend)">↩ তালিকায় ফিরুন</button></div>';
    body.innerHTML=h;window.__listRows2026=currentRows;
  };
  window.listSave2026=function(i){
    var r=currentRows[i]||{},data={},body=document.getElementById('moduleListBody2026');
    body.querySelectorAll('[data-edit-key]').forEach(function(x){data[x.dataset.editKey]=x.value;});
    var serial=r.Serial||r.serial||r.ID||r.Id||'';
    if(!serial){toast('এই রেকর্ডের Serial/ID পাওয়া যায়নি।');return;}
    call('updateRecord',token,currentBackend,serial,data).then(function(v){
      if(v&&v.ok){Object.keys(data).forEach(function(k){r[k]=data[k]});toast('সেভ সফল হয়েছে।');renderRows(currentRows,currentBackend);}
      else toast((v&&v.message)||'সেভ ব্যর্থ হয়েছে।');
    }).catch(function(e){toast('সেভ সার্ভার ত্রুটি: '+esc(e&&e.message||e));});
  };
  window.printListWindow2026=function(){var body=document.getElementById('moduleListBody2026');if(!body)return;var w=window.open('','_blank');w.document.write('<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>'+esc(currentTitle)+'</title><style>body{font-family:Arial}.data-table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%}.data-table th,.data-table td{border:1px solid #333;padding:6px}.list-actions{display:none!important}</style></head><body><h2>'+esc(currentTitle)+'</h2>'+body.innerHTML+'</body></html>');w.document.close();w.print();};

<!-- ===== MEDIA PLAYBACK STABLE FIX — 2026-09-30 ===== -->
<style id="media-playback-stable-fix-2026-09-30">
#mediaStageContent .media-stable-frame{width:100%!important;height:100%!important;border:0!important;background:#111!important;display:block!important}
#mediaStageContent .media-stable-video{width:100%!important;height:100%!important;object-fit:contain!important;background:#111!important;display:block!important}
</style>
<script id="media-playback-stable-fix-js-2026-09-30">
(function(){
  function list(){return Array.isArray(window.media)?window.media:[]}
  function idOf(x){var s=String((x&&x.FileID)||(x&&x.DriveFileId)||'');if(s)return s;s=String((x&&x.DataURL)||'');var m=s.match(/[?&]id=([^&]+)/)||s.match(/\/d\/([^/]+)/);return m?decodeURIComponent(m[1]):''}
  function resourceOf(x){return String((x&&x.ResourceKey)||'').trim()}
  function previewOf(x){var id=idOf(x);if(!id)return String((x&&x.PreviewURL)||'');var rk=resourceOf(x);return 'https://drive.google.com/file/d/'+encodeURIComponent(id)+'/preview'+(rk?'?resourcekey='+encodeURIComponent(rk):'')}
  function directOf(x){var u=String((x&&x.DirectURL)||(x&&x.DataURL)||'').trim();if(/^https?:\/\//i.test(u)&&u.indexOf('drive.google.com/uc?')>=0)return u;var id=idOf(x);if(!id)return u;var rk=resourceOf(x);return 'https://drive.google.com/uc?export=download&id='+encodeURIComponent(id)+(rk?'&resourcekey='+encodeURIComponent(rk):'')}
  function renderStable(){
    var box=document.getElementById('mediaStageContent'),m=list();if(!box)return;
    if(!m.length){box.innerHTML='<span class="media-empty">মিডিয়া আপলোড করে স্লাইড দেখুন</span>';return}
    var n=Number(window.mediaIndex||0);if(!isFinite(n))n=0;window.mediaIndex=((n%m.length)+m.length)%m.length;
    var x=m[window.mediaIndex]||{},t=String(x.Type||x.type||'').toLowerCase();box.innerHTML='';
    if(t==='video'){
      var fr=document.createElement('iframe');fr.className='media-stable-frame';fr.allow='autoplay; fullscreen; encrypted-media; picture-in-picture';fr.setAttribute('allowfullscreen','');fr.setAttribute('referrerpolicy','no-referrer-when-downgrade');fr.src=previewOf(x);box.appendChild(fr);
    }else if(t==='audio'){
      var a=document.createElement('audio');a.controls=true;a.preload='metadata';a.src=directOf(x);a.style.cssText='width:90%;max-height:80px';box.appendChild(a);
    }else if(t==='image'){
      var im=document.createElement('img');im.src=directOf(x);im.alt=String(x.Name||x.name||'');im.style.cssText='width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:contain;background:#111;display:block';box.appendChild(im);
    }else box.innerHTML='<span class="media-empty">মিডিয়ার ধরন পাওয়া যায়নি।</span>';
    if(typeof window.renderMediaList==='function')window.renderMediaList();
  }
  window.renderMedia=renderStable;
  window.nextMedia=function(){var m=list();if(!m.length)return;window.mediaIndex=(Number(window.mediaIndex||0)+1)%m.length;renderStable()};
  window.prevMedia=function(){var m=list();if(!m.length)return;window.mediaIndex=(Number(window.mediaIndex||0)-1+m.length)%m.length;renderStable()};
  window.toggleMediaPlay=function(){var f=document.querySelector('#mediaStageContent .media-stable-frame');if(f)f.focus();var v=document.querySelector('#mediaStageContent video.media-stable-video,#mediaStageContent audio');if(v){if(v.paused){var p=v.play();if(p&&p.catch)p.catch(function(){})}else v.pause()}};
})();
</script>

  var busy=false,tries=0;

.jb-universal-fs-2026{width:34px!important;min-width:34px!important;height:30px!important;padding:0!important;display:inline-flex!important;align-items:center!important;justify-content:center!important;border:2px solid var(--border)!important;border-radius:7px!important;background:var(--panel)!important;color:inherit!important;font-weight:900!important;cursor:pointer!important}

<script id="jb-universal-fullscreen-js-2026">
(function(){
  function isTarget(w){return w&&((w.classList&&(w.classList.contains('window')||w.classList.contains('form-window')))||w.id==='formWin')}
  function toggle(w){
    if(!isTarget(w))return;
    if(w.classList.contains('jb-universal-fullscreen-2026')){
      w.classList.remove('jb-universal-fullscreen-2026');
      w.style.width=w.dataset.jbFsWidth||'';w.style.height=w.dataset.jbFsHeight||'';w.style.maxHeight=w.dataset.jbFsMaxHeight||'';
      w.style.left=w.dataset.jbFsLeft||'';w.style.top=w.dataset.jbFsTop||'';w.style.transform=w.dataset.jbFsTransform||'';
    }else{
      var r=w.getBoundingClientRect();
      w.dataset.jbFsWidth=w.style.width||r.width+'px';w.dataset.jbFsHeight=w.style.height||r.height+'px';w.dataset.jbFsMaxHeight=w.style.maxHeight||'';
      w.dataset.jbFsLeft=w.style.left||r.left+'px';w.dataset.jbFsTop=w.style.top||r.top+'px';w.dataset.jbFsTransform=w.style.transform||'';
      w.classList.add('jb-universal-fullscreen-2026');
    }
  }
  function add(w){
    if(!isTarget(w))return;
    var head=w.querySelector('.win-head');if(!head)return;
    var b=head.querySelector('.jb-universal-fs-2026');
    if(!b){
      b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.className='jb-universal-fs-2026';b.textContent='⛶';b.title='ফুল স্ক্রিন / স্বাভাবিক';b.setAttribute('aria-label','ফুল স্ক্রিন / স্বাভাবিক');
      var controls=head.querySelector('.task3-window-controls');
      if(controls)controls.insertBefore(b,controls.lastElementChild||null);else head.appendChild(b);
    }
    if(b.dataset.jbBound==='1')return;b.dataset.jbBound='1';
    b.onclick=function(e){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();toggle(w)};
  }
  function scan(){document.querySelectorAll('.window,.form-window').forEach(add);var f=document.getElementById('formWin');if(f)add(f)}
  window.jbUniversalFullscreen2026=toggle;
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',scan);else scan();
  setTimeout(scan,300);setTimeout(scan,1000);setInterval(scan,1500);
  new MutationObserver(function(ms){ms.forEach(function(m){m.addedNodes&&m.addedNodes.forEach(function(n){if(n.nodeType===1){if(n.matches&&n.matches('.window,.form-window,#formWin'))add(n);n.querySelectorAll&&n.querySelectorAll('.window').forEach(add)}})})}).observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>

(function(){
  function roleOf(u){return String((u||{}).Role||(u||{}).role||'').toUpperCase().replace(/\\s+/g,'_')}

  function isCustom(u){var t=String((u||{}).accountType||(u||{}).AccountType||'').toLowerCase();return t==='madrasa'||(t==='admin'&&!isSuper(u))}
  function isPending(u){return isCustom(u)&&String((u||{}).Status||(u||{}).status||'PENDING').toUpperCase()!=='ACTIVE'}
  function photoOf(u){return String((u||{}).Photo||(u||{}).photo||(u||{}).PhotoURL||(u||{}).ImageURL||'').trim()}

  }

(function(){
  function setContactLogo(){
    var logo=document.getElementById('contactLoginLogo2026');
    var login=document.getElementById('loginLogo');
    if(logo && login && login.src) logo.src=login.src;
  }
  function setContactQR(url){
    var qr=document.getElementById('contactMainQR2026');
    if(qr && url){
      qr.src='https://quickchart.io/qr?size=220&margin=2&text='+encodeURIComponent(url)+'&v='+Date.now();
    }
  }
  function setContactMedia(){
    setContactLogo();
    if(typeof call==='function'){
      call('getMainWebAppUrl').then(function(r){
        if(r&&r.ok&&r.url) setContactQR(r.url);
      }).catch(function(){});
    }
  }
  function run(){setContactMedia();}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',run);else run();
  setTimeout(run,1000);
  setInterval(run,60000);
})();

<script id="all-profile-print-main-qr-js-2026-10-05">
(function(){
  var MAIN_QR_URL='';
  function qrImage(url){
    return 'https://quickchart.io/qr?size=220&margin=2&text='+encodeURIComponent(url)+'&v='+Date.now();
  }
  function applyMainQR(){
    if(!MAIN_QR_URL)return;
    document.querySelectorAll('.search-profile .sp-qr').forEach(function(q){
      q.src=qrImage(MAIN_QR_URL);
      q.alt='Main Web App QR Code';
    });
  }
  function loadMainQR(){
    if(typeof call!=='function')return;
    call('getMainWebAppUrl').then(function(r){
      if(r&&r.ok&&r.url){
        MAIN_QR_URL=String(r.url).trim();
        applyMainQR();
      }
    }).catch(function(){});
  }
  window.jbRefreshAllProfilePrintQR2026=function(){applyMainQR();loadMainQR()};
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',loadMainQR);
  else loadMainQR();
  setTimeout(loadMainQR,1200);
  setInterval(applyMainQR,5000);
})();
</script>

<script id="all-profile-print-header-repair-js-2026-10-05">
(function(){
  var mainUrl='';
  function qr(url){
    return 'https://quickchart.io/qr?size=220&margin=2&text='+encodeURIComponent(url)+'&v='+Date.now();
  }
  function logoSrc(){
    var x=document.getElementById('loginLogo');
    return x && x.src ? x.src : '';
  }
  function ensureHeader(){
    var p=document.getElementById('searchProfilePrint');
    if(!p)return;
    var h=p.querySelector('.search-profile-head');
    if(!h)return;
    var logo=h.querySelector('.sp-logo');
    var q=h.querySelector('.sp-qr');
    if(!logo){
      logo=document.createElement('img');
      logo.className='sp-logo';
      logo.alt='মাদ্রাসার Logo';
      h.insertBefore(logo,h.firstChild);
    }
    var ls=logoSrc();
    if(ls)logo.src=ls;
    if(!q){
      q=document.createElement('img');
      q.className='sp-qr';
      q.alt='Main Web App QR Code';
      h.appendChild(q);
    }
    if(mainUrl)q.src=qr(mainUrl);
  }
  function loadUrl(){
    if(typeof call!=='function')return;
    call('getMainWebAppUrl').then(function(r){
      if(r&&r.ok&&r.url){mainUrl=String(r.url).trim();ensureHeader();}
    }).catch(function(){});
  }
  function run(){ensureHeader();loadUrl();}
  window.jbRepairAllProfilePrintHeader2026=run;
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',run);else run();
  setTimeout(run,500);setTimeout(run,1500);setInterval(ensureHeader,3000);
})();
</script>

<script id="login-logo-left-qr-right-2026-10-06-js">
(function(){
  function arrangeLoginBrand(){
    var page=document.getElementById('loginPage');
    if(!page)return;
    var logoWrap=page.querySelector('.loginLogoWrap');
    var qr=page.querySelector('.main-qr-802268e');
    var auth=page.querySelector('.login-auth-qr-802268e');
    if(!logoWrap||!qr||!auth)return;
    var brand=page.querySelector('.login-brand-qr-2026');
    if(!brand){
      brand=document.createElement('div');
      brand.className='login-brand-qr-2026';
      logoWrap.parentNode.insertBefore(brand,logoWrap);
      brand.appendChild(logoWrap);
    }
    if(qr.parentNode!==brand)brand.appendChild(qr);
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',arrangeLoginBrand);
  else arrangeLoginBrand();
  setTimeout(arrangeLoginBrand,300);
  setTimeout(arrangeLoginBrand,1000);
})();
</script>

var cur="bn",busy=false;
function tr(v){v=String(v==null?"":v);return (D[cur]&&D[cur][v])||v}
function walk(root){if(!root)return;var w=document.createTreeWalker(root,NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT),a=[];while(w.nextNode())a.push(w.currentNode);a.forEach(function(n){var p=n.parentElement;if(!p||/^(SCRIPT|STYLE)$/.test(p.tagName))return;var raw=n.__jbOrig; if(raw==null){raw=n.nodeValue;n.__jbOrig=raw}var x=raw.trim();if(!x)return;var y=tr(x);n.nodeValue=raw.replace(x,y)});root.querySelectorAll&&root.querySelectorAll("input,textarea,select,button,[title],[aria-label]").forEach(function(e){e.__jbOrigAttrs=e.__jbOrigAttrs||{};["placeholder","title","aria-label"].forEach(function(k){if(e.hasAttribute(k)){if(e.__jbOrigAttrs[k]==null)e.__jbOrigAttrs[k]=e.getAttribute(k);var y=tr(e.__jbOrigAttrs[k]);e.setAttribute(k,y)}});e.querySelectorAll&&e.querySelectorAll("option").forEach(function(o){if(o.__jbOrig==null)o.__jbOrig=o.textContent; o.textContent=tr(o.__jbOrig)})})}
function modulesText(){if(!window.modules)return;document.querySelectorAll("#leftModules .module,#rightModules .module").forEach(function(b,i){var m=modules[i];if(m&&M[m[0]])b.textContent=M[m[0]][cur==="bn"?0:cur==="ar"?1:2]});var fs=document.getElementById("featureSelect");if(fs)fs.querySelectorAll("option").forEach(function(o){var m=modules.find(function(x){return String(x[0])===String(o.value)});if(m&&M[m[0]])o.textContent=M[m[0]][cur==="bn"?0:cur==="ar"?1:2]})}
function sync(){["loginLang","appLang","headerCalendarSelect"].forEach(function(id){var e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.value=cur});document.documentElement.lang=cur;document.documentElement.dir=cur==="ar"?"rtl":"ltr"}
function setLang(l){if(busy)return;busy=true;cur=(l==="ar"||l==="en")?l:"bn";localStorage.lang=cur;sync();walk(document.body);modulesText();busy=false}
window.setLang=setLang;window.jbSetLang2026=setLang;
window.tick=function(){var d=new Date(),tz="Asia/Dhaka",loc=cur==="ar"?"ar-BD":cur==="en"?"en-GB":"bn-BD",tm=d.toLocaleTimeString(loc,{timeZone:tz,hour:"2-digit",minute:"2-digit",second:"2-digit",hour12:false}),dt=d.toLocaleDateString(loc,{timeZone:tz,weekday:"long",year:"numeric",month:"long",day:"numeric"});var e=document.getElementById("loginDateTime");if(e)e.textContent=tr("সময়")+" "+tm+" • "+dt;e=document.getElementById("appClock");if(e)e.textContent=tm;e=document.getElementById("appDate");if(e)e.textContent=dt};
var obs=new MutationObserver(function(ms){if(!cur)return;ms.forEach(function(m){m.addedNodes&&m.addedNodes.forEach(function(n){if(n.nodeType===1)walk(n)})})});
function init(){cur=localStorage.lang||"bn";obs.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});setLang(cur);setInterval(window.tick,1000)}
if(document.readyState==="loading")document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded",init);else init();
})();
</script>

function gp(v){v=Number(v)||0;return v>=80?5:v>=70?4:v>=60?3.5:v>=50?3:v>=40?2:v>=33?1:0}
function gr(v){v=Number(v)||0;return v>=80?'A+':v>=70?'A':v>=60?'A-':v>=50?'B':v>=40?'C':v>=33?'D':'F'}
var gpa=data.length?(data.reduce(function(a,x){return a+gp(x.marks)},0)/data.length).toFixed(2):'0.00',grade=gr(avg),rankLabel='হিসাব হচ্ছে...';

  function print(){
    var p=E('jbDocPreviewWrap')?.querySelector('.jb-doc-preview');
    if(!p)return toast('আগে Document তৈরি করুন।');

    var title=esc(currentDoc&&currentDoc.title||'Document');

      '.jb-doc-head{text-align:center;border-bottom:2px solid #333;padding-bottom:8px;margin-bottom:10px}.jb-doc-head h2,.jb-doc-head h3,.jb-doc-head p{margin:3px}'+
      '.jb-doc-photo{float:right;width:90px;height:110px;object-fit:cover;border:2px solid #222;margin-left:10px}.jb-doc-idcard .jb-doc-photo{width:70px;height:82px}'+
      '.jb-doc-row{display:flex;gap:8px;padding:4px 0;border-bottom:1px dotted #999}.jb-doc-row b{min-width:135px}.jb-corner{position:absolute;inset:0;border:3px solid #c62828;pointer-events:none}'+
      '.jb-verification-qr{break-inside:avoid}.jb-verification-qr img{display:block;margin:auto}'+
      '.jb-doc-actions,.jb-marksheet-back-2026{display:none!important;visibility:hidden!important}'+
      '.jb-institution-header-2026{display:grid!important;grid-template-columns:18mm minmax(0,1fr) 18mm!important;gap:2mm!important;align-items:start!important;width:100%!important;box-sizing:border-box!important;padding:0 0 3mm!important;margin:0 0 4mm!important;direction:ltr!important;overflow:visible!important}'+

      '</style></head><body>'+p.outerHTML+'</body></html>');
