<!-- ===== CENTRAL DOCUMENT BUILDER — HISTORY ACTIONS FINAL — 2026-10-06 ===== -->
<script id="central-document-builder-history-actions-2026-10-06">
(function(){
  function H(){return document.getElementById('jbDocBuilderTitle')?.textContent||''}
  function key(){
    var t=H();
    return t.indexOf('মার্কশিট')>=0?'marks':
      t.indexOf('ফলাফল')>=0?'result':
      t.indexOf('আইডি কার্ড')>=0?'id_cards':
      t.indexOf('অ্যাডমিট কার্ড')>=0?'admit_cards':
      t.indexOf('সার্টিফিকেট')>=0?'certificates':
      t.indexOf('মানি রিসিট')>=0?'receipts':'marks';
  }
  function rid(x){return x.ResultID||x.CardID||x.AdmitID||x.CertificateID||x.ReceiptID||''}
  function dt(x){return x.IssueDate||x.Date||x.CreatedAt||''}
  function fill(data){
    Object.keys(data||{}).forEach(function(k){
      var e=document.querySelector('#jbDocFields [data-key="'+CSS.escape(k)+'"]');
      if(e)e.value=data[k]==null?'':data[k];
    });
    if(data.StudentID){
      var id=document.getElementById('jbDocId');if(id)id.value=data.StudentID;
    }
  }
  async function loadRecord(id){
    var r=await call('getDocumentBuilderRecord',token,key(),id);
    if(!r||!r.ok)throw new Error((r&&r.message)||'History record পাওয়া যায়নি।');
    fill(r.data||{});
    return r;
  }
  window.jbShowHistory=async function(){
    var sid=(document.getElementById('jbDocId')?.value||'').trim();
    if(!sid)return toast('প্রথমে Student ID দিন।');
    try{
      var r=await call('getDocumentBuilderHistory',token,sid,key());
      if(!r||!r.ok)return toast((r&&r.message)||'History পাওয়া যায়নি।');
      var box=document.getElementById('jbDocPreviewWrap'), rows=r.rows||[];
      var d=document.createElement('div');d.className='jb-doc-note';
      d.innerHTML='<b>📚 Document History — '+esc(sid)+'</b><br><br>'+
        (rows.length?rows.map(function(x){
          var id=rid(x);
          return '<div style="padding:9px 0;border-bottom:1px solid #ccc;display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap">'+
            '<b style="min-width:210px">'+esc(String(id))+'</b>'+
            '<span style="margin-right:auto">'+esc(String(dt(x)))+'</span>'+
            '<button class="secondary" onclick="jbHistoryView('+JSON.stringify(String(id))+')">👁 দেখুন</button>'+
            '<button class="secondary" onclick="jbHistoryEdit('+JSON.stringify(String(id))+')">✏️ Edit</button>'+
            '<button class="secondary" onclick="jbHistoryDownload('+JSON.stringify(String(id))+')">⬇️ Download</button>'+
            '<button class="primary" onclick="jbHistoryPrint('+JSON.stringify(String(id))+')">🖨️ Print</button>'+
          '</div>';
        }).join(''):'কোনো সংরক্ষিত Document নেই।');
      box.innerHTML='';box.appendChild(d);
    }catch(e){toast('History লোড হয়নি: '+(e&&e.message?e.message:e))}
  };
  window.jbHistoryView=async function(id){
    try{await loadRecord(id);jbCreatePreview();toast('👁 পুরোনো Document দেখা যাচ্ছে।')}
    catch(e){toast('Document খোলা যায়নি: '+e.message)}
  };
  window.jbHistoryEdit=async function(id){
    try{await loadRecord(id);jbCreatePreview();jbEditPreview();toast('✏️ পুরোনো Document Edit-এর জন্য খোলা হয়েছে। Save করলে নতুন History version তৈরি হবে।')}
    catch(e){toast('Edit খোলা যায়নি: '+e.message)}
  };
  window.jbHistoryDownload=async function(id){
    try{await loadRecord(id);jbCreatePreview();jbDownload()}
    catch(e){toast('Download করা যায়নি: '+e.message)}
  };
  window.jbHistoryPrint=async function(id){
    try{await loadRecord(id);jbCreatePreview();setTimeout(function(){jbPrint()},150)}
    catch(e){toast('Print খোলা যায়নি: '+e.message)}
  };
})();
</script>
<script id="central-document-builder-all-history-2026-10-06">
(function(){
  function rid(x){return x.ResultID||x.CardID||x.AdmitID||x.CertificateID||x.ReceiptID||''}
  function dt(x){return x.IssueDate||x.Date||x.CreatedAt||''}
  function title(k){return k==='marks'?'মার্কশিট':k==='result'?'ফলাফল / মার্কশিট':k==='id_cards'?'আইডি কার্ড':k==='admit_cards'?'অ্যাডমিট কার্ড':k==='certificates'?'সার্টিফিকেট':k==='receipts'?'মানি রিসিট':k}
  window.jbShowAllHistory=async function(){
    var sid=(document.getElementById('jbDocId')?.value||'').trim();
    if(!sid)return toast('প্রথমে Student ID দিন।');
    try{
      var r=await call('getAllDocumentBuilderHistory',token,sid);
      if(!r||!r.ok)return toast((r&&r.message)||'সব Document-এর তালিকা পাওয়া যায়নি।');
      var box=document.getElementById('jbDocPreviewWrap'),rows=r.rows||[];
      var d=document.createElement('div');d.className='jb-doc-note';
      d.innerHTML='<b>📚 সব Document / Card — '+esc(sid)+'</b><br><br>'+
        (rows.length?rows.map(function(x){
          var id=rid(x),k=x._docKey||'';
          return '<div style="padding:9px 0;border-bottom:1px solid #ccc;display:flex;gap:7px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap">'+
            '<b style="min-width:145px">'+esc(title(k))+'</b><span style="min-width:210px">'+esc(String(id))+'</span>'+
            '<span style="margin-right:auto">'+esc(String(dt(x)))+'</span>'+
            '<button class="secondary" onclick="jbAllHistoryOpen('+JSON.stringify(String(k))+','+JSON.stringify(String(id))+')">👁 দেখুন</button>'+
            '<button class="primary" onclick="jbAllHistoryPrint('+JSON.stringify(String(k))+','+JSON.stringify(String(id))+')">🖨️ Print</button>'+
          '</div>';
        }).join(''):'কোনো সংরক্ষিত Document/Card নেই।');
      box.innerHTML='';box.appendChild(d);
    }catch(e){toast('সব Document-এর তালিকা লোড হয়নি: '+(e&&e.message?e.message:e))}
  };
  async function openOne(k,id,doPrint){
    try{
      if(window.jbOpenDocumentType2026)window.jbOpenDocumentType2026(k);
      var r=await call('getDocumentBuilderRecord',token,k,id);
      if(!r||!r.ok)throw new Error((r&&r.message)||'Document পাওয়া যায়নি।');
      var data=r.data||{};Object.keys(data).forEach(function(n){
        var e=document.querySelector('#jbDocFields [data-key="'+CSS.escape(n)+'"]');if(e)e.value=data[n]==null?'':data[n];
      });
      var e=document.getElementById('jbDocId');if(e)e.value=data.StudentID||r.row?.StudentID||r.row?.RefID||'';
      jbCreatePreview();
      if(doPrint)setTimeout(function(){jbPrint()},180);
    }catch(e){toast('Document খোলা যায়নি: '+(e&&e.message?e.message:e))}
  }
  window.jbAllHistoryOpen=function(k,id){return openOne(k,id,false)};
  window.jbAllHistoryPrint=function(k,id){return openOne(k,id,true)};
})();
</script>
<!-- ===== END HISTORY ACTIONS FINAL ===== -->


<!-- ===== CENTRAL DOCUMENT BUILDER — QR VERIFICATION CLIENT — 2026-10-06 ===== -->
<script id="central-document-builder-qr-verification-2026-10-06">
(function(){
  function escQ(s){return String(s==null?'':s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;').replace(/'/g,'&#39;');}
  function b64(s){try{return btoa(unescape(encodeURIComponent(s)));}catch(e){return btoa(String(s));}}
  function docVerifyUrl(base,key,id){
    base=String(base||window.location.href||'').split('#')[0];
    var q='verify=1&doc='+encodeURIComponent(key)+'&id='+encodeURIComponent(id);
    return base+(base.indexOf('?')>=0?'&':'?')+q;
  }
  function renderQR(box,url){
    box.setAttribute('data-qr',url);
    box.innerHTML='';
    try{
      if(window.QRCode){new QRCode(box,{text:url,width:110,height:110});}
      else box.textContent=url;
    }catch(e){box.textContent=url;}
  }
  function addQR(container,key,id){
    if(!container||!id)return;
    var box=document.createElement('div');
    box.style.cssText='text-align:center;margin:12px auto;padding:8px;border:1px dashed #777;width:max-content;background:#fff';
    box.innerHTML='<div style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:5px">QR যাচাই</div><div class="jb-qr-box"></div><small>Document ID: '+escQ(id)+'</small>';
    container.appendChild(box);
    var qrBox=box.querySelector('.jb-qr-box');
    var fallback=docVerifyUrl(window.location.href,key,id);
    renderQR(qrBox,fallback);
    try{
      if(typeof call==='function'){
        call('getMainWebAppUrl').then(function(r){
          if(r&&r.ok&&r.url) renderQR(qrBox,docVerifyUrl(String(r.url).trim(),key,id));
        }).catch(function(){});
      }
    }catch(e){}
  }
  window.jbAddVerificationQR=function(key,id){
    var p=document.getElementById('jbDocPreview');
    if(!p)return;
    var old=p.querySelector('.jb-verification-qr');if(old)old.remove();
    var holder=document.createElement('div');holder.className='jb-verification-qr';
    p.appendChild(holder);addQR(holder,key,id);
  };
  window.jbVerifyDocument=async function(key,id){
    try{
      var r=await call('getDocumentVerification','',key,id);
      var p=document.getElementById('jbDocPreviewWrap')||document.body;
      var old=document.getElementById('jbDocVerifyResult');if(old)old.remove();
      var d=document.createElement('div');d.id='jbDocVerifyResult';d.style.cssText='position:fixed;z-index:999999;left:50%;top:50%;transform:translate(-50%,-50%);max-width:420px;width:90%;padding:22px;border:4px solid #222;background:#fff;box-shadow:0 10px 35px rgba(0,0,0,.35);text-align:center';
      if(r&&r.verified){
        d.innerHTML='<div style="font-size:28px">✅</div><h3>ডকুমেন্ট যাচাই সফল</h3><p><b>Document ID:</b> '+escQ(r.recordId)+'</p><p><b>Student ID:</b> '+escQ(r.studentId)+'</p><p><b>নাম:</b> '+escQ(r.name)+'</p><p><b>শ্রেণি:</b> '+escQ(r.className)+'</p><p>'+escQ(r.message)+'</p>';
      }else d.innerHTML='<div style="font-size:28px">❌</div><h3>ডকুমেন্ট যাচাই হয়নি</h3><p>'+escQ((r&&r.message)||'রেকর্ড পাওয়া যায়নি।')+'</p>';
      var btn=document.createElement('button');btn.textContent='বন্ধ করুন';btn.className='secondary';btn.onclick=function(){d.remove();};d.appendChild(btn);
      document.body.appendChild(d);
    }catch(e){toast('যাচাই করা যায়নি: '+(e&&e.message?e.message:e));}
  };
  var oldCreate=window.jbCreatePreview;
  if(typeof oldCreate==='function'){
    window.jbCreatePreview=function(){
      var r=oldCreate.apply(this,arguments);
      setTimeout(function(){
        var key=(typeof window.jbDocCurrentKey==='function'?window.jbDocCurrentKey():'');
        var id=(document.getElementById('jbDocId')?.value||'').trim();
        if(key&&id&&window.jbAddVerificationQR) window.jbAddVerificationQR(key,id);
      },80);
      return r;
    };
  }
  window.addEventListener('load',function(){
    var q=new URLSearchParams(location.search);
    if(q.get('verify')==='1'&&q.get('doc')&&q.get('id')){
      setTimeout(function(){jbVerifyDocument(q.get('doc'),q.get('id'));},300);
    }
  });
})();
</script>
<!-- ===== END QR VERIFICATION CLIENT ===== -->

<!-- ===== CENTRAL DOCUMENT BUILDER — AUTO SAVE DRAFT + RESTORE — 2026-10-06 ===== -->
<script id="central-document-builder-autosave-restore-2026-10-06">
(function(){
  var DKEY='jbDocAutoDraft2026';
  var timer=null;
  function E(id){return document.getElementById(id)}
  function key(){
    var t=E('jbDocBuilderTitle')?.textContent||'';
    return t.indexOf('মার্কশিট')>=0?'marks':t.indexOf('ফলাফল')>=0?'result':t.indexOf('আইডি কার্ড')>=0?'id_cards':t.indexOf('অ্যাডমিট কার্ড')>=0?'admit_cards':t.indexOf('সার্টিফিকেট')>=0?'certificates':t.indexOf('মানি রিসিট')>=0?'receipts':'marks';
  }
  function collect(){
    var data={};document.querySelectorAll('#jbDocFields [data-key]').forEach(function(x){data[x.dataset.key]=x.value||''});
    return {key:key(),id:(E('jbDocId')?.value||'').trim(),data:data,updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()};
  }
  function saveAuto(){
    if(!E('jbDocBuilderWin')?.classList.contains('show')||!E('jbDocFields'))return;
    var d=collect();if(!d.id&&!Object.values(d.data).some(Boolean))return;
    try{localStorage.setItem(DKEY,JSON.stringify(d));var s=E('jbDocAutoStatus');if(s)s.textContent='📝 Draft auto-save: '+new Date().toLocaleTimeString('bn-BD')}catch(e){}
  }
  function restore(d){
    if(!d)return;
    if(E('jbDocId'))E('jbDocId').value=d.id||'';
    Object.keys(d.data||{}).forEach(function(k){var e=document.querySelector('#jbDocFields [data-key="'+CSS.escape(k)+'"]');if(e)e.value=d.data[k]==null?'':d.data[k]});
    if(typeof toast==='function')toast('♻️ Draft পুনরুদ্ধার হয়েছে। এখন তথ্য যাচাই করে Document তৈরি করুন।');
  }
  function offer(){
    var raw;try{raw=localStorage.getItem(DKEY)}catch(e){return} if(!raw)return;
    var d;try{d=JSON.parse(raw)}catch(e){return} if(!d||!d.data)return;
    var box=E('jbDocDraftRestore');if(box)box.remove();
    box=document.createElement('div');box.id='jbDocDraftRestore';box.className='jb-doc-note';
    box.style.cssText='position:sticky;top:0;z-index:20;margin:8px 0;padding:10px;border:2px solid #b8860b;background:#fff8dc';
    var when=d.updatedAt?new Date(d.updatedAt).toLocaleString('bn-BD'):'আগের কোনো সময়';
    box.innerHTML='<b>📝 সংরক্ষিত Draft পাওয়া গেছে</b><br><span>শেষ Auto-save: '+esc(when)+'</span> ';
    var yes=document.createElement('button');yes.className='primary';yes.textContent='♻️ পুনরুদ্ধার';yes.onclick=function(){restore(d);box.remove()};
    var no=document.createElement('button');no.className='secondary';no.textContent='✖️ বাদ দিন';no.onclick=function(){try{localStorage.removeItem(DKEY)}catch(e){}box.remove()};
    box.appendChild(yes);box.appendChild(no);
    var fields=E('jbDocFields');if(fields&&fields.parentNode)fields.parentNode.insertBefore(box,fields);
  }
  function start(){clearInterval(timer);timer=setInterval(saveAuto,10000);setTimeout(offer,300)}
  var oldOpen=window.openBuilder;
  if(typeof oldOpen==='function')window.openBuilder=function(){var r=oldOpen.apply(this,arguments);setTimeout(start,500);return r};
  window.jbAutoSaveDraft=saveAuto;
  window.jbRestoreAutoDraft=function(){offer()};
  window.addEventListener('beforeunload',saveAuto);
  window.addEventListener('load',function(){setTimeout(offer,800)});
})();
</script>
<!-- ===== END AUTO SAVE DRAFT + RESTORE ===== -->

<!-- ===== CENTRAL DOCUMENT BUILDER — DRAFT LIFECYCLE SAFETY — 2026-10-06 ===== -->
<script id="central-document-builder-draft-lifecycle-safety-2026-10-06">
(function(){
  var DKEY='jbDocAutoDraft2026';
  function currentKey(){
    var t=document.getElementById('jbDocBuilderTitle')?.textContent||'';
    return t.indexOf('মার্কশিট')>=0?'marks':t.indexOf('ফলাফল')>=0?'result':t.indexOf('আইডি কার্ড')>=0?'id_cards':t.indexOf('অ্যাডমিট কার্ড')>=0?'admit_cards':t.indexOf('সার্টিফিকেট')>=0?'certificates':t.indexOf('মানি রিসিট')>=0?'receipts':'marks';
  }
  function clearStored(){try{localStorage.removeItem(DKEY)}catch(e){}}
  var oldSave=window.jbSaveDraft;
  if(typeof oldSave==='function')window.jbSaveDraft=async function(){
    var r=await oldSave.apply(this,arguments);
    /* Final server save completed; prevent an old Draft from reappearing. */
    clearStored();
    return r;
  };
  var oldClear=window.jbClearDoc;
  if(typeof oldClear==='function')window.jbClearDoc=function(){clearStored();return oldClear.apply(this,arguments)};
  var oldRestore=window.jbRestoreAutoDraft;
  if(typeof oldRestore==='function')window.jbRestoreAutoDraft=function(){return oldRestore.apply(this,arguments)};
  /* Only offer a Draft for the currently opened document type. */
  var oldOpen=window.openBuilder;
  if(typeof oldOpen==='function')window.openBuilder=function(){
    var r=oldOpen.apply(this,arguments);
    setTimeout(function(){
      try{
        var raw=localStorage.getItem(DKEY);if(!raw)return;
        var d=JSON.parse(raw);if(d&&d.key&&d.key!==currentKey()){localStorage.removeItem(DKEY);}
      }catch(e){}
    },700);
    return r;
  };
})();
</script>
<!-- ===== END DRAFT LIFECYCLE SAFETY ===== -->

<!-- ===== CENTRAL DOCUMENT BUILDER — DRAFT SAVE SUCCESS SAFETY FIX — 2026-10-06 ===== -->
<script id="central-document-builder-draft-save-success-safety-2026-10-06">
(function(){
  var DKEY='jbDocAutoDraft2026';
  function key(){
    var t=document.getElementById('jbDocBuilderTitle')?.textContent||'';
    return t.indexOf('মার্কশিট')>=0?'marks':t.indexOf('ফলাফল')>=0?'result':t.indexOf('আইডি কার্ড')>=0?'id_cards':t.indexOf('অ্যাডমিট কার্ড')>=0?'admit_cards':t.indexOf('সার্টিফিকেট')>=0?'certificates':t.indexOf('মানি রিসিট')>=0?'receipts':'marks';
  }
  function clearStored(){try{localStorage.removeItem(DKEY)}catch(e){}}
  function saveSafely(){
    var p=document.getElementById('jbDocPreviewWrap')?.querySelector('.jb-doc-preview');
    if(!p)return toast('আগে Document তৈরি করুন।');
    var id=(document.getElementById('jbDocId')?.value||'').trim();
    if(!id)return toast('Student ID প্রয়োজন।');
    var data={};
    document.querySelectorAll('#jbDocFields [data-key]').forEach(function(x){data[x.dataset.key]=x.value||''});
    var draftRaw=null;
    try{draftRaw=localStorage.getItem(DKEY)}catch(e){}
    return call('saveDocumentBuilderRecord',token,key(),id,data,p.outerHTML).then(function(r){
      if(r&&r.ok){
        clearStored();
        toast('💾 Document + History-তে সংরক্ষণ হয়েছে। Draft মুছে দেওয়া হয়েছে।');
      }else{
        if(draftRaw!==null)try{localStorage.setItem(DKEY,draftRaw)}catch(e){}
        toast((r&&r.message)||'সার্ভারে সংরক্ষণ হয়নি। Draft নিরাপদে রাখা হয়েছে।');
      }
      return r;
    }).catch(function(e){
      if(draftRaw!==null)try{localStorage.setItem(DKEY,draftRaw)}catch(x){}
      toast('সার্ভারে সংরক্ষণ হয়নি: '+(e&&e.message?e.message:e)+' — Draft নিরাপদে রাখা হয়েছে।');
      return {ok:false,message:e&&e.message?e.message:String(e)};
    });
  }
  window.jbSaveDraft=saveSafely;
})();
</script>
<!-- ===== END DRAFT SAVE SUCCESS SAFETY FIX ===== -->


<!-- ===== CENTRAL DOCUMENT BUILDER — EDIT SYNC SAFETY — 2026-10-06 ===== -->
<script id="central-document-builder-edit-sync-safety-2026-10-06">
(function(){
  function syncPreviewToFields(){
    var wrap=document.getElementById('jbDocPreviewWrap');
    var fields=document.querySelectorAll('#jbDocFields [data-key]');
    if(!wrap||!fields.length)return;
    var map={};
    fields.forEach(function(inp){
      var lab=inp.closest('.jb-doc-field')?.querySelector('label');
      if(lab){
        var text=Array.from(lab.childNodes).map(function(n){return n.nodeType===3?n.textContent:''}).join('').trim();        if(text&&!map[text])map[text]=inp;
      }
    });
    wrap.querySelectorAll('.jb-doc-row').forEach(function(row){
      var b=row.querySelector('b'), span=row.querySelector('[contenteditable]');
      if(!b||!span)return;
      var inp=map[b.textContent.trim()];
      if(!inp)return;
      span.dataset.jbEditKey=inp.dataset.key;
      span.oninput=function(){inp.value=span.textContent.trim()};
      span.onblur=function(){inp.value=span.textContent.trim()};
    });
  }
  var oldCreate=window.jbCreatePreview;
  if(typeof oldCreate==='function'){
    window.jbCreatePreview=function(){
      var r=oldCreate.apply(this,arguments);
      setTimeout(syncPreviewToFields,0);
      return r;
    };
  }
  var oldEdit=window.jbEditPreview;
  if(typeof oldEdit==='function'){
    window.jbEditPreview=function(){
      var r=oldEdit.apply(this,arguments);
      syncPreviewToFields();
      return r;
    };
  }
  window.jbSyncPreviewToFields2026=syncPreviewToFields;
  setTimeout(syncPreviewToFields,500);
})();
</script>
<!-- ===== END EDIT SYNC SAFETY ===== -->


<!-- ===== CENTRAL DOCUMENT BUILDER — SAVED QR VERIFICATION FIX — 2026-10-06 ===== -->
<script id="central-document-builder-saved-qr-verification-fix-2026-10-06">
(function(){
  function currentKey(){
    var t=document.getElementById('jbDocBuilderTitle')?.textContent||'';
    return t.indexOf('ফলাফল')>=0?'result':
      t.indexOf('মার্কশিট')>=0?'marks':
      t.indexOf('আইডি কার্ড')>=0?'id_cards':
      t.indexOf('অ্যাডমিট কার্ড')>=0?'admit_cards':
      t.indexOf('সার্টিফিকেট')>=0?'certificates':
      t.indexOf('মানি রিসিট')>=0?'receipts':'marks';
  }
  function addSavedQR(docId){
    var wrap=document.getElementById('jbDocPreviewWrap');
    var p=wrap&&wrap.querySelector('.jb-doc-preview');
    if(!p||!docId||!window.jbAddVerificationQR)return;
    p.querySelectorAll('.jb-verification-qr').forEach(function(x){x.remove()});
    window.jbAddVerificationQR(currentKey(),String(docId));
  }
  window.jbDocCurrentKey=currentKey;
  var oldSave=window.jbSaveDraft;
  if(typeof oldSave==='function'){
    window.jbSaveDraft=function(){
      return Promise.resolve(oldSave.apply(this,arguments)).then(function(r){
        if(r&&r.ok&&r.documentId){
          addSavedQR(r.documentId);
          toast('🔐 QR যাচাই কোড Document-এর সাথে যুক্ত হয়েছে।');
        }
        return r;
      });
    };
  }
  var oldCreate=window.jbCreatePreview;
  if(typeof oldCreate==='function'){
    window.jbCreatePreview=function(){
      var r=oldCreate.apply(this,arguments);
      setTimeout(function(){
        var wrap=document.getElementById('jbDocPreviewWrap');
        var p=wrap&&wrap.querySelector('.jb-doc-preview');
        if(p)p.querySelectorAll('.jb-verification-qr').forEach(function(x){x.remove()});
      },20);
      return r;
    };
  }
})();
</script>
<!-- ===== END SAVED QR VERIFICATION FIX ===== -->

<!-- ===== CENTRAL DOCUMENT BUILDER — QR RENDER TARGET FIX — 2026-10-06 ===== -->
<script id="central-document-builder-qr-render-target-fix-2026-10-06">
(function(){
  function renderSavedQR(key,id){
    var wrap=document.getElementById('jbDocPreviewWrap');
    var p=wrap&&wrap.querySelector('.jb-doc-preview');
    if(!p||!id)return;
    p.querySelectorAll('.jb-verification-qr').forEach(function(x){x.remove()});
    var url=(window.getMainWebAppUrl?window.getMainWebAppUrl():window.location.href).split('#')[0];
    url+= (url.indexOf('?')>=0?'&':'?')+'verify=1&doc='+encodeURIComponent(key)+'&id='+encodeURIComponent(id);
    var holder=document.createElement('div');
    holder.className='jb-verification-qr';
    holder.style.cssText='text-align:center;margin:12px auto;padding:8px;border:1px dashed #777;width:max-content;background:#fff';
    holder.innerHTML='<div style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:5px">QR যাচাই</div><div class="jb-qr-box"></div><small>Document ID: '+String(id).replace(/[&<>"]/g,function(c){return {"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;","\"":"&quot;"}[c]})+'</small>';
    p.appendChild(holder);
    var box=holder.querySelector('.jb-qr-box');
    try{
      if(window.QRCode){
        new QRCode(box,{text:url,width:110,height:110});
      }else{
        box.innerHTML='<img alt="QR" style="width:110px;height:110px" src="https://quickchart.io/qr?size=220&margin=2&text='+encodeURIComponent(url)+'">';
      }
    }catch(e){
      box.innerHTML='<img alt="QR" style="width:110px;height:110px" src="https://quickchart.io/qr?size=220&margin=2&text='+encodeURIComponent(url)+'">';
    }
  }
  window.jbRenderSavedVerificationQR2026=renderSavedQR;
  var oldSave=window.jbSaveDraft;
  if(typeof oldSave==='function'){
    window.jbSaveDraft=function(){
      return Promise.resolve(oldSave.apply(this,arguments)).then(function(r){
        if(r&&r.ok&&r.documentId){
          var key=(typeof window.jbDocCurrentKey==='function'?window.jbDocCurrentKey():'marks');
          renderSavedQR(key,String(r.documentId));
        }
        return r;
      });
    };
  }
})();
</script>

<script id="central-document-builder-history-edit-new-version-qr-cycle-2026-10-06">
(function(){
  var editingFrom='';
  var oldEdit=window.jbHistoryEdit;
  if(typeof oldEdit==='function'){
    window.jbHistoryEdit=async function(id){
      editingFrom=String(id||'');
      var r=await oldEdit.apply(this,arguments);
      if(editingFrom) toast('🔁 History থেকে Edit চলছে — Save করলে পুরোনো রেকর্ড অপরিবর্তিত রেখে নতুন History version তৈরি হবে।');
      return r;
    };
  }

  var oldSave=window.jbSaveDraft;
  if(typeof oldSave==='function'){
    window.jbSaveDraft=function(){
      var source=editingFrom;
      return Promise.resolve(oldSave.apply(this,arguments)).then(function(r){
        if(source && r && r.ok && r.documentId){
          if(window.jbRenderSavedVerificationQR2026){
            window.jbRenderSavedVerificationQR2026(
              typeof window.jbDocCurrentKey==='function'?window.jbDocCurrentKey():'marks',
              String(r.documentId)
            );
          }
          toast('✅ নতুন History version সংরক্ষিত হয়েছে। পুরোনোটি রাখা হয়েছে। নতুন QR-ও যুক্ত হয়েছে।');
          editingFrom='';
        }
        return r;
      });
    };
  }
})();
</script>

<!-- ===== CENTRAL DOCUMENT BUILDER — QR WHITE PAGE FIX — 2026-10-06 ===== -->
<script id="central-document-builder-qr-white-page-fix-2026-10-06">
(function(){
  function appUrl(){
    var u=String(window.location.href||'').split('#')[0];
    return u;
  }

  function canonicalWebAppUrl_(u){
    u=String(u||'').trim().split('#')[0];

    // Never put the temporary googleusercontent userCodeAppPanel URL in a QR.
    if(/userCodeAppPanel/i.test(u)) return '';

    // Accept only the Apps Script web-app /exec URL.
    if(/https:\/\/script\.google\.com\/macros\/s\/[^/]+\/exec/i.test(u)){
      return u;
    }

    return '';
  }

  function verifyUrl(key,id,base){
    base=canonicalWebAppUrl_(base);

    if(!base){
      base=canonicalWebAppUrl_(appUrl());
    }

    if(!base){
      // Last-resort: keep the current host but remove the temporary panel path.
      var origin=String(window.location.origin||'');
      if(/^https:\/\/script\.google\.com$/i.test(origin)){
        base=origin;
      }
    }

    if(!base) return '';

    return base+(base.indexOf('?')>=0?'&':'?')+
      'verify=1&doc='+encodeURIComponent(String(key||'marks'))+
      '&id='+encodeURIComponent(String(id||''));
  }

  function safeQR(key,id){
    var p=document.getElementById('jbDocPreview');
    if(!p||!id)return;

    p.querySelectorAll('.jb-verification-qr').forEach(function(x){
      x.remove();
    });

    var holder=document.createElement('div');
    holder.className='jb-verification-qr';
    holder.style.cssText='text-align:center;margin:12px auto;padding:8px;border:1px dashed #777;width:max-content;background:#fff';
    holder.innerHTML='<div style="font-weight:700;margin-bottom:5px">QR যাচাই</div><div class="jb-qr-box"></div><small>Document ID: '+String(id).replace(/[&<>"]/g,function(c){return {"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;","\"":"&quot;"}[c]})+'</small>';
    p.appendChild(holder);

    var box=holder.querySelector('.jb-qr-box');

    function renderQR(base){
      var url=verifyUrl(key,id,base);

      // Do not generate a QR at all if a canonical /exec URL cannot be obtained.
      // This prevents another broken white-page QR from being saved.
      if(!url){
        box.innerHTML='<div style="color:#b00;font-weight:700;padding:8px">QR URL পাওয়া যায়নি</div>';
        return;
      }

      try{
        if(window.QRCode){
          new QRCode(box,{text:url,width:110,height:110});
        }else{
          box.innerHTML='<img alt="QR" style="width:110px;height:110px" src="https://quickchart.io/qr?size=220&margin=2&text='+encodeURIComponent(url)+'">';
        }
      }catch(e){
        box.innerHTML='<img alt="QR" style="width:110px;height:110px" src="https://quickchart.io/qr?size=220&margin=2&text='+encodeURIComponent(url)+'">';
      }
    }

    try{
      if(window.google&&google.script&&google.script.run&&google.script.run.getMainWebAppUrl){
        google.script.run
          .withSuccessHandler(function(u){
            var canonical=canonicalWebAppUrl_(u);
            if(canonical){
              renderQR(canonical);
            }else{
              renderQR('');
            }
          })
          .withFailureHandler(function(){
            renderQR('');
          })
          .getMainWebAppUrl();
      }else{
        renderQR('');
      }
    }catch(e){
      renderQR('');
    }
  }
    window.jbAddVerificationQR=safeQR;
  window.jbRenderSavedVerificationQR2026=function(key,id){safeQR(key,id)};
  var oldShow=window.jbShowPublicVerification2026;
  if(typeof oldShow==='function'){
    window.jbShowPublicVerification2026=function(key,id,r){
      oldShow.apply(this,arguments);
      var card=document.getElementById('jbPublicVerifyCard');
      if(!card)return;
      var base=appUrl();
      var url=base+(base.indexOf('?')>=0?'&':'?')+
        'verify=1&doc='+encodeURIComponent(String(key||'marks'))+
        '&id='+encodeURIComponent(String(id||''));
      var old=card.querySelector('.jb-whitepage-link-tools');
      if(old)old.remove();
      var tools=document.createElement('div');
      tools.className='jb-whitepage-link-tools';
      tools.style.cssText='display:flex;gap:7px;justify-content:center;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:10px';
      var copy=document.createElement('button');
      copy.className='secondary';
      copy.textContent='🔗 যাচাই লিংক কপি';
      copy.onclick=function(){
        if(navigator.clipboard&&navigator.clipboard.writeText){
          navigator.clipboard.writeText(url).then(function(){copy.textContent='✅ লিংক কপি হয়েছে';});
        }else{
          var t=document.createElement('textarea');t.value=url;document.body.appendChild(t);t.select();
          try{document.execCommand('copy');copy.textContent='✅ লিংক কপি হয়েছে';}catch(e){}
          t.remove();
        }
      };
      tools.appendChild(copy);
      card.appendChild(tools);
    };
  }
})();
</script>

var CLASSES=['প্লে','শিশু','আউয়াল','ছানী','ছালেছ','রাবে','পঞ্চম','মিজান','নাহবেমীর','কুদুরী','শরহে বেকায়া','হেদায়া','মেশকাত','দাওরায়ে হাদীস'];
window.jbClassSubjects2026=CLASS_SUBJECTS;
function currentKey(){var t=document.getElementById('jbDocBuilderTitle');var s=t?t.textContent:'';return /ফলাফল/.test(s)?'result':/মার্কশিট/.test(s)?'marks':''}
function addGrid(){
 var host=document.getElementById('jbDocFields'); if(!host)return;
 var key=currentKey(); if(key!=='marks'&&key!=='result')return;
 var old=document.getElementById('jbMarksGrid2026');if(old)old.remove();
 host.querySelectorAll('.jb-doc-field').forEach(function(d){var x=d.querySelector('input[data-key]');if(x&&['Class','SubjectData','Total','GPA','Grade'].indexOf(x.dataset.key)>=0)d.style.display='none'});
 var box=document.createElement('div');box.id='jbMarksGrid2026';
 box.innerHTML='<div class="jb-ms-class"><span>শ্রেণি/জামাত নির্বাচন</span><select id="jbMsClass2026"><option value="">নির্বাচন করুন</option></select></div><div class="jb-ms-subjects" id="jbMsSubjects2026"></div>';
 host.parentNode.insertBefore(box,host.nextSibling);
 var cs=box.querySelector('#jbMsClass2026'); CLASSES.forEach(function(c){var o=document.createElement('option');o.value=c;o.textContent=c;cs.appendChild(o)});
 var oldClass=host.querySelector('input[data-key="Class"]');if(oldClass&&oldClass.value)cs.value=oldClass.value;
 function buildSubjects(){
   var sg=box.querySelector('#jbMsSubjects2026');sg.innerHTML='';
   (CLASS_SUBJECTS[cs.value]||[]).slice(0,12).forEach(function(s,n){
     var item=document.createElement('div');item.className='jb-ms-item';
     item.innerHTML='<label class="jb-ms-subject-check"><input type="checkbox" class="jb-ms-subject-toggle" checked><span>বিষয় '+(n+1)+'</span></label><select class="jb-ms-subject"><option value="'+String(s).replace(/"/g,'&quot;')+'">'+s+'</option></select><input type="number" min="0" max="100" step="1" class="jb-ms-mark" placeholder="নম্বর">';
     sg.appendChild(item);
   });
 }
 cs.onchange=function(){if(oldClass)oldClass.value=cs.value;buildSubjects()};
 buildSubjects();
}
var oldFind=window.jbFindStudent;
window.jbFindStudent=async function(){var r=await oldFind.apply(this,arguments);setTimeout(addGrid,80);return r};
var oldManual=window.jbManualEntry;
window.jbManualEntry=function(){var r=oldManual.apply(this,arguments);setTimeout(addGrid,80);return r};
var oldCreate=window.jbCreatePreview;
window.jbCreatePreview=function(){var box=document.getElementById('jbMarksGrid2026');if(box){var data=[];box.querySelectorAll('.jb-ms-item').forEach(function(it,n){var s=it.querySelector('.jb-ms-subject')?.value||'',m=it.querySelector('.jb-ms-mark')?.value||'',ok=it.querySelector('.jb-ms-subject-toggle')?.checked;if(ok&&(s||m))data.push({no:n+1,subject:s,marks:m});var c=box.querySelector('#jbMsClass2026')?.value||'',ci=document.querySelector('#jbDocFields input[data-key="Class"]');if(ci)ci.value=c;var sd=document.querySelector('#jbDocFields input[data-key="SubjectData"]');if(sd)sd.value=JSON.stringify(data)})}return oldCreate.apply(this,arguments)};
})();
</script>

  #jbDocBuilderHead .jb-win-control-2026{width:34px!important;min-width:34px!important}

<script id="marksheet-window-controls-2026-10-07-js">
(function(){
  var state={x:null,y:null,full:false,min:false,drag:false,sx:0,sy:0,ox:0,oy:0};
  function get(){return document.getElementById('jbDocBuilderWin')}
  function controls(w){
    if(!w||document.getElementById('jbDocWindowControls2026'))return;
    var head=document.getElementById('jbDocBuilderHead');if(!head)return;
    var close=head.querySelector('button');
    var box=document.createElement('div');box.id='jbDocWindowControls2026';box.className='jb-win-controls-2026';
    var min=document.createElement('button');min.type='button';min.className='jb-win-control-2026';min.title='মিনিমাইজ';min.textContent='—';
    var full=document.createElement('button');full.type='button';full.className='jb-win-control-2026';full.title='ফুল স্ক্রিন';full.textContent='⛶';
    box.appendChild(min);box.appendChild(full);
    if(close){head.insertBefore(box,close);close.title='বন্ধ'}else head.appendChild(box);
    min.onclick=function(e){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();toggleMin(w)};
    full.onclick=function(e){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();toggleFull(w)};
  }
  function savePos(w){
    var r=w.getBoundingClientRect();state.x=r.left;state.y=r.top;
  }
  function clamp(w,x,y){
    var maxX=Math.max(0,window.innerWidth-w.offsetWidth),maxY=Math.max(0,window.innerHeight-w.offsetHeight);
    return {x:Math.min(Math.max(0,x),maxX),y:Math.min(Math.max(0,y),maxY)};
  }
  function restorePosition(w){
    if(state.x==null||state.y==null)return;
    w.style.left=state.x+'px';w.style.top=state.y+'px';w.style.right='auto';w.style.bottom='auto';w.style.inset='auto';w.style.transform='none';
    var p=clamp(w,state.x,state.y);w.style.left=p.x+'px';w.style.top=p.y+'px';state.x=p.x;state.y=p.y;
  }
  function toggleMin(w){
    if(state.min){state.min=false;w.classList.remove('jb-window-min');if(state.full){w.classList.add('jb-window-full')}else restorePosition(w);return;}
    if(state.full){state.full=false;w.classList.remove('jb-window-full')}
    savePos(w);state.min=true;w.classList.add('jb-window-min');
  }
  function toggleFull(w){
    if(state.min){state.min=false;w.classList.remove('jb-window-min')}
    state.full=!state.full;
    w.classList.toggle('jb-window-full',state.full);
    if(!state.full)restorePosition(w);
  }
  function bindDrag(w){
    if(w.dataset.jbDragBound==='1')return;w.dataset.jbDragBound='1';
    var head=document.getElementById('jbDocBuilderHead');if(!head)return;
    head.addEventListener('pointerdown',function(e){
      if(e.button!==0)return;
      if(e.target.closest('button,input,select,textarea,a'))return;
      if(state.min){state.min=false;w.classList.remove('jb-window-min');state.full=false;w.classList.remove('jb-window-full')}
      if(state.full)return;
      var r=w.getBoundingClientRect();state.drag=true;state.sx=e.clientX;state.sy=e.clientY;state.ox=r.left;state.oy=r.top;
      w.classList.add('jb-window-dragging');w.setPointerCapture&&w.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);e.preventDefault();
    });
    head.addEventListener('pointermove',function(e){
      if(!state.drag)return;
      var p=clamp(w,state.ox+(e.clientX-state.sx),state.oy+(e.clientY-state.sy));
      w.style.left=p.x+'px';w.style.top=p.y+'px';w.style.right='auto';w.style.bottom='auto';w.style.inset='auto';w.style.transform='none';state.x=p.x;state.y=p.y;
    });
    function up(){state.drag=false;w.classList.remove('jb-window-dragging')}
    head.addEventListener('pointerup',up);head.addEventListener('pointercancel',up);
  }
  function ensure(){var w=get();if(!w)return;controls(w);bindDrag(w)}
  var oldOpen=window.jbOpenDocumentType2026;
  if(typeof oldOpen==='function')window.jbOpenDocumentType2026=function(){var r=oldOpen.apply(this,arguments);setTimeout(ensure,30);return r};
  var oldModule=window.openModule;
  if(typeof oldModule==='function')window.openModule=function(k){var r=oldModule.apply(this,arguments);setTimeout(ensure,30);return r};
  var oldCreate=window.jbCreatePreview;
  if(typeof oldCreate==='function')window.jbCreatePreview=function(){var r=oldCreate.apply(this,arguments);setTimeout(ensure,30);return r};
  window.addEventListener('resize',function(){var w=get();if(!w||!w.classList.contains('show')||state.full)return;if(state.min)return;var p=clamp(w,state.x==null?w.getBoundingClientRect().left:state.x,state.y==null?w.getBoundingClientRect().top:state.y);w.style.left=p.x+'px';w.style.top=p.y+'px';w.style.right='auto';w.style.bottom='auto';w.style.inset='auto';w.style.transform='none';state.x=p.x;state.y=p.y});
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',ensure);else ensure();
})();
</script>
<!-- ===== END MARKSHEET WINDOW CONTROLS ===== -->

<script id="all-documents-contact-header-2026-10-07-js">
(function(){
  var NAME='জান্নাতুল বাক্বী মহিলা মাদ্রাসা ও এতিমখানা';
  var ADDRESS='উপজেলা মডেল টাউন কোনাখোলা, কেরাণীগঞ্জ, ঢাকা-১৩১০';

  function esc(v){
    return String(v==null?'':v).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
  }
  function logoSrc(){
    var x=document.getElementById('contactLoginLogo2026')||document.getElementById('loginLogo')||document.getElementById('headerLogo');
    return x&&x.src?x.src:'';
  }
  function qrSrc(){
    var x=document.getElementById('contactMainQR2026');
    if(x&&x.src)return x.src;
    var base=String(window.location.href||'').split('#')[0];
    return 'https://quickchart.io/qr?size=220&margin=2&text='+encodeURIComponent(base);
  }
  function removeOldInstitutionLines(p){
    var nodes=Array.prototype.slice.call(p.querySelectorAll('h1,h2,h3,p,div,span'));
    nodes.forEach(function(n){
      if(n.dataset&&n.dataset.jbKeepInstitution==='1')return;
      var t=(n.textContent||'').trim();
      if((t===NAME||t===ADDRESS)&&n.parentNode){
        n.remove();
      }
    });
  }
  function addHeader(p){
    if(!p||p.dataset.jbContactHeader2026==='1')return;
    p.dataset.jbContactHeader2026='1';

    var h=document.createElement('div');
    h.className='jb-institution-header-2026';
    h.innerHTML=
      '<div class="jb-inst-side-2026"><img alt="মাদ্রাসার লোগো" src="'+esc(logoSrc())+'"></div>'+
      '<div class="jb-inst-center-2026">'+
        '<div class="jb-inst-name-2026" data-jb-keep-institution="1">'+NAME+'</div>'+
        '<div class="jb-inst-address-2026" data-jb-keep-institution="1">'+ADDRESS+'</div>'+
      '</div>'+
      '<div class="jb-inst-side-2026"><img alt="মূল পেজের QR কোড" src="'+esc(qrSrc())+'"></div>';
    p.insertBefore(h,p.firstChild);
    removeOldInstitutionLines(p);
  }
  function scan(){
    document.querySelectorAll('#jbDocPreviewWrap .jb-doc-preview').forEach(addHeader);
  }
  var oldCreate=window.jbCreatePreview;
  if(typeof oldCreate==='function'){
    window.jbCreatePreview=function(){
      var r=oldCreate.apply(this,arguments);
      setTimeout(scan,30);
      setTimeout(scan,250);
      return r;
    };
  }
  var obs=new MutationObserver(function(){scan()});
  if(document.body)obs.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',scan);else scan();
  setTimeout(scan,500);
})();
</script>
<!-- ===== END ALL DOCUMENTS CONTACT HEADER ===== -->

<!-- ===== DOCUMENT BUILDER — NON-MARKS CARD PREVIEW FIX — 2026-10-07 ===== -->
<script id="document-builder-non-marks-card-preview-fix-2026-10-07">
(function(){
  var oldCreate=window.jbCreatePreview;
  if(typeof oldCreate!=='function')return;

  function key(){
    var t=document.getElementById('jbDocBuilderTitle')?.textContent||'';
    return t.indexOf('ফলাফল')>=0?'result':
      t.indexOf('মার্কশিট')>=0?'marks':
      t.indexOf('আইডি কার্ড')>=0?'id_cards':
      t.indexOf('অ্যাডমিট কার্ড')>=0?'admit_cards':
      t.indexOf('সার্টিফিকেট')>=0?'certificates':
      t.indexOf('মানি রিসিট')>=0?'receipts':'marks';
  }
  function e(v){
    return String(v==null?'':v).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
  }
  function v(k){
    var x=document.querySelector('#jbDocFields [data-key="'+k+'"]');
    return x?String(x.value||'').trim():'';
  }
  function checked(k){
    var x=document.querySelector('#jbDocFields [data-key="'+k+'"]');
    var box=x&&x.parentElement?x.parentElement.querySelector('input[type="checkbox"]'):null;
    return !box||box.checked;
  }
  function title(k){
    return k==='id_cards'?'আইডি কার্ড':
      k==='admit_cards'?'অ্যাডমিট কার্ড':
      k==='certificates'?'সার্টিফিকেট':
      k==='receipts'?'মানি রিসিট':
      k==='result'?'ফলাফল / মার্কশিট':'মার্কশিট';
  }
  function make(){
    var k=key();
    if(k==='marks'||k==='result')return oldCreate.apply(this,arguments);

    var wrap=document.getElementById('jbDocPreviewWrap');
    if(!wrap)return;
    var sid=v('StudentID')||document.getElementById('jbDocId')?.value||'';
    var name=v('NameBN'), photo=v('Photo'), cls=v('Class'), roll=v('Roll'), mobile=v('Mobile');
    var rows=[],defs=[
      ['StudentID','ID'],['NameBN','নাম'],['NameAR','আরবি নাম'],['NameEN','ইংরেজি নাম'],
      ['FatherBN','পিতার নাম'],['MotherBN','মাতার নাম'],['DOB','জন্ম তারিখ'],['Mobile','মোবাইল'],
      ['WhatsApp','WhatsApp'],['Address','ঠিকানা'],['Division','বিভাগ'],['District','জেলা'],
      ['Upazila','উপজেলা'],['Union','ইউনিয়ন'],['Post','ডাকঘর'],['PostCode','পোস্ট কোড'],
      ['Ward','ওয়ার্ড'],['Village','গ্রাম'],['Class','শ্রেণি/জামাত'],['Department','বিভাগ/ডিপার্টমেন্ট'],
      ['Branch','শাখা'],['Roll','রোল'],['BirthReg','জন্ম নিবন্ধন'],['NID','NID'],
      ['Exam','পরীক্ষা'],['ExamYear','পরীক্ষা সাল'],['ExamDate','পরীক্ষার তারিখ'],
      ['ExamTime','সময়'],['Center','পরীক্ষা কেন্দ্র'],['CertificateNo','সনদ নম্বর'],
      ['IssueDate','প্রদানের তারিখ'],['Result','ফলাফল'],['Signature','স্বাক্ষর'],['Seal','সিল'],
      ['ReceiptNo','রিসিট নম্বর'],['Month','মাস/সময়'],['Category','খাত/কারণ'],
      ['Amount','টাকার পরিমাণ'],['Method','পরিশোধের মাধ্যম'],['Date','তারিখ'],['Note','বিবরণ'],
      ['EmergencyContact','জরুরি যোগাযোগ'],['Guardian','অভিভাবক']
    ];
    defs.forEach(function(d){
      var val=v(d[0]);
      if(val&&checked(d[0]))rows.push('<div class="jb-doc-row"><b>'+e(d[1])+'</b><span>'+e(val)+'</span></div>');
    });

    var extra='';
    if(k==='admit_cards'){
      var subs=v('SubjectData');
      if(subs)extra='<div style="margin-top:8px;border:1px solid #555;padding:7px"><b>বিষয়সমূহ:</b><div style="white-space:pre-wrap;margin-top:4px">'+e(subs)+'</div></div>';
    }
    if(k==='certificates'){
      extra='<div style="margin-top:24px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:20px;font-size:12px"><span>স্বাক্ষর</span><span>সিল</span><span>প্রধান শিক্ষিকা / মুহতামিম</span></div>';
    }
    if(k==='receipts'){
      extra='<div style="margin-top:10px;border:2px solid #222;padding:8px;text-align:center;font-size:16px"><b>টাকার পরিমাণ: '+e(v('Amount'))+'</b></div>';
    }

    var size=k==='id_cards'?'idcard':k==='receipts'?'half':'a4';
    var p=document.createElement('div');
    p.className='jb-doc-preview '+size;
    p.style.cssText='box-sizing:border-box;background:#fff;color:#111;position:relative;overflow:hidden;font-family:"Noto Sans Bengali","Noto Sans",Arial,sans-serif';

    var photoHtml=photo?'<img class="jb-doc-photo" src="'+e(photo)+'" alt="ছবি">':'';
    var head='<div class="jb-doc-head"><h2>'+e(title(k))+'</h2><p>'+e(name||'')+'</p></div>';
    if(k==='id_cards'){
      head='<div class="jb-doc-head"><h2>আইডি কার্ড</h2><p style="font-weight:900">'+e(name||'')+'</p></div>';
      p.innerHTML=photoHtml+head+
        '<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:3px">'+rows.join('')+'</div>'+
        '<div style="margin-top:8px;text-align:center;font-weight:900">জান্নাতুল বাক্বী মহিলা মাদ্রাসা ও এতিমখানা</div>';
    }else{
      p.innerHTML=photoHtml+head+
        '<div style="text-align:center;font-size:11px;margin-bottom:7px">উপজেলা মডেল টাউন কোনাখোলা, কেরাণীগঞ্জ, ঢাকা-১৩১০</div>'+
        rows.join('')+extra;
    }

    wrap.innerHTML='';
    var bar=document.createElement('div');bar.className='jb-doc-actions';
    bar.innerHTML='<button class="secondary" onclick="jbEditPreview()">✏️ এডিট</button><button class="secondary" onclick="jbSaveDraft()">💾 সেভ + History</button><button class="secondary" onclick="jbShowHistory()">📚 History</button><button class="secondary" onclick="jbDownload()">⬇️ ডাউনলোড</button><button class="primary" onclick="jbPrint()">🖨️ প্রিন্ট</button>';
    p.appendChild(bar);wrap.appendChild(p);
  }
  window.jbCreatePreview=make;
})();
</script>

<script id="document-builder-narrow-info-window-2026-10-07-js">
(function(){
  function addBack(){
    var title=document.getElementById('jbDocBuilderTitle');
    if(!title)return;
    var t=title.textContent||'';
    if(t.indexOf('মার্কশিট')<0 && t.indexOf('ফলাফল')<0)return;
    var wrap=document.getElementById('jbDocPreviewWrap');
    if(!wrap)return;
    var p=wrap.querySelector('.jb-doc-preview');
    if(!p)return;
    if(p.querySelector('.jb-marksheet-back-2026'))return;
    var b=document.createElement('button');
    b.type='button';
    b.className='jb-marksheet-back-2026';
    b.textContent='↩️';b.title='পূর্বে ফিরে যান';b.setAttribute('aria-label','পূর্বে ফিরে যান');
    b.onclick=function(){
      wrap.innerHTML='';
      var body=document.getElementById('jbDocBuilderBody');
      if(body)body.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'start'});
      var id=document.getElementById('jbDocId');
      if(id)setTimeout(function(){id.focus()},250);
    };
    var bar=p.querySelector('.jb-doc-actions');
    if(bar)bar.appendChild(b);else p.appendChild(b);
  }
  var old=window.jbCreatePreview;
  if(typeof old==='function'){
    window.jbCreatePreview=function(){
      var r=old.apply(this,arguments);
      setTimeout(addBack,60);
      return r;
    };
  }
  document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
    if(e.target && e.target.id==='jbDocBuilderTitle')return;
    setTimeout(addBack,80);
  });
})();
</script>
<!-- ===== END NARROW ID INFO WINDOW + MARKSHEET BACK BUTTON ===== -->

<script id="document-builder-direct-print-window-fix-2026-10-07">
(function(){
  var oldPrint=window.jbPrint;
  if(typeof oldPrint!=='function')return;
  window.jbPrint=function(){
    var p=document.getElementById('jbDocPreviewWrap')?.querySelector('.jb-doc-preview');
    if(!p)return toast('আগে Document তৈরি করুন।');
    var w=window.open('','_blank','width=1100,height=900');
    if(!w)return toast('Print window খোলা যায়নি। Browser popup অনুমতি দিন।');
    var title=String((window.currentDoc&&window.currentDoc.title)||document.getElementById('jbDocBuilderTitle')?.textContent||'Document')
      .replace(/[<>&"]/g,'');
    w.document.open();
    w.document.write('<!doctype html><html lang="bn"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>'+title+'</title><style>'+
      '@page{size:A4 portrait;margin:8mm}'+

      '.jb-institution-header-2026 .jb-inst-name-2026{font-size:16px!important;font-weight:900!important;margin:0 0 2px!important}'+
      '.jb-institution-header-2026 .jb-inst-address-2026{font-size:10px!important;font-weight:800!important;margin:0!important}'+

      '.jb-doc-head h2,.jb-doc-head h3,.jb-doc-head p{margin:3px}'+

<script id="jb-exam-routine-js-2026">
(function(){
  var KEY='jb_exam_routine_2026';
  var $r=function(id){return document.getElementById(id)};
  var CLASSES=['প্লে শ্রেনি','শিশু শ্রেনি','আউয়াল জামাত','ছানী জামাত','ছালেছ জামাত','রাবে জামাত','পঞ্চম জামাত','মিজান জামাত','নাহবেমীর জামাত','কুদুরী জামাত','শরহে বেকায়া জামাত','হেদায়া জামাত','মিশকাত জামাত','দাওরায়ে হাদিস (মাস্টার্স-তাকমিল)'];
  function esc(v){return String(v==null?'':v).replace(/[&<>"']/g,function(m){return({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'})[m]})}
  function data(){try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY)||'[]')}catch(e){return[]}}
  function save(a){localStorage.setItem(KEY,JSON.stringify(a))}
  function values(){return {exam:$r('jbRtExam').value.trim(),cls:$r('jbRtClass').value,subject:$r('jbRtSubject').value.trim(),date:$r('jbRtDate').value,start:$r('jbRtStart').value,end:$r('jbRtEnd').value,room:$r('jbRtRoom').value.trim(),teacher:$r('jbRtTeacher').value.trim()}}
  function render(){
    var a=data(),p=$r('jbRtPreview');if(!p)return;
    if(!a.length){p.innerHTML='<div style="text-align:center">রুটিনের পূর্বরূপ এখানে দেখা যাবে</div>';return}
    a.sort(function(x,y){return String(x.date+' '+x.start).localeCompare(String(y.date+' '+y.start))});
    var rows=a.map(function(x,i){return '<tr><td>'+(i+1)+'</td><td>'+esc(x.cls)+'</td><td>'+esc(x.subject)+'</td><td>'+esc(x.date)+'</td><td>'+esc(x.start)+' - '+esc(x.end)+'</td><td>'+esc(x.room)+'</td><td>'+esc(x.teacher)+'</td><td><button onclick="jbRtDelete('+x.id+')">🗑️</button></td></tr>'}).join('');
    p.innerHTML='<h2>জান্নাতুল বাক্বী মহিলা মাদ্রাসা ও এতিমখানা</h2><h3>'+esc((a[0]&&a[0].exam)||'পরীক্ষার রুটিন')+'</h3><table><thead><tr><th>ক্রম</th><th>শ্রেণি/জামাত</th><th>বিষয়</th><th>তারিখ</th><th>সময়</th><th>কক্ষ/কেন্দ্র</th><th>পরীক্ষক</th><th>কার্যক্রম</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+rows+'</tbody></table>';
  }
  window.jbRtAdd=function(){
    var v=values();
    if(!v.exam||!v.cls||!v.subject||!v.date||!v.start){toast('পরীক্ষার নাম, শ্রেণি/জামাত, বিষয়, তারিখ ও শুরু সময় দিন।');return}
    if(v.end&&v.end<=v.start){toast('শেষ সময় শুরু সময়ের পরে হতে হবে।');return}
    var a=data();a.push({id:Date.now(),...v});save(a);render();toast('রুটিনে যোগ হয়েছে।');
  };
  window.jbRtDelete=function(id){save(data().filter(function(x){return x.id!==id}));render();toast('রুটিনের এন্ট্রি মুছে দেওয়া হয়েছে।')};
  window.jbRtPreview=render;
  window.jbRtClear=function(){['jbRtExam','jbRtClass','jbRtSubject','jbRtDate','jbRtStart','jbRtEnd','jbRtRoom','jbRtTeacher'].forEach(function(id){var x=$r(id);if(x)x.value=''});render()};
  window.jbRtPrint=function(){render();var p=$r('jbRtPreview');var w=window.open('','_blank');if(!w){toast('প্রিন্ট উইন্ডো খোলা যাচ্ছে না।');return}w.document.write('<html><head><title>পরীক্ষার রুটিন</title><style>body{font-family:Arial,sans-serif;padding:20px;color:#111}table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse}th,td{border:1px solid #555;padding:7px;text-align:center}button{display:none}</style></head><body>'+p.innerHTML+'</body></html>');w.document.close();setTimeout(function(){w.focus();w.print()},300)};
  window.openExamRoutine2026=function(){openWindow('jbExamRoutineWin2026');render()};
  window.examComing=window.examComing||function(n){if(String(n).indexOf('প্রশ্নপত্র')>-1){openWindow('jbQuestionPaperWin2026');return}if(String(n).indexOf('পরীক্ষার রুটিন')>-1){openWindow('jbExamRoutineWin2026');render();return}toast(n+' মডিউল পরবর্তী ধাপে যুক্ত হবে।')};
  var oldComing=window.examComing;
  window.examComing=function(n){var s=String(n||'');if(s.indexOf('পরীক্ষার্থী')>-1){if(typeof window.openModule==='function'){window.openModule('exam');}return}if(s.indexOf('পরীক্ষার রুটিন')>-1){openWindow('jbExamRoutineWin2026');render();return}return oldComing(n)};
})();
</script>
<!-- ===== END EXAM ROUTINE MODULE 2026 ===== -->
</body></html>');
    w.document.close();
    setTimeout(function(){try{w.focus();w.print()}catch(e){}},500);
  };
})();
</script>

    executive_form:[['MemberNo','সদস্য নম্বর'],['Position','পদ/দায়িত্ব'],['Term','মেয়াদ'],['Profession','পেশা'],['MeetingDate','সভা/নির্ধারণের তারিখ'],['Decision','সিদ্ধান্ত/মন্তব্য'],['MemberSignature','সদস্যের স্বাক্ষর']],
    executive_id:[['MemberNo','সদস্য নম্বর'],['Position','পদ/দায়িত্ব'],['Term','মেয়াদ'],['MeetingDate','ইস্যুর তারিখ']]
  };
  var TITLES={};TYPES.forEach(function(x){TITLES[x[0]]=x[1].replace(/^\\S+\\s*/,'').replace(' তৈরি','')});
  var NEW={};['teacher_id','admission_form','leave_form','exam_routine','tc','attestation','lifetime_member_form','lifetime_member_certificate','teacher_recruitment_form','teacher_appointment','executive_form','executive_id'].forEach(function(k){NEW[k]=1});

  }

  function addFields(k){

    defs.forEach(function(d){

    });
  }

  function fieldRows(k){

  function makePreview(){

    }else{
      p.innerHTML='<div class="jb-doc-head"><h2>'+esc(titleText)+'</h2><p>জান্নাতুল বাক্বী মহিলা মাদ্রাসা ও এতিমখানা</p><p style="font-size:10px">উপজেলা মডেল টাউন কোনাখোলা, কেরাণীগঞ্জ, ঢাকা-১৩১০</p></div>'+intro+table+'<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;margin-top:28px;font-size:11px"><span>আবেদনকারী/সদস্য</span><span>যাচাইকারী</span><span>প্রধান শিক্ষিকা/মুহতামিম</span></div>';

<script id="jb-doc-final-window-nav-contact-qr-layout-js-2026-10-07">
(function(){
  var ORDER=[
    'marks','result','id_cards','admit_cards','certificates','receipts',
    'teacher_id','admission_form','leave_form','exam_routine','tc','attestation',
    'lifetime_member_form','lifetime_member_certificate',
    'teacher_recruitment_form','teacher_appointment','executive_form','executive_id'
  ];

  function currentKey(){
    if(typeof window.jbDocCurrentKey==='function'){
      var k=window.jbDocCurrentKey();
      if(k&&ORDER.indexOf(k)>=0)return k;
    }
    var t=(document.getElementById('jbDocBuilderTitle')||{}).textContent||'';
    var map={
      'মার্কশিট':'marks','ফলাফল / মার্কশিট':'result','আইডি কার্ড':'id_cards',
      'অ্যাডমিট কার্ড':'admit_cards','সার্টিফিকেট':'certificates','মানি রিসিট':'receipts',
      'শিক্ষক/শিক্ষিকা আইডি কার্ড':'teacher_id','ভর্তি আবেদন ফর্ম':'admission_form',
      'ছুটির আবেদন ফর্ম':'leave_form','পরীক্ষার রুটিন':'exam_routine','টিসি / ছাড়পত্র':'tc',
      'প্রত্যয়ন পত্র':'attestation','আজীবন সদস্য ফর্ম':'lifetime_member_form',
      'আজীবন সদস্য সনদ':'lifetime_member_certificate','শিক্ষক/শিক্ষিকা নিয়োগ আবেদন':'teacher_recruitment_form',
      'শিক্ষক/শিক্ষিকা নিয়োগ পত্র':'teacher_appointment','নির্বাহী পরিষদ ফর্ম':'executive_form',
      'নির্বাহী পরিষদ সদস্য আইডি কার্ড':'executive_id'
    };
    for(var name in map)if(t.indexOf(name)>=0)return map[name];
    return '';
  }

  function addNav(){
    var head=document.getElementById('jbDocBuilderHead');
    if(!head||document.getElementById('jbDocNav2026'))return;
    var close=head.querySelector('button');
    var box=document.createElement('div');
    box.id='jbDocNav2026';
    box.className='jb-doc-nav-2026';

    var prev=document.createElement('button');
    prev.type='button';
    prev.className='secondary';
    prev.textContent='◀ পূর্বে';
    prev.title='পূর্বের Document';
    prev.onclick=function(e){
      e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
      var k=currentKey(),i=ORDER.indexOf(k);
      if(i<0)i=0;
      var target=ORDER[(i-1+ORDER.length)%ORDER.length];
      if(typeof window.jbOpenDocumentType2026==='function')window.jbOpenDocumentType2026(target);
    };

    var next=document.createElement('button');
    next.type='button';
    next.className='secondary';
    next.textContent='পরে ▶';
    next.title='পরের Document';
    next.onclick=function(e){
      e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
      var k=currentKey(),i=ORDER.indexOf(k);
      if(i<0)i=-1;
      var target=ORDER[(i+1)%ORDER.length];
      if(typeof window.jbOpenDocumentType2026==='function')window.jbOpenDocumentType2026(target);
    };

    box.appendChild(prev);box.appendChild(next);
    if(close)head.insertBefore(box,close);else head.appendChild(box);
  }

  function addContact(p){
    if(!p||p.querySelector('.jb-institution-contact-2026'))return;
    var address=p.querySelector('.jb-inst-address-2026');
    if(!address)return;
    var c=document.createElement('div');
    c.className='jb-institution-contact-2026';
    c.innerHTML='<span class="jb-hotline-2026">হটলাইন-01823316630</span><span class="jb-email-2026">ইমেইল-hmatik90@gmail.com</span>';
    address.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',c);
  }

  function moveQRBeforeActions(p){
    if(!p)return;
    var qr=p.querySelector('.jb-verification-qr');
    var bar=p.querySelector(':scope > .jb-doc-actions');
    if(qr&&bar&&bar.previousElementSibling!==qr)bar.parentNode.insertBefore(qr,bar);

    if(qr){
      qr.style.position='absolute';
      qr.style.left='50%';
      qr.style.transform='translateX(-50%)';
    }
  }

  function patchQR(){
    var old=window.jbAddVerificationQR;
    if(typeof old!=='function'||old.__jbFinalLayout2026)return;
    function wrapped(key,id){
      var r=old.apply(this,arguments);
      setTimeout(function(){
        var p=document.querySelector('#jbDocPreviewWrap .jb-doc-preview');
        if(p)moveQRBeforeActions(p);
      },0);
      setTimeout(function(){
        var p=document.querySelector('#jbDocPreviewWrap .jb-doc-preview');
        if(p)moveQRBeforeActions(p);
      },120);
      return r;
    }
    wrapped.__jbFinalLayout2026=true;
    window.jbAddVerificationQR=wrapped;
    window.jbRenderSavedVerificationQR2026=wrapped;
  }

  function scan(){
    addNav();
    document.querySelectorAll('#jbDocPreviewWrap .jb-doc-preview').forEach(function(p){
      addContact(p);
      moveQRBeforeActions(p);
    });
    patchQR();
  }

  var oldOpen=window.jbOpenDocumentType2026;
  if(typeof oldOpen==='function'&&!oldOpen.__jbFinalNav2026){
    function openWrapped(){
      var r=oldOpen.apply(this,arguments);
      setTimeout(scan,30);
      setTimeout(scan,150);
      return r;
    }
    openWrapped.__jbFinalNav2026=true;
    window.jbOpenDocumentType2026=openWrapped;
  }

  var oldCreate=window.jbCreatePreview;
  if(typeof oldCreate==='function'&&!oldCreate.__jbFinalNav2026){
    function createWrapped(){
      var r=oldCreate.apply(this,arguments);
      setTimeout(scan,30);
      setTimeout(scan,150);
      return r;
    }
    createWrapped.__jbFinalNav2026=true;
    window.jbCreatePreview=createWrapped;
  }

  addNav();
  scan();
  if(document.body){
    new MutationObserver(function(){scan()}).observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  }
})();
</script>

<script id="jb-existing-online-result-2026">
(function(){
  function E(id){return document.getElementById(id)}
  function esc(v){return String(v==null?'':v).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;')}
  function findSignal(list){
    var a=list||[];
    for(var i=0;i<a.length;i++){
      var code=String(a[i]&&a[i][0]||'').toUpperCase(), label=String(a[i]&&a[i][1]||'');
      if(code==='RS'||/ফলাফল|রেজাল্ট|result/i.test(label))return code;
    }
    return 'RS';
  }
  function renderRows(rows,q){
    var box=E('jbOnlineResultBody2026'); if(!box)return;
    if(!rows||!rows.length){box.innerHTML='<div class="jb-or-empty">❌ '+esc(q)+' এর কোনো প্রকাশিত ফলাফল পাওয়া যায়নি।</div>';return}
    var r=rows[0], subjects={};
    try{subjects=JSON.parse(String(r.SubjectData||r.Subjects||r.Marks||'{}'))||{}}catch(e){}
    var total=r.Total||r.TotalMarks||r.MarksTotal||'';

  };

(function(){
  function render(){

      var a=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('jb_exam_dashboard_drafts_2026')||'[]');

      var pub=document.getElementById('examStatPublished');

      if(pub)pub.textContent=String(a.filter(function(x){return x&&x.published}).length);
      if(draft)draft.textContent=String(a.filter(function(x){return !x||!x.published}).length);
    }catch(e){}
  }
  window.openExamDashboard=function(){

    if(!w){try{toast('পরীক্ষা ড্যাশবোর্ড পাওয়া যাচ্ছে না।')}catch(e){}return;}

    render();
  };

      window.openExamDashboard();
    };
  }
  bind();
  if(document.body)new MutationObserver(bind).observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();

  function escResult(r){return r;}

<script id="jb-login-online-payment-2026-js">
(function(){
  function add(){
    var lp=document.getElementById('loginPage');
    if(!lp||document.getElementById('jbLoginOnlinePayment2026'))return;
    var b=document.createElement('button');
    b.id='jbLoginOnlinePayment2026';b.type='button';
    b.textContent='💳 অনলাইন পেমেন্ট — লগইন ছাড়াই';
    b.onclick=function(){
      var w=window.open('about:blank','_blank');
      if(!w){alert('নতুন পেমেন্ট উইন্ডো খোলা যাচ্ছে না। Browser popup অনুমতি দিন।');return;}
      try{
        if(typeof google!=='undefined'&&google.script&&google.script.run){
          google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(r){
            var u=String(r&&r.ok&&r.url?r.url:location.href).split('#')[0].split('?')[0];
            w.location.href=u+'?payment=1';
          }).withFailureHandler(function(){
            w.location.href=location.href.split('#')[0].split('?')[0]+'?payment=1';
          }).getMainWebAppUrl();
        }else{
          w.location.href=location.href.split('#')[0].split('?')[0]+'?payment=1';
        }
      }catch(e){w.location.href=location.href.split('#')[0].split('?')[0]+'?payment=1';}
    };
    var q=lp.querySelector('.quick');
    if(q)q.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',b);else lp.appendChild(b);
  }
  add();
  if(document.body)new MutationObserver(add).observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>

<script id="jb-login-service-payment-row-2026-js">
(function(){
  function arrange(){
    var lp=document.getElementById('loginPage'),
        panel=lp&&lp.querySelector('.login'),
        p=document.getElementById('jbLoginOnlinePayment2026'),
        s=document.getElementById('jbLoginOnlineService2026');
    if(!lp||!p||!s)return;
    panel=panel||lp;
    var row=document.getElementById('jbLoginServicePaymentRow2026');
    if(!row){
      row=document.createElement('div');
      row.id='jbLoginServicePaymentRow2026';
      row.className='jb-login-service-payment-row-2026';
    }
    if(row.parentNode!==panel)panel.appendChild(row);
    if(s.parentNode!==row)row.appendChild(s);
    if(p.parentNode!==row)row.appendChild(p);
    row.style.position='relative';
    row.style.left='auto';
    row.style.right='auto';
    row.style.transform='none';
    row.style.marginLeft='auto';
    row.style.marginRight='auto';
  }
  arrange();
  if(document.body)new MutationObserver(arrange).observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();

<script id="jb-online-service-home-2026-10-07-js">
(function(){
  var CODES={
    marks:'MS',result:'RS',id_cards:'ID',admit_cards:'AC',certificates:'CT',receipts:'RC',
    teacher_id:'TI',admission_form:'AF',leave_form:'LF',exam_routine:'ER',tc:'TC',attestation:'AP',
    lifetime_member_form:'LM',lifetime_member_certificate:'LC',teacher_recruitment_form:'TR',
    teacher_appointment:'TA',executive_form:'EF',executive_id:'EI'
  };
  function key(){
    if(typeof window.__jbAdditionalDocKey==='string'&&CODES[window.__jbAdditionalDocKey])return window.__jbAdditionalDocKey;
    var t=(document.getElementById('jbDocBuilderTitle')||{}).textContent||'';
    var map={
      'ফলাফল / মার্কশিট':'result','মার্কশিট':'marks','আইডি কার্ড':'id_cards','অ্যাডমিট কার্ড':'admit_cards',
      'সার্টিফিকেট':'certificates','মানি রিসিট':'receipts','শিক্ষক/শিক্ষিকা আইডি কার্ড':'teacher_id',
      'ভর্তি আবেদন ফর্ম':'admission_form','ছুটির আবেদন ফর্ম':'leave_form','পরীক্ষার রুটিন':'exam_routine',
      'টিসি / ছাড়পত্র':'tc','প্রত্যয়ন পত্র':'attestation','আজীবন সদস্য ফর্ম':'lifetime_member_form',
      'আজীবন সদস্য সনদ':'lifetime_member_certificate','শিক্ষক/শিক্ষিকা নিয়োগ আবেদন':'teacher_recruitment_form',
      'শিক্ষক/শিক্ষিকা নিয়োগ পত্র':'teacher_appointment','নির্বাহী পরিষদ ফর্ম':'executive_form',
      'নির্বাহী পরিষদ সদস্য আইডি কার্ড':'executive_id'
    };
    for(var n in map)if(t.indexOf(n)>=0)return map[n];return 'marks';
  }
  function addOnlineButton(){
    document.querySelectorAll('#jbDocPreviewWrap .jb-doc-preview').forEach(function(p){
      var wrap=p.parentNode;
      if(!wrap||wrap.querySelector('.jb-online-service-save-2026'))return;
      var b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.className='jb-online-service-save-2026';
      b.textContent='🌐 অনলাইন সেবা ঘরে বসেই গ্রহন করুন';
      b.onclick=async function(){
        var id=(document.getElementById('jbDocId')?.value||'').trim();
        if(!id)return toast('ID প্রয়োজন।');
        var data={};document.querySelectorAll('#jbDocFields [data-key]').forEach(function(x){data[x.dataset.key]=x.value||''});
        var k=key();
        try{
          b.disabled=true;b.textContent='⏳ অনলাইন সেবায় সংরক্ষণ হচ্ছে...';
          var r=await call('saveOnlineServiceRecord',token,k,id,data,p.outerHTML);
          if(r&&r.ok){
            b.textContent='✅ অনলাইন সেবায় সংরক্ষিত';
            toast('🌐 '+r.message+' সংকেত: '+r.serviceCode+' | ID: '+r.personId);
            if(r.publicUrl)setTimeout(function(){try{window.open(r.publicUrl,'_blank')}catch(e){}},250);
          }else{
            b.disabled=false;b.textContent='🌐 অনলাইন সেবা ঘরে বসেই গ্রহন করুন';
            toast((r&&r.message)||'অনলাইন সেবায় সংরক্ষণ হয়নি।');
          }
        }catch(e){
          b.disabled=false;b.textContent='🌐 অনলাইন সেবা ঘরে বসেই গ্রহন করুন';
          toast('অনলাইন সেবা সংরক্ষণে ত্রুটি: '+(e&&e.message?e.message:e));
        }
      };
      var bar=p.querySelector('.jb-doc-actions');
      if(bar)bar.appendChild(b);else wrap.appendChild(b);
    });
  }
  function addLoginButton(){
    var lp=document.getElementById('loginPage');if(!lp||document.getElementById('jbLoginOnlineService2026'))return;
    var b=document.createElement('button');b.id='jbLoginOnlineService2026';b.type='button';
    b.textContent='🌐 অনলাইন সেবা ঘরে বসেই গ্রহন করুন';
    b.className='primary jb-login-online-service-2026';
    b.onclick=function(){var w=window.open('about:blank','_blank');if(!w){alert('নতুন সেবা উইন্ডো খোলা যাচ্ছে না। Browser popup অনুমতি দিন।');return;}try{if(typeof google!=='undefined'&&google.script&&google.script.run){google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(function(r){var u=String(r&&r.ok&&r.url?r.url:location.href).split('#')[0].split('?')[0];w.location.href=u+'?online=1';}).withFailureHandler(function(){w.location.href=location.href.split('#')[0].split('?')[0]+'?online=1';}).getMainWebAppUrl();}else{w.location.href=location.href.split('#')[0].split('?')[0]+'?online=1';}}catch(e){w.location.href=location.href.split('#')[0].split('?')[0]+'?online=1';}};
    var q=lp.querySelector('.quick');
    if(q)q.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',b);else lp.appendChild(b);
  }
  function scan(){addOnlineButton();addLoginButton()}
  scan();
  if(document.body)new MutationObserver(scan).observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
})();
</script>

<!-- ===== ONLINE SERVICE PUBLIC SEARCH — SIGNAL + ID DIRECT VIEW — 2026-10-07 ===== -->
<script id="jb-online-service-public-search-2026-10-07">
(function(){
  var CODES={TI:1,AF:1,LF:1,ER:1,TC:1,AP:1,LM:1,LC:1,TR:1,TA:1,EF:1,EI:1};
  function E(id){return document.getElementById(id)}
  function openOnline(sig,q){
    var base='';
    try{
      if(typeof call==='function'){
        call('getMainWebAppUrl').then(function(r){
          base=String(r&&r.ok&&r.url?r.url:window.location.href).split('#')[0].split('?')[0];
          window.open(base+'?online=1&code='+encodeURIComponent(sig)+'&id='+encodeURIComponent(q),'_blank');
        }).catch(function(){
          base=window.location.href.split('#')[0].split('?')[0];
          window.open(base+'?online=1&code='+encodeURIComponent(sig)+'&id='+encodeURIComponent(q),'_blank');
        });
        return;
      }
    }catch(e){}
    base=window.location.href.split('#')[0].split('?')[0];
    window.open(base+'?online=1&code='+encodeURIComponent(sig)+'&id='+encodeURIComponent(q),'_blank');
  }
  var old=window.doSearch;
  if(typeof old!=='function')return;
  window.doSearch=function(where){
    var se=E(where==='login'?'loginSignal':'appSignal');
    var inp=E(where==='login'?'loginSearch':'appSearch');
    var sig=String(se&&se.value||'').trim().toUpperCase();
    var q=String(inp&&inp.value||'').trim();
    if(CODES[sig]){
      if(!q)return toast('সংকেত নির্বাচন করে ID দিন।');
      openOnline(sig,q);
      return;
    }
    return old.apply(this,arguments);
  };
})();
</script>
<!-- ===== END ONLINE SERVICE PUBLIC SEARCH ===== -->

  }
  function bindModule(){
    try{
      if(!oldModuleForm && typeof moduleForm==='function')oldModuleForm=moduleForm;
      if(typeof oldModuleForm!=='function')return;
      window.moduleForm=function(k){
        if(k==='sms')return smsPortalHtml();
        return oldModuleForm.apply(this,arguments);
      };
    }catch(e){}
  }
  function rows(){return window.__jbSmsRows2026||[]}
  function filtered(){
    var type=(document.getElementById('jbSmsType2026')||{}).value||'ALL',q=((document.getElementById('jbSmsSearch2026')||{}).value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
    return rows().filter(function(x){return (type==='ALL'||x.type===type)&&(!q||[x.id,x.name,x.mobile,x.whatsapp,x.email].join(' ').toLowerCase().indexOf(q)>=0)});
  }
  function selected(){return rows().filter(function(x){var e=document.querySelector('[data-jb-sms-check="'+CSS.escape(String(x.source)+'|'+String(x.id))+'"]');return e&&e.checked})}
  window.jbSmsFilter2026=function(){renderRows()};
  window.jbSmsToggleVisible2026=function(on){filtered().forEach(function(x){x.__selected=!!on;var e=document.querySelector('[data-jb-sms-check="'+CSS.escape(String(x.source)+'|'+String(x.id))+'"]');if(e)e.checked=!!on});updateCount()};
  window.jbSmsSelectAll2026=function(){rows().forEach(function(x){x.__selected=true});renderRows(true)};
  window.jbSmsClear2026=function(){rows().forEach(function(x){x.__selected=false});renderRows()};
  function keyOf(x){return String(x.source)+'|'+String(x.id)}
  function renderRows(forceSelected){
    var b=document.getElementById('jbSmsRows2026');if(!b)return;
    var f=filtered();
    b.innerHTML=f.map(function(x){
      var key=keyOf(x),checked=forceSelected||x.__selected?' checked':'';
      return '<tr><td class="sms-check"><input type="checkbox" data-jb-sms-check="'+esc(key)+'"'+checked+' onchange="jbSmsOneChange2026(this)"></td><td>'+esc(x.type)+'</td><td class="sms-id">'+esc(x.id)+'</td><td>'+esc(x.name)+'</td><td>'+esc(x.mobile||'—')+'</td><td class="sms-contact">'+esc(x.email||'—')+'</td><td class="sms-contact">'+esc(x.whatsapp||'—')+'</td></tr>';
    }).join('')||( '<tr><td colspan="7" style="text-align:center;padding:25px">কোনো যোগাযোগ পাওয়া যায়নি।</td></tr>');
    f.forEach(function(x){var e=document.querySelector('[data-jb-sms-check="'+CSS.escape(keyOf(x))+'"]');if(e)e.checked=!!x.__selected||!!forceSelected;});
    updateCount();
  }
  window.jbSmsOneChange2026=function(el){
    var k=String(el.getAttribute('data-jb-sms-check')||''),a=k.indexOf('|'),s=k.slice(0,a),id=k.slice(a+1);
    rows().forEach(function(x){if(String(x.source)===s&&String(x.id)===id)x.__selected=!!el.checked});
    updateCount();
  };
  function updateCount(){
    var a=rows(),s=a.filter(function(x){return x.__selected}),m=s.filter(function(x){return !!x.mobile||!!x.whatsapp});
    var t=document.getElementById('jbSmsTotal2026'),z=document.getElementById('jbSmsSelected2026'),mm=document.getElementById('jbSmsMobile2026');
    if(t)t.textContent=a.length;if(z)z.textContent=s.length;if(mm)mm.textContent=m.length;
  }
  window.jbSmsTemplate2026=function(n){
    var a=['','আসসালামু আলাইকুম। জান্নাতুল বাক্বী মহিলা মাদ্রাসা ও এতিমখানার একটি গুরুত্বপূর্ণ ঘোষণা রয়েছে।','আসসালামু আলাইকুম। আপনার বেতন/ফি সংক্রান্ত তথ্য জানার জন্য মাদ্রাসার প্রশাসনিক বিভাগে যোগাযোগ করুন।','আসসালামু আলাইকুম। পরীক্ষার সময়সূচি/পরীক্ষা সংক্রান্ত গুরুত্বপূর্ণ তথ্যের জন্য মাদ্রাসার নোটিশ দেখুন।','আসসালামু আলাইকুম। উপস্থিতি/অনুপস্থিতি সংক্রান্ত তথ্যের জন্য মাদ্রাসার প্রশাসনিক বিভাগে যোগাযোগ করুন।'];var e=document.getElementById('jbSmsMessage2026');if(e)e.value=a[n]||''};
  window.jbSmsSend2026=async function(){
    var s=rows().filter(function(x){return x.__selected}),msg=((document.getElementById('jbSmsMessage2026')||{}).value||'').trim();
    if(!s.length)return toast('প্রথমে নামের পাশে টিক দিয়ে প্রাপক নির্বাচন করুন।');
    if(!msg)return toast('SMS মেসেজ লিখুন।');
    var r=await call('sendSmsPortal',token,s.map(function(x){return {id:x.id,name:x.name,mobile:x.mobile,source:x.source};}),msg);
    var fb=document.getElementById('jbSmsFeedback2026');if(fb)fb.textContent=r&&r.ok?'✅ '+r.message:'❌ '+((r&&r.message)||'SMS পাঠানো যায়নি।');
    toast((r&&r.message)||'SMS সম্পন্ন হয়নি।');
    if(r&&r.ok){s.forEach(function(x){x.__selected=false});renderRows();jbSmsHistory2026();}
  };
  window.jbSmsHistory2026=async function(){
    var r=await call('getSmsQueueHistory',token,500),b=document.getElementById('jbSmsRows2026');if(!b)return;
    if(!r||!r.ok)return toast((r&&r.message)||'SMS history পাওয়া যায়নি।');
    var h=r.rows||[];
    b.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="7"><div style="padding:8px"><b>SMS Queue / ইতিহাস — '+h.length+' টি</b></div>'+h.map(function(x){return '<div style="padding:7px;border-top:1px solid var(--border)"><b>'+esc(x.Mobile||'')+'</b> — '+esc(x.Status||'')+' — '+esc(x.CreatedAt||'')+'<br>'+esc(x.Message||'')+'</div>'}).join('')+'</td></tr>';
  };
  function load(){
    if(!document.getElementById('jbSmsPortal2026')||typeof call!=='function'||!window.token)return;
    call('getSmsPortalContacts',token).then(function(r){
      window.__jbSmsRows2026=(r&&r.ok?r.rows:[])||[];
      renderRows();
      var fb=document.getElementById('jbSmsFeedback2026');if(fb)fb.textContent='✅ '+window.__jbSmsRows2026.length+' জনের যোগাযোগ তথ্য স্বয়ংক্রিয়ভাবে পাওয়া গেছে।';
    }).catch(function(e){var fb=document.getElementById('jbSmsFeedback2026');if(fb)fb.textContent='❌ SMS Portal লোডে সার্ভার ত্রুটি: '+(e&&e.message?e.message:e)});
  }
  var oldOpen=window.openModule;
  window.openModule=function(k){var r=oldOpen.apply(this,arguments);if(k==='sms')setTimeout(load,120);return r};
  function boot(){bindModule()}
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot);else boot();
  setTimeout(bindModule,500);setTimeout(bindModule,1500);
})();
</script>
<!-- ===== END SMS PORTAL UI ===== -->
